-- CELESTIAL Duels | Mobile + PC (UNC / Synapse / Fluxus / Solara / Delta / Wave / etc.)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
GuiService=game:GetService("GuiService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
-- Prevent double-inject (stacked Heartbeats caused random physics deaths / respawns)
if getgenv then
    if getgenv()._CELESTIALDuelsRunning then
        return
    end
    getgenv()._CELESTIALDuelsRunning = true
end

-- ============================================================
-- FAST LOADER (S2-style) — cut graphics load during script boot
-- Runs in background so loadstring does not freeze the whole client
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local function fastLoaderDisable(obj)
        pcall(function()
            local class = obj.ClassName
            if class=="ParticleEmitter" or class=="Fire" or class=="Smoke" or class=="Sparkles" or class=="Trail" or class=="Beam" then
                obj.Enabled = false
            elseif class=="PointLight" or class=="SpotLight" or class=="SurfaceLight" then
                obj.Enabled = false
            elseif class=="Decal" or class=="Texture" then
                obj.Transparency = 1
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.CastShadow then
                obj.CastShadow = false
            end
        end)
    end
    -- Batched scan (yield every N objects) so map scan never freezes one frame
    local n = 0
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        fastLoaderDisable(obj)
        n = n + 1
        if n % 80 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(fastLoaderDisable)
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.FogEnd = 1e6
        Lighting.FogStart = 0
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
        for _, v in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") then
                v.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
    end)
end)

-- Load persisted intro preference from session (disk load happens later in loadConfigKeys)
pcall(function()
    if getgenv and type(getgenv()._CELESTIALDuelsCfg)=="table" and getgenv()._CELESTIALDuelsCfg.introEnabled ~= nil then
        -- will be applied again after disk load; session hint only
    end
end)

-- ============================
-- EXECUTOR COMPAT (PC + Mobile)
-- ============================
local function _resolve(name)
    local g = rawget(_G, name) or (getgenv and getgenv()[name])
    if typeof(g)=="function" then return g end
    if syn and typeof(syn[name])=="function" then return syn[name] end
    if fluxus and typeof(fluxus[name])=="function" then return fluxus[name] end
    return nil
end

local _gethui = _resolve("gethui") or (typeof(gethui)=="function" and gethui) or nil
local _getcustomasset = _resolve("getcustomasset") or (typeof(getcustomasset)=="function" and getcustomasset) or nil
local _request = _resolve("request")
    or _resolve("http_request")
    or (syn and syn.request)
    or (http and (http.request or http.Request))
    or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    or (typeof(request)=="function" and request)
    or (typeof(http_request)=="function" and http_request)
    or nil

local function protectGui(gui)
    if not gui then return end
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(protect_gui)=="function" then protect_gui(gui)
        elseif syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(gui)
        elseif typeof(hide_in_gcoregui)=="function" then hide_in_gcoregui(gui)
        end
    end)
end

local function parentGui(gui)
    if not gui then return end
    protectGui(gui)
    local ok = false
    if _gethui then
        ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = _gethui() end)
    end
    if not ok or not gui.Parent then
        ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    end
    if not gui.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
            if plr then gui.Parent = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
        end)
    end
end

-- File API polyfills (Synapse / Fluxus / Delta / UNC / Solara / Wave)
local _isfile = (typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile)
    or (syn and syn.isfile)
    or (fluxus and fluxus.isfile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().isfile)
    or function(p)
        local ok, data = pcall(function() return readfile(p) end)
        return ok and data ~= nil
    end
local _readfile = (typeof(readfile)=="function" and readfile)
    or (syn and syn.readfile)
    or (fluxus and fluxus.readfile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().readfile)
    or function() return nil end
local _writefile = (typeof(writefile)=="function" and writefile)
    or (syn and syn.writefile)
    or (fluxus and fluxus.writefile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().writefile)
    or function() end
isfile, readfile, writefile = _isfile, _readfile, _writefile

-- Unified HTTP get (binary-safe when request exists)
local function celestialHttpGet(url)
    if _request then
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            return _request({
                Url = url,
                Method = "GET",
                Headers = { ["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0" },
            })
        end)
        if ok and type(res)=="table" then
            return res.Body or res.body or res.Data or res.data
        elseif ok and type(res)=="string" then
            return res
        end
    end
    local ok2, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
    if ok2 then return body end
    return nil
end

local function celestialGetCustomAsset(path)
    if not path then return nil end
    local asset = nil
    pcall(function()
        if _getcustomasset then asset = _getcustomasset(path) end
    end)
    if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    pcall(function()
        if syn and syn.getcustomasset then asset = syn.getcustomasset(path) end
    end)
    if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    pcall(function()
        if getcustomasset then asset = getcustomasset(path, true) end
    end)
    if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    return nil
end

-- getconnections polyfill
getconnections = getconnections or get_signal_cons or getconnects
    or (syn and syn.get_signal_cons) or (getgenv and getgenv().getconnections)

-- CELESTIAL HUB LOGO (rbxassetid only — no embedded image)
-- ============================================================
local CELESTIAL_LOGO_RBX = ""
local _celestialLogoAsset = CELESTIAL_LOGO_RBX
local function getCELESTIALLogoAsset()
    return CELESTIAL_LOGO_RBX
end



local function destroyOldGui(name)
    if not name then return end
    pcall(function()
        if _gethui then
            local h = _gethui(); local o = h and h:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui"); local o = cg:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
        if plr then
            local pg = plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then local o = pg:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end end
        end
    end)
end

local SkyOrder={"Off","Night","Aurora","Sunset","Galaxy","Cyber","Sakura","Pink Night","Blood Moon","Emerald Dawn","Volcanic","Arctic","Midnight Ocean","Vaporwave","Toxic","Solar Eclipse","Hellscape","Heaven","Storm","Sunrise","Deep Space","Lavender Dream","Inferno","Mint Sky"}
local function celestialSkyColor(rgb) return Color3.fromRGB(rgb[1],rgb[2],rgb[3]) end
local function CELESTIALApplyCustomSky(mode)
    for _,child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if child:GetAttribute(CELESTIAL_SKY_TAG) then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end end
    local terrain=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if terrain then for _,child in ipairs(terrain:GetChildren()) do if child:GetAttribute(CELESTIAL_SKY_TAG) then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end end end
    local preset=CELESTIAL_SKY_PRESETS[mode]
    if not preset or preset.kind=="off" then Lighting.ClockTime=14;Lighting.Brightness=2;Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(127,127,127);Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(127,127,127);Lighting.FogEnd=100000;Lighting.GlobalShadows=true;return end
    Lighting.FogStart=0;Lighting.FogEnd=100000;Lighting.FogColor=Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200);Lighting.ColorShift_Top=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0);Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0);Lighting.GlobalShadows=true
    Lighting.ClockTime=preset.clock or 14;Lighting.Brightness=preset.brightness or 2
    if preset.outAmb then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=celestialSkyColor(preset.outAmb) end
    if preset.ambient then Lighting.Ambient=celestialSkyColor(preset.ambient) end
    if preset.sky then
        local skyInst=Instance.new("Sky");skyInst:SetAttribute(CELESTIAL_SKY_TAG,true)
        if preset.sky.stars then skyInst.StarCount=preset.sky.stars end
        if preset.sky.moon then skyInst.MoonAngularSize=preset.sky.moon end
        if preset.sky.sun then skyInst.SunAngularSize=preset.sky.sun end
        if preset.sky.moonTex then skyInst.MoonTextureId="rbxasset://sky/moon.jpg" end
        skyInst.Parent=Lighting
    end
    if preset.atm then
        local atm=Instance.new("Atmosphere");atm:SetAttribute(CELESTIAL_SKY_TAG,true)
        atm.Density=preset.atm.dens or 0.3;atm.Color=celestialSkyColor(preset.atm.color);atm.Decay=celestialSkyColor(preset.atm.decay);atm.Glare=preset.atm.glare or 1;atm.Haze=preset.atm.haze or 1;atm.Parent=Lighting
    end
    if preset.clouds and terrain then
        local clouds=Instance.new("Clouds");clouds:SetAttribute(CELESTIAL_SKY_TAG,true)
        clouds.Cover=preset.clouds.cover or 0.5;clouds.Density=preset.clouds.dens or 0.5;clouds.Color=celestialSkyColor(preset.clouds.color);clouds.Parent=terrain
    end
end

        BG=Color3.fromRGB(10,8,14), BG2=Color3.fromRGB(16,12,22), ROW_BG=Color3.fromRGB(20,16,28),

        BG=Color3.fromRGB(12,10,8), BG2=Color3.fromRGB(18,16,12), ROW_BG=Color3.fromRGB(24,20,14),

function getTheme()
    return GUI_THEMES[currentGuiTheme] or GUI_THEMES["Obsidian"]
end

-- ============================================================
-- CELESTIAL BACKGROUNDS  (5 code-drawn backgrounds)
-- 1 RED / 2 BLUE / 3 PURPLE / 4 GREEN  -> "CELESTIAL HUB"
-- 5 HALLOWEEN                          -> emojis
-- Applied to the Settings GUI (main window) and the Auto Steal bar.
-- ============================================================
CelestialBG = {
    index = 1, enabled = true, hosts = {}, onChange = nil,
    _anim = {}, _animRunning = false, _orig = {},
    THEMES = {
        {name="RED",    c1=Color3.fromRGB(70,18,28),  c2=Color3.fromRGB(200,45,70),  c3=Color3.fromRGB(255,100,120), style="stripes"},
        {name="BLUE",   c1=Color3.fromRGB(12,35,70),  c2=Color3.fromRGB(40,130,220), c3=Color3.fromRGB(120,230,255), style="rings"},
        {name="PURPLE", c1=Color3.fromRGB(40,15,70),  c2=Color3.fromRGB(140,55,210), c3=Color3.fromRGB(230,150,255), style="stars"},
        {name="GREEN",  c1=Color3.fromRGB(10,45,30),  c2=Color3.fromRGB(25,160,100), c3=Color3.fromRGB(100,255,200), style="grid"},
        {name="HALLOWEEN", c1=Color3.fromRGB(50,18,60), c2=Color3.fromRGB(200,85,25), c3=Color3.fromRGB(255,175,50), style="halloween",
         emojis={"\u{1F383}","\u{1F47B}","\u{1F987}","\u{1F577}","\u{1F480}","\u{1F578}","\u{1F36C}","\u{1F319}"}},
    },
}

function CelestialBG._ensureAnim()
    if CelestialBG._animRunning then return end
    CelestialBG._animRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.06)
            local list = CelestialBG._anim
            if #list == 0 then CelestialBG._animRunning = false; return end
            local now = tick()
            for i = #list, 1, -1 do
                local ok, alive = pcall(list[i], now)
                if not ok or not alive then table.remove(list, i) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function CelestialBG._build(host, kind)
    local T = CelestialBG.THEMES[CelestialBG.index]
    if not T or not host then return end
    local main = (kind == "main")
    local old = host:FindFirstChild("CelestialBGLayer")
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local zi = main and 2 or 10
    local layer = Instance.new("Frame")
    layer.Name = "CelestialBGLayer"
    layer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    layer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    layer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    layer.ClipsDescendants = true
    layer.Active = false
    layer.ZIndex = zi
    layer.Visible = CelestialBG.enabled
    local cr = Instance.new("UICorner", layer)
    cr.CornerRadius = main and UDim.new(0, 14) or UDim.new(1, 0)
    local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", layer)
    grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, T.c1),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, T.c2),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, T.c1),
    })
    grad.Rotation = main and 35 or 0
    layer.Parent = host

    local anim = CelestialBG._anim
    local t0 = tick()
    anim[#anim + 1] = function(now)
        if not layer.Parent then return false end
        if not layer.Visible then return true end
        grad.Rotation = (main and 35 or 0) + (main and math.sin((now - t0) * 0.5) * 18 or 0)
        grad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin((now - t0) * 0.4) * 0.25, 0)
        return true
    end

    local rnd = Random.new(7)
    local function frame(pos, size, col, tr)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", layer)
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Position = pos
        f.Size = size
        f.BackgroundColor3 = col
        f.BackgroundTransparency = tr
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.ZIndex = zi
        return f
    end
    local function circle(px, py, size, col, tr)
        local f = frame(UDim2.fromScale(px, py), UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), col, tr)
        Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        return f
    end
    local function glyph(txt, px, py, size, col, tr, font)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", layer)
        l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        l.Position = UDim2.fromScale(px, py)
        l.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size + 10, size + 10)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.TextSize = size
        l.TextColor3 = col
        l.TextTransparency = tr
        l.Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.ZIndex = zi
        return l
    end

    -- soft glow orbs (all styles)
    local orbA = circle(0.15, 0.2, main and 170 or 44, T.c3, 0.88)
    local orbB = circle(0.88, 0.85, main and 210 or 54, T.c2, 0.8)
    anim[#anim + 1] = function(now)
        if not layer.Parent then return false end
        orbA.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86 + math.sin((now - t0) * 0.9) * 0.05
        orbB.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78 + math.cos((now - t0) * 0.7) * 0.05
        return true
    end

    local style = T.style
    if style == "stripes" then
        for i = 1, (main and 7 or 9) do
            local s = frame(UDim2.fromScale((i - 0.5) / (main and 7 or 9), 0.5), UDim2.new(0, 2, 1.8, 0), T.c3, 0.9)
            s.Rotation = 28
        end
    elseif style == "rings" then
        local cx, cy = main and 0.8 or 0.9, main and 0.22 or 0.5
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local sz = (main and 60 or 14) + i * (main and 55 or 14)
            local r = circle(cx, cy, sz, T.c3, 1)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", r)
            st.Color = T.c3; st.Thickness = 1.5; st.Transparency = 0.7
            anim[#anim + 1] = function(now)
                if not r.Parent then return false end
                st.Transparency = 0.65 + math.sin((now - t0) * 1.2 + i) * 0.2
                return true
            end
        end
        local cx2, cy2 = main and 0.12 or 0.1, main and 0.88 or 0.5
        for i = 1, 2 do
            local r = circle(cx2, cy2, (main and 40 or 12) + i * (main and 40 or 12), T.c3, 1)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", r)
            st.Color = T.c3; st.Thickness = 1; st.Transparency = 0.8
        end
    elseif style == "stars" then
        local glyphs = {"\u{2726}", "\u{2727}", "\u{00B7}", "\u{2726}"}
        local n = main and 18 or 12
        for i = 1, n do
            local px, py = rnd:NextNumber(0.03, 0.97), rnd:NextNumber(0.05, 0.95)
            local sz = main and rnd:NextInteger(8, 18) or rnd:NextInteger(7, 11)
            local base = rnd:NextNumber(0.35, 0.8)
            local ph = rnd:NextNumber(0, 6)
            local g = glyph(glyphs[(i % #glyphs) + 1], px, py, sz, T.c3, base)
            anim[#anim + 1] = function(now)
                if not g.Parent then return false end
                g.TextTransparency = math.clamp(base + math.sin((now - t0) * 1.4 + ph) * 0.25, 0.1, 0.95)
                return true
            end
        end
    elseif style == "grid" then
        local n = main and 7 or 14
        for i = 1, n do
            frame(UDim2.fromScale((i - 0.5) / n, 0.5), UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), T.c3, 0.92)
        end
        for i = 1, (main and 9 or 3) do
            frame(UDim2.fromScale(0.5, (i - 0.5) / (main and 9 or 3)), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), T.c3, 0.92)
        end
        local scan = frame(UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, main and 3 or 2), T.c3, 0.75)
        anim[#anim + 1] = function(now)
            if not scan.Parent then return false end
            scan.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, ((now - t0) * 0.18) % 1)
            return true
        end
    elseif style == "halloween" then
        circle(main and 0.84 or 0.93, main and 0.13 or 0.5, main and 78 or 26, T.c3, 0.72)
        circle(main and 0.84 or 0.93, main and 0.13 or 0.5, main and 52 or 17, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 90), 0.8)
        local list = T.emojis
        local n = main and 14 or 8
        for i = 1, n do
            local px, py
            if main then
                px, py = rnd:NextNumber(0.06, 0.94), rnd:NextNumber(0.06, 0.94)
            else
                px, py = (i - 0.5) / n, 0.5
            end
            local sz = main and rnd:NextInteger(20, 32) or 15
            local g = glyph(list[((i - 1) % #list) + 1], px, py, sz, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), main and 0.45 or 0.35, Enum.Font.SourceSansBold)
            local ph = rnd:NextNumber(0, 6)
            anim[#anim + 1] = function(now)
                if not g.Parent then return false end
                local s = math.sin((now - t0) * 0.9 + ph)
                g.Position = UDim2.new(px, 0, py, s * (main and 6 or 2))
                g.Rotation = s * 9
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    -- "CELESTIAL HUB" text (styles 1-4)
    if style ~= "halloween" then
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel", layer)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        t.TextScaled = true
        t.TextColor3 = T.c3
        t.ZIndex = zi
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", t)
        st.Color = T.c3; st.Thickness = 1.5; st.Transparency = 0.55
        if main then
            t.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            t.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 150)
            t.Text = "CELESTIAL\nHUB"
            t.TextTransparency = 0.78
            t.Rotation = -14
        else
            t.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            t.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0)
            t.Size = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 0.62, 0)
            t.Text = "CELESTIAL HUB"
            t.TextTransparency = 0.68
            t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            local c = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", t)
            c.MaxTextSize = 14; c.MinTextSize = 6
        end
    end

    CelestialBG._ensureAnim()
end

-- Makes headers / rows see-through so the background shows, and hides the old CD/HUB watermark
function CelestialBG.skinRows()
    local mf = mainFrame
    if not mf or not mf.Parent then return end
    local on = CelestialBG.enabled
    local orig = CelestialBG._orig
    local function skin(f)
        if on then
            if orig[f] == nil then orig[f] = f.BackgroundTransparency end
            f.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(orig[f], 0.5)
        elseif orig[f] ~= nil then
            f.BackgroundTransparency = orig[f]
            orig[f] = nil
        end
    end
    for _, ch in ipairs(mf:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Frame") and ch.Name ~= "CelestialBGLayer" and ch.Size.Y.Scale == 0 and (ch.Size.Y.Offset == 48 or ch.Size.Y.Offset == 14) then
            if ch.BackgroundTransparency < 0.5 or orig[ch] ~= nil then skin(ch) end
        elseif ch:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
            for _, row in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
                if row:IsA("Frame") and row.Size.Y.Offset >= 26 and (row.BackgroundTransparency < 0.5 or orig[row] ~= nil) then
                    skin(row)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local l1, l2 = mf:FindFirstChild("CDLogo"), mf:FindFirstChild("CDHub")
    if l1 then l1.Visible = not on end
    if l2 then l2.Visible = not on end
end

function CelestialBG.register(kind, host)
    if not host then return end
    CelestialBG.hosts[host] = kind
    pcall(CelestialBG._build, host, kind)
    if kind == "main" then
        task.delay(0.8, function() pcall(CelestialBG.skinRows) end)
    end
end

function CelestialBG.setIndex(i)
    i = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(i) or 1), 1, #CelestialBG.THEMES)
    CelestialBG.index = i
    for host, kind in pairs(CelestialBG.hosts) do
        if host and host.Parent then pcall(CelestialBG._build, host, kind) else CelestialBG.hosts[host] = nil end
    end
    pcall(CelestialBG.skinRows)
    if CelestialBG.onChange then pcall(CelestialBG.onChange) end
end

function CelestialBG.setEnabled(on)
    CelestialBG.enabled = on == true
    for host in pairs(CelestialBG.hosts) do
        local layer = host and host:FindFirstChild("CelestialBGLayer")
        if layer then layer.Visible = CelestialBG.enabled end
    end
    pcall(CelestialBG.skinRows)
end

        Swim=616143378, SwimIdle=616144772, Climb=616133594,
        Idle={616136790,616138447,886888594}
    },
    Bubbly = {
        Run=910025107, Walk=910034870, Jump=910016857, Fall=910001910,
        Swim=910028158, SwimIdle=910030921, Climb=909997997,
        Idle={910004836,910009958,1018536639}
    },
    Cartoon = {
        Run=742638842, Walk=742640026, Jump=742637942, Fall=742637151,
        Swim=742639220, SwimIdle=742639812, Climb=742636889,
        Idle={742637544,742638445,885477856}
    },
}

animPack = "Adidas Sports"
animPackEnabled = true
headlessEnabled = false
korbloxEnabled = false


-- ============================================================
-- BAT SKINS + SWING SOUND EFFECTS (from Synapse X Celestial Hub)
-- Options: Katana | Minecraft Sword | None
-- Swing SFX play on bat activate (manual + auto bat / aimbot)
-- ============================================================
batSkin = batSkin or "Katana"

CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ASSETS = {
    Scythe = "8699386664",
    ["Ban Hammer"] = "14701192921",
    ["Minecraft Sword"] = "minecraft_mesh",
}
CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ADJUST = {
    Scythe = {x = 0.09, y = -1, z = -0.8, rx = 0, ry = -90, rz = 0, size = 6},
    ["Ban Hammer"] = {x = 0, y = -1, z = 0, rx = 0, ry = 180, rz = 0, size = 4},
}
CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_FOLDER = "CelestialHubBatSkin"

function celestialIsBatTool(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false end
    local n = string.lower(tool.Name or "")
    return string.find(n, "bat", 1, true) ~= nil or string.find(n, "slap", 1, true) ~= nil
end

function celestialGetHandle(tool)
    return tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or tool:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end

CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ANIM_CONNECTIONS = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

function celestialRestoreBatTool(tool)
    if CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ANIM_CONNECTIONS[tool] then
        pcall(function() CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ANIM_CONNECTIONS[tool]:Disconnect() end)
        CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ANIM_CONNECTIONS[tool] = nil
    end
    local oldFolder = tool:FindFirstChild(CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_FOLDER)
    if oldFolder then pcall(function() oldFolder:Destroy() end) end
    for _, obj in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj:GetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinHidden") then
            pcall(function()
                obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = obj:GetAttribute("CelestialHubOldLTM") or 0
                obj.Transparency = obj:GetAttribute("CelestialHubOldTransparency") or 0
                obj.CastShadow = true
                obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinHidden", nil)
                obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubOldLTM", nil)
                obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubOldTransparency", nil)
            end)
        end
    end
end

function celestialHideBatOriginal(tool)
    for _, obj in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and not obj:GetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinPart") then
            if not obj:GetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinHidden") then
                obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubOldLTM", obj.LocalTransparencyModifier)
                obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubOldTransparency", obj.Transparency)
                obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinHidden", true)
            end
            obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            obj.Transparency = 1
            obj.CastShadow = false
        end
    end
end

function celestialMakeSkinPart(parent, name, size, color, material)
    local p = Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name = name
    p.Size = size
    p.Color = color
    p.Material = material or Enum.Material.Metal
    p.Anchored = false
    p.CanCollide = false
    p.CanTouch = false
    p.CanQuery = false
    p.Massless = true
    p:SetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinPart", true)
    p.Parent = parent
    return p
end

function celestialWeldSkin(handle, part, offset)
    part.CFrame = handle.CFrame * (offset or CFrame.new())
    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    weld.Part0 = handle
    weld.Part1 = part
    weld.Parent = part
end

CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_SOUNDS = {
    Katana = "rbxassetid://111808555599832",
    ["Minecraft Sword"] = "rbxassetid://12222216",
}

celestialSwingSoundLastAt = 0

function celestialMuteDefaultSlash(tool)
    if not tool then return end
    local slash = tool:FindFirstChild("Slash")
    if slash and slash:IsA("Sound") then
        slash.Volume = 0
        slash.Playing = false
    end
end

function celestialEnsureSwingSound(tool, soundId)
    if not tool or not soundId then return nil end
    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or tool
    local s = tool:FindFirstChild("CelestialHubSwingSound") or (handle and handle:FindFirstChild("CelestialHubSwingSound"))
    if not s then
        s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.Name = "CelestialHubSwingSound"
        s.Volume = 1
        s.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.InverseTapered
        s.Parent = handle or tool
    end
    if s.SoundId ~= soundId then
        s.SoundId = soundId
    end
    return s
end

function celestialPlaySwingSound(tool, skin)
    skin = skin or batSkin
    local soundId = CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_SOUNDS[skin]
    if not soundId or not tool then return end
    celestialMuteDefaultSlash(tool)
    local now = tick()
    local cooldown = (skin == "Minecraft Sword") and 0.1 or 0.2
    if now - celestialSwingSoundLastAt < cooldown then return end
    celestialSwingSoundLastAt = now
    local s = celestialEnsureSwingSound(tool, soundId)
    if not s then return end
    pcall(function()
        s:Stop()
        if skin == "Minecraft Sword" then
            s.PlaybackSpeed = 1.45
            s.TimePosition = 0
            s.Volume = 1
        else
            s.PlaybackSpeed = 1
            s.TimePosition = 0
            s.Volume = 0.95
        end
        s:Play()
    end)
end

function celestialSetupBatSkinEffects(tool, skin, folder)
    if not tool or not folder or not folder.Parent then return end
    local handle = celestialGetHandle(tool)
    if not handle then return end
    if CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool] then
        pcall(function() CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool]:Disconnect() end)
        CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool] = nil
    end

    local bestPart, bestAxis, bestLength = nil, "Y", 0
    for _, obj in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            local sz = obj.Size
            local axis, length = "Y", sz.Y
            if sz.X > length then axis, length = "X", sz.X end
            if sz.Z > length then axis, length = "Z", sz.Z end
            if length > bestLength then bestPart, bestAxis, bestLength = obj, axis, length end
        end
    end
    if bestPart and bestLength > 0.5 then
        local a0, a1 = Instance.new("Attachment"), Instance.new("Attachment")
        a0.Name, a1.Name = "CelestialBatTrailTop", "CelestialBatTrailBottom"
        local half = bestLength * 0.46
        if bestAxis == "X" then
            a0.Position, a1.Position = Vector3.new(-half, 0, 0), Vector3.new(half, 0, 0)
        elseif bestAxis == "Z" then
            a0.Position, a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, -half), Vector3.new(0, 0, half)
        else
            a0.Position, a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, half, 0), Vector3.new(0, -half, 0)
        end
        a0.Parent, a1.Parent = bestPart, bestPart
        local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
        trail.Name = "CelestialBatSwingTrail"
        trail.Attachment0, trail.Attachment1 = a0, a1
        trail.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 70), Color3.fromRGB(255, 95, 0))
        trail.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.12), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
        trail.Lifetime = 0.22
        trail.LightEmission = 0.8
        trail.Enabled = false
        trail.Parent = folder
    end

    if CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_SOUNDS[skin] then
        celestialMuteDefaultSlash(tool)
        celestialEnsureSwingSound(tool, CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_SOUNDS[skin])
    end

    CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool] = tool.Activated:Connect(function()
        if not tool.Parent or batSkin ~= skin then return end
        local trail = folder:FindFirstChild("CelestialBatSwingTrail", true)
        if trail and trail:IsA("Trail") then
            trail.Enabled = true
            task.delay(0.24, function()
                if trail and trail.Parent then trail.Enabled = false end
            end)
        end
        if CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_SOUNDS[batSkin] then
            celestialPlaySwingSound(tool, batSkin)
        end
    end)
end

function celestialApplyBatSkin(tool)
    if not celestialIsBatTool(tool) then return end
    if batSkin == "Scythe" or batSkin == "Ban Hammer" then batSkin = "Katana" end
    local skin = batSkin or "Katana"
    if CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool] then
        pcall(function() CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool]:Disconnect() end)
        CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_CONNECTIONS[tool] = nil
    end
    celestialRestoreBatTool(tool)
    if skin == "None" then return end
    local handle = celestialGetHandle(tool)
    if not handle then return end

    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_FOLDER
    folder.Parent = tool

    if skin == "Minecraft Sword" then
        celestialHideBatOriginal(tool)
        local swordPart = Instance.new("Part")
        swordPart.Name = "CelestialMinecraftSwordMesh"
        swordPart.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        swordPart.Transparency = 0
        swordPart.Anchored = false
        swordPart.CanCollide = false
        swordPart.CanTouch = false
        swordPart.CanQuery = false
        swordPart.Massless = true
        swordPart:SetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinPart", true)
        swordPart.Parent = folder
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://8827558932"
        mesh.TextureId = "rbxassetid://8827558969"
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
        mesh.Parent = swordPart
        local motor = Instance.new("Motor6D")
        motor.Name = "CelestialMinecraftSwordGrip"
        motor.Part0 = handle
        motor.Part1 = swordPart
        motor.C0 = CFrame.new(-0.05, -0.1, -0.12)
            * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), math.rad(180), math.rad(300))
        motor.Parent = handle
        celestialSetupBatSkinEffects(tool, skin, folder)
        return
    end

    if skin == "Katana" then
        celestialHideBatOriginal(tool)
        local blade = celestialMakeSkinPart(folder, "CelestialKatanaBlade",
            Vector3.new(0.19, 4.15, 0.065), Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 225), Enum.Material.Metal)
        celestialWeldSkin(handle, blade, CFrame.new(0, 1.17, 0))
        local edge = celestialMakeSkinPart(folder, "CelestialKatanaEdge",
            Vector3.new(0.025, 4.05, 0.075), Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255), Enum.Material.Neon)
        celestialWeldSkin(handle, edge, CFrame.new(0.105, 1.15, -0.008))
        local guard = celestialMakeSkinPart(folder, "CelestialKatanaGuard",
            Vector3.new(0.78, 0.09, 0.27), Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35), Enum.Material.Metal)
        celestialWeldSkin(handle, guard, CFrame.new(0, -0.39, 0))
        local grip = celestialMakeSkinPart(folder, "CelestialKatanaGrip",
            Vector3.new(0.22, 0.82, 0.22), Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
        celestialWeldSkin(handle, grip, CFrame.new(0, -1.15, 0))
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local wrap = celestialMakeSkinPart(folder, "CelestialKatanaWrap" .. i,
                Vector3.new(0.235, 0.035, 0.235),
                i % 2 == 0 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
                Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
            celestialWeldSkin(handle, wrap, CFrame.new(0, -1.45 + i * 0.145, 0))
        end
        local pommel = celestialMakeSkinPart(folder, "CelestialKatanaPommel",
            Vector3.new(0.27, 0.13, 0.27), Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255), Enum.Material.Metal)
        celestialWeldSkin(handle, pommel, CFrame.new(0, -1.59, 0))
        task.defer(function()
            if tool and tool.Parent and folder and folder.Parent then
                celestialSetupBatSkinEffects(tool, skin, folder)
            end
        end)
        return
    end

    local assetId = CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ASSETS[skin]
    if not assetId then return end
    local ok, objects = pcall(function()
        return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. assetId)
    end)
    if not ok or not objects or not objects[1] then return end

    local model = objects[1]
    if model:IsA("BasePart") then
        local wrapper = Instance.new("Model")
        wrapper.Name = "CelestialHubBatSkinModel"
        model.Parent = wrapper
        model = wrapper
    end
    model.Name = "CelestialHubBatSkinAsset"
    model.Parent = folder

    local parts = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            table.insert(parts, obj)
            obj.Anchored = false
            obj.CanCollide = false
            obj.CanTouch = false
            obj.CanQuery = false
            obj.Massless = true
            obj:SetAttribute("CelestialHubBatSkinPart", true)
        elseif obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("LocalScript") or obj:IsA("ModuleScript")
            or obj:IsA("ScreenGui") or obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    if #parts == 0 then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end); return end

    local adjust = CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_ADJUST[skin] or {x = 0, y = 0, z = -0.5, rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0, size = 5}
    local bboxSize
    local okBounds = pcall(function()
        local _, size = model:GetBoundingBox()
        bboxSize = size
    end)
    if okBounds and bboxSize then
        local longest = math.max(bboxSize.X, bboxSize.Y, bboxSize.Z)
        if longest > 0 then
            pcall(function() model:ScaleTo(math.clamp((adjust.size or 5) / longest, 0.05, 8)) end)
        end
    end

    local orient = CFrame.new()
    local _, scaledSize = model:GetBoundingBox()
    if scaledSize.X > scaledSize.Y and scaledSize.X > scaledSize.Z then
        orient = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
    elseif scaledSize.Z > scaledSize.Y and scaledSize.Z > scaledSize.X then
        orient = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
    end
    local desiredBox = handle.CFrame
        * CFrame.new(adjust.x or 0, adjust.y or 0, adjust.z or 0)
        * orient
        * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(adjust.rx or 0), math.rad(adjust.ry or 0), math.rad(adjust.rz or 0))

    local currentPivot = model:GetPivot()
    local currentBox = select(1, model:GetBoundingBox())
    local boxFromPivot = currentPivot:ToObjectSpace(currentBox)
    pcall(function() model:PivotTo(desiredBox * boxFromPivot:Inverse()) end)

    celestialHideBatOriginal(tool)
    for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
        local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
        weld.Part0 = handle
        weld.Part1 = part
        weld.Parent = part
    end
    celestialSetupBatSkinEffects(tool, skin, folder)
end

function applyCelestialBatSkinToAll()
    local containers = {}
    if LP.Character then table.insert(containers, LP.Character) end
    local backpack = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if backpack then table.insert(containers, backpack) end
    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if celestialIsBatTool(tool) then pcall(celestialApplyBatSkin, tool) end
        end
    end
end

function celestialWatchBatContainer(container)
    if not container then return end
    container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if celestialIsBatTool(child) then
            task.wait(0.15)
            pcall(celestialApplyBatSkin, child)
        end
    end)
end
celestialWatchBatContainer(LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
celestialWatchBatContainer(LP.Character)
LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child:IsA("Backpack") then celestialWatchBatContainer(child) end
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.75)
    celestialWatchBatContainer(char)
    pcall(applyCelestialBatSkinToAll)
end)
task.defer(function()
    task.wait(1)
    pcall(applyCelestialBatSkinToAll)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        pcall(function()
            local skin = batSkin
            local soundId = CELESTIAL_BAT_SKIN_SOUNDS[skin]
            if not soundId then return end
            local containers = {LP.Character, LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")}
            for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
                if container then
                    for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        if celestialIsBatTool(tool) then
                            celestialMuteDefaultSlash(tool)
                            celestialEnsureSwingSound(tool, soundId)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)


local HEADLESS_MESH_ID = "rbxassetid://1095708"
local KORBLOX_MESH_ID = "rbxassetid://101851696"
local KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID = "rbxassetid://101851254"
local DARK_GREY_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)

local function removeFace(head)
    local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
    if face then face:Destroy() end
end

function applyHeadlessToChar(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end
    if enabled then
        head.Transparency = 1
        head.CanCollide = false
        removeFace(head)
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.MeshId == HEADLESS_MESH_ID then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = HEADLESS_MESH_ID
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
        mesh.Name = "HeadlessMesh"
        mesh.Parent = head
        head:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
            if head.Transparency ~= 1 then head.Transparency = 1 end
        end)
        head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "face" and child:IsA("Decal") then child:Destroy() end
        end)
    else
        head.Transparency = 0
        head.CanCollide = true
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "HeadlessMesh" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        removeFace(head)
    end
end

function applyKorbloxToChar(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end
    if enabled then
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then child:Destroy() end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                rightLeg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
                    if rightLeg.Color ~= DARK_GREY_COLOR then rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR end
                end)
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = rightLeg
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 1
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1 end
                if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 1 end
                local oldKorblox = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if oldKorblox then oldKorblox:Destroy() end
                local korbloxLeg = Instance.new("Part")
                korbloxLeg.Name = "KorbloxLeg"
                korbloxLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
                korbloxLeg.Anchored = false
                korbloxLeg.CanCollide = false
                korbloxLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                korbloxLeg.Parent = char
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = korbloxLeg
                local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                weld.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
                weld.Part1 = korbloxLeg
                weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
                weld.Name = "KorbloxWeld"
                weld.Parent = korbloxLeg
            end
        end
    else
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then child:Destroy() end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 0
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 0 end
                if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 0 end
                local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if korbloxLeg then korbloxLeg:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end
end

        shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=86896487",

        colors = {

-- ============================================================
-- SMVLL SKIN-CHANGER OUTFITS (added to the list below the originals)
-- Outfit 1-4 (headless + korblox + accessory) and Homero Chino.
-- Local visual only. Needs executor game:GetObjects / InsertService.
-- ============================================================
SMVLLOutfits = { active = false, current = nil }
;(function()
    local M = SMVLLOutfits
    local TAG = "CelestialSkin_"
    local HID = "CelestialSkinHid"
    local V3, CF = Vector3.new, CFrame.new

    local HOMERO_CFG = {
        body = { 10725826963, 86500008, 86500054, 86500036, 86500064, 86500078 },
        aplicarCuerpo = true,
        items = {
            { id = 103227869700418, offset = CF(0, 0, 0) },
            { id = 84952305140948,  offset = CF(0, 0, 0) },
            { id = 122465238537030, offset = CF(0, 0, 0) },
        },
        shirt = nil,
        pants = "rbxassetid://78591690208112",
        skinColor = Color3.fromRGB(234, 184, 146),
        headColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    }

    local assetCache = {}
    local snap = nil
    local removedAccessories = {}
    local followItems = {}
    local needReapply = false

    local function cloneList(list)
        local out = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(list) do
            local ok, c = pcall(function() return o:Clone() end)
            if ok and c then out[#out + 1] = c end
        end
        if #out == 0 then return nil end
        return out
    end

    local function loadObjects(id)
        if assetCache[id] then
            local c = cloneList(assetCache[id])
            if c then return c end
        end
        for attempt = 1, 4 do
            local ok, res = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)) end)
            if ok and typeof(res) == "table" and #res > 0 then
                assetCache[id] = res
                return cloneList(res) or res
            end
            ok, res = pcall(function() return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(id) end)
            if ok and res then
                assetCache[id] = { res }
                return cloneList({ res }) or { res }
            end
            task.wait(0.35 * attempt)
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function waitReady(char, timeout)
        local t0 = tick()
        timeout = timeout or 10
        while tick() - t0 < timeout do
            local ready = char.Parent
                and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                and (char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
            local okA, loaded = pcall(function() return LP:HasAppearanceLoaded() end)
            if ready and ((not okA) or loaded) then return true end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return false
    end

    local function collectParts(objs)
        local out = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
            if o:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = o end
            for _, d in ipairs(o:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = d end
            end
        end
        return out
    end

    local function destroyAll(objs)
        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
    end

    local function findAtt(char, name)
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local a = part:FindFirstChild(name)
                if a and a:IsA("Attachment") then return a end
            end
        end
    end

    local function buildWeld(it, target)
        if it.weld then pcall(function() it.weld:Destroy() end) end
        local H = it.H
        H.Anchored = false
        H.CFrame = target.CFrame * it.c0 * it.c1:Inverse()
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Name = TAG .. "weld"
        w.Part0, w.Part1, w.C0, w.C1 = target, H, it.c0, it.c1
        w.Parent = H
        it.weld = w
    end

    local function addFollow(char, H, targetName, c0, c1)
        local target = char:FindFirstChild(targetName)
        if not target then
            pcall(function() H:Destroy() end)
            return false
        end
        H.CanCollide = false
        H.Massless = true
        H.Anchored = false
        H.Parent = char
        local it = { H = H, targetName = targetName, c0 = c0 or CF(), c1 = c1 or CF(), bad = 0, mode = "weld" }
        buildWeld(it, target)
        followItems[#followItems + 1] = it
        return true
    end

    -- keeps attached pieces glued to the body
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if #followItems == 0 then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        for i = #followItems, 1, -1 do
            local it = followItems[i]
            local H = it.H
            local alive = true
            if H.Parent ~= char then
                alive = pcall(function() H.Parent = char end)
            end
            if not alive then
                table.remove(followItems, i)
                needReapply = true
            else
                local target = char:FindFirstChild(it.targetName)
                if target then
                    local want = target.CFrame * it.c0 * it.c1:Inverse()
                    if it.mode == "weld" then
                        local w = it.weld
                        if (not w) or w.Parent ~= H or w.Part0 ~= target or w.Part1 ~= H then
                            buildWeld(it, target)
                        elseif (H.Position - want.Position).Magnitude > 1.5 then
                            it.bad = it.bad + 1
                            if it.bad > 15 then
                                it.mode = "anchor"
                                pcall(function() w:Destroy() end)
                                H.Anchored = true
                            end
                        else
                            it.bad = 0
                        end
                    else
                        H.CFrame = want
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local function takeSnapshot(char)
        snap = { charMeshes = {} }
        local shirt = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt")
        local pants = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants")
        snap.shirt = shirt and shirt.ShirtTemplate or nil
        snap.pants = pants and pants.PantsTemplate or nil

        local bc = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors")
        if bc then
            snap.colors = {
                bc.HeadColor3, bc.TorsoColor3, bc.LeftArmColor3,
                bc.RightArmColor3, bc.LeftLegColor3, bc.RightLegColor3,
            }
        end

        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            if head:IsA("MeshPart") then
                snap.headMeshId, snap.headTexId = head.MeshId, head.TextureID
            end
            local sm = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh")
            if sm then
                snap.sm = { MeshId = sm.MeshId, TextureId = sm.TextureId, MeshType = sm.MeshType, Scale = sm.Scale }
            end
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("CharacterMesh") and d.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then
                snap.charMeshes[#snap.charMeshes + 1] = d:Clone()
            end
        end
        removedAccessories = {}
    end

    local function hide(part)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part:SetAttribute(HID, true)
            part.Transparency = 1
        end
    end

    local function restoreDefault(char)
        if not char then return end

        followItems = {}
        needReapply = false

        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if d.Name:sub(1, #TAG) == TAG then
                pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
            end
        end

        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and d:GetAttribute(HID) then
                d.Transparency = 0
                d:SetAttribute(HID, nil)
            end
        end

        for _, acc in ipairs(removedAccessories) do
            if acc and not acc.Parent then pcall(function() acc.Parent = char end) end
        end
        removedAccessories = {}

        if not snap then return end

        local shirt = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt")
        if snap.shirt then
            shirt = shirt or Instance.new("Shirt")
            shirt.ShirtTemplate = snap.shirt
            shirt.Parent = char
        elseif shirt then
            shirt:Destroy()
        end
        local pants = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants")
        if snap.pants then
            pants = pants or Instance.new("Pants")
            pants.PantsTemplate = snap.pants
            pants.Parent = char
        elseif pants then
            pants:Destroy()
        end

        if snap.colors then
            local bc = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors") or Instance.new("BodyColors")
            bc.HeadColor3, bc.TorsoColor3, bc.LeftArmColor3 = snap.colors[1], snap.colors[2], snap.colors[3]
            bc.RightArmColor3, bc.LeftLegColor3, bc.RightLegColor3 = snap.colors[4], snap.colors[5], snap.colors[6]
            bc.Parent = char
        end

        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            head.Transparency = 0
            head.CanCollide = true
            head.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
            local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
            if face then face.Transparency = 0 end
            if head:IsA("MeshPart") and snap.headMeshId then
                pcall(function()
                    head.MeshId = snap.headMeshId
                    head.TextureID = snap.headTexId
                end)
            end
            local sm = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh")
            if snap.sm then
                sm = sm or Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                sm.MeshType = snap.sm.MeshType
                sm.MeshId, sm.TextureId, sm.Scale = snap.sm.MeshId, snap.sm.TextureId, snap.sm.Scale
                sm.Parent = head
            elseif sm then
                sm:Destroy()
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            local neck = char:FindFirstChild("Neck", true)
            if neck then neck.Enabled = true end
        end)

        local hasMesh = false
        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("CharacterMesh") and d.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then hasMesh = true end
        end
        if not hasMesh then
            for _, m in ipairs(snap.charMeshes) do m:Clone().Parent = char end
        end
    end

    local function applyBody(char)
        for _, id in ipairs(HOMERO_CFG.body) do
            local objs = loadObjects(id)
            if objs then
                for _, mp in ipairs(collectParts(objs)) do
                    local orig = char:FindFirstChild(mp.Name)
                    if orig and orig:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local c = mp:Clone()
                        c.Name = TAG .. "body_" .. mp.Name
                        c.CanCollide = false
                        c.Anchored = false
                        c.Massless = true
                        c.Size = orig.Size
                        addFollow(char, c, orig.Name, CF(), CF())
                        hide(orig)
                    end
                end
                destroyAll(objs)
            end
        end
    end

    local function attachItem(char, entry)
        local objs = loadObjects(entry.id)
        if not objs then return false end

        local handle
        for _, p in ipairs(collectParts(objs)) do
            if p.Name == "Handle" then handle = p; break end
            if not handle then handle = p end
        end
        if not handle then destroyAll(objs); return false end

        local H = handle:Clone()
        destroyAll(objs)

        H.Name = TAG .. "item_" .. tostring(entry.id)
        local wrap = H:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("WrapLayer")
        local target, c0, c1

        if wrap then
            target = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            c0 = entry.offset or CF()
            c1 = CF()
        else
            local hAtt = H:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
            local bAtt = hAtt and findAtt(char, hAtt.Name)
            if bAtt then
                target = bAtt.Parent
                c0 = bAtt.CFrame * (entry.offset or CF())
                c1 = hAtt.CFrame
            else
                target = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                c0 = entry.offset or CF(0, 1.4, 0)
                c1 = CF()
            end
        end

        if not target then H:Destroy(); return false end

        return addFollow(char, H, target.Name, c0, c1)
    end

    local function applyHomero(char)
        if HOMERO_CFG.skinColor then
            local bc = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors") or Instance.new("BodyColors")
            bc.HeadColor3 = HOMERO_CFG.headColor or HOMERO_CFG.skinColor
            bc.TorsoColor3 = HOMERO_CFG.skinColor
            bc.LeftArmColor3, bc.RightArmColor3 = HOMERO_CFG.skinColor, HOMERO_CFG.skinColor
            bc.LeftLegColor3, bc.RightLegColor3 = HOMERO_CFG.skinColor, HOMERO_CFG.skinColor
            bc.Parent = char
        end
        for _, a in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if a:IsA("Accessory") then hide(a:FindFirstChild("Handle")) end
        end
        if HOMERO_CFG.shirt then
            local s = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt") or Instance.new("Shirt")
            s.ShirtTemplate = HOMERO_CFG.shirt
            s.Parent = char
        end
        if HOMERO_CFG.pants then
            local p = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants") or Instance.new("Pants")
            p.PantsTemplate = HOMERO_CFG.pants
            p.Parent = char
        end
        if HOMERO_CFG.aplicarCuerpo then applyBody(char) end
        for _, e in ipairs(HOMERO_CFG.items) do attachItem(char, e) end
    end

    local function applyHeadlessKorblox(char)
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            hide(head)
            head.CanCollide = false
            head.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
            if face then face.Transparency = 1 end
        end
        for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Accessory") then
                local w = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld") or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("WeldConstraint") or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
                if w then
                    local p0, p1 = w.Part0, w.Part1
                    if (p0 and p0.Name == "Head") or (p1 and p1.Name == "Head") then
                        removedAccessories[#removedAccessories + 1] = v
                        v.Parent = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local targetPart = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
        if targetPart then
            for _, n in ipairs({ "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" }) do
                hide(char:FindFirstChild(n))
            end
            local objects = loadObjects(139607718)
            if objects then
                local mesh = collectParts(objects)[1]
                if mesh then
                    local leg = mesh:Clone()
                    leg.Name = TAG .. "korblox"
                    addFollow(char, leg, "RightUpperLeg", CF(), CF())
                end
                destroyAll(objects)
            end
        end
    end

    local function applyStandardOutfit(char, cfg)
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end

        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("CharacterMesh") and d.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then
                pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
            end
        end

        local done = false
        if head:IsA("MeshPart") then
            done = pcall(function()
                head.MeshId = cfg.headMesh
                if cfg.headTexture then head.TextureID = cfg.headTexture end
            end)
        end
        if not done then
            local sm = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh") or Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            sm.Parent = head
            sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            sm.MeshId = cfg.headMesh
            sm.TextureId = cfg.headTexture or ""
        end

        if cfg.shirt then
            local s = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt") or Instance.new("Shirt")
            s.ShirtTemplate = cfg.shirt
            s.Parent = char
        end
        if cfg.pants then
            local p = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants") or Instance.new("Pants")
            p.PantsTemplate = cfg.pants
            p.Parent = char
        end

        if cfg.accessory then
            local objs = loadObjects(cfg.accessory)
            if objs then
                local handle
                for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                    if o:IsA("BasePart") then handle = o; break end
                    local f = o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if f then handle = f; break end
                end
                if handle then
                    local h = handle:Clone()
                    h.Name = TAG .. "accessory"
                    addFollow(char, h, "Head", CF(cfg.offset), CF())
                end
                destroyAll(objs)
            end
        end

        if cfg.headless then applyHeadlessKorblox(char) end
    end

    local function applyOutfit(cfg)
        local char = LP.Character
        if not (cfg and char) then return end
        waitReady(char, 10)
        if LP.Character ~= char or M.current ~= cfg then return end
        if not snap then takeSnapshot(char) end
        task.wait(0.2)
        if LP.Character ~= char or M.current ~= cfg then return end

        restoreDefault(char)
        if cfg.homero then
            applyHomero(char)
        else
            applyStandardOutfit(char, cfg)
        end
    end

    local busy, pending = false, nil
    function M.apply(cfg)
        M.active = true
        M.current = cfg
        if busy then pending = cfg; return end
        busy = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local target = cfg
            while target do
                pending = nil
                local ok, err = pcall(applyOutfit, target)
                if not ok then warn("[CelestialSkin] " .. tostring(err)) end
                target = pending
            end
            busy = false
        end)
    end

    function M.clear()
        M.active = false
        M.current = nil
        pending = nil
        local char = LP.Character
        if char then pcall(restoreDefault, char) end
    end

    -- re-apply if the game strips a piece
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1.5)
            if needReapply and M.active and M.current then
                needReapply = false
                M.apply(M.current)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- per-character setup (snapshot of the untouched look)
    local setupToken = 0
    local function setupCharacter(char)
        setupToken = setupToken + 1
        local my = setupToken
        snap, removedAccessories = nil, {}
        followItems = {}
        needReapply = false
        waitReady(char, 12)
        task.wait(0.3)
        if my ~= setupToken or LP.Character ~= char then return end
        if not snap then takeSnapshot(char) end
        if M.active and M.current then M.apply(M.current) end
    end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) task.spawn(setupCharacter, char) end)
    if LP.Character then task.spawn(setupCharacter, LP.Character) end

    -- add the outfits to the Career Outfits list (after the originals, so saved indexes stay valid)
    local list = OUTFIT_LIST
    list[#list + 1] = { label = "SMVLL Outfit 1", smvll = true, accessory = 10159600649, offset = V3(0, 1, -0.2),
        shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9683332638", pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=93182020184041",
        headMesh = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=134079402", headTexture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=133940918 ", headless = true }
    list[#list + 1] = { label = "SMVLL Outfit 2", smvll = true, accessory = 1744060292, offset = V3(0, 1.3, -0.2),
        shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9683332638", pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=93182020184041",
        headMesh = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=134079402", headTexture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=133940918 ", headless = true }
    list[#list + 1] = { label = "SMVLL Outfit 3", smvll = true, accessory = 8349240186, offset = V3(0, 0.9, 0),
        shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=11926549070", pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=13189494471",
        headMesh = "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=16673245747", headless = true }
    list[#list + 1] = { label = "SMVLL Outfit 4", smvll = true, accessory = 121097973925756, offset = V3(0, 0.9, 0),
        shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=123181702116947", pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=93330291631062",
        headMesh = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=134079402", headTexture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=133940918 ", headless = true }
    list[#list + 1] = { label = "Homero Chino", smvll = true, homero = true }
end)()

local _savedBodyColors = nil

local function captureBodyColors(char)
    local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
    if not bc then return nil end
    return {
        Head = bc.HeadColor3,
        Torso = bc.TorsoColor3,
        LeftArm = bc.LeftArmColor3,
        RightArm = bc.RightArmColor3,
        LeftLeg = bc.LeftLegColor3,
        RightLeg = bc.RightLegColor3,
    }
end

local function applyBodyColors(char, colors)
    if not colors then return end
    local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
    if not bc then
        bc = Instance.new("BodyColors")
        bc.Name = OUTFIT_TAG .. "BodyColors"
        bc.Parent = char
    end
    pcall(function()
        if colors.Head then bc.HeadColor3 = colors.Head end
        if colors.Torso then bc.TorsoColor3 = colors.Torso end
        if colors.LeftArm then bc.LeftArmColor3 = colors.LeftArm end
        if colors.RightArm then bc.RightArmColor3 = colors.RightArm end
        if colors.LeftLeg then bc.LeftLegColor3 = colors.LeftLeg end
        if colors.RightLeg then bc.RightLegColor3 = colors.RightLeg end
    end)
    -- also tint BaseParts directly (stronger local visual on R15/R6)
    local map = {
        Head = colors.Head,
        Torso = colors.Torso,
        ["UpperTorso"] = colors.Torso,
        ["LowerTorso"] = colors.Torso,
        ["Left Arm"] = colors.LeftArm,
        ["Right Arm"] = colors.RightArm,
        ["Left Leg"] = colors.LeftLeg,
        ["Right Leg"] = colors.RightLeg,
        LeftUpperArm = colors.LeftArm,
        LeftLowerArm = colors.LeftArm,
        LeftHand = colors.LeftArm,
        RightUpperArm = colors.RightArm,
        RightLowerArm = colors.RightArm,
        RightHand = colors.RightArm,
        LeftUpperLeg = colors.LeftLeg,
        LeftLowerLeg = colors.LeftLeg,
        LeftFoot = colors.LeftLeg,
        RightUpperLeg = colors.RightLeg,
        RightLowerLeg = colors.RightLeg,
        RightFoot = colors.RightLeg,
    }
    for name, col in pairs(map) do
        if col then
            local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function() part.Color = col end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function applyLocalShirtPants(char, shirtUrl, pantsUrl)
    if shirtUrl then
        local s = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
        if not s then
            s = Instance.new("Shirt")
            s.Name = OUTFIT_TAG .. "Shirt"
            s.Parent = char
        end
        pcall(function() s.ShirtTemplate = shirtUrl end)
    end
    if pantsUrl then
        local p = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
        if not p then
            p = Instance.new("Pants")
            p.Name = OUTFIT_TAG .. "Pants"
            p.Parent = char
        end
        pcall(function() p.PantsTemplate = pantsUrl end)
    end
end

function applyOutfitByIndex(index)
    local cfg = OUTFIT_LIST and OUTFIT_LIST[index]
    if not cfg then return end
    currentOutfitIndex = index
    if outfitSelectorLabel then
        outfitSelectorLabel.Text = cfg.label
    end

    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end

    -- SMVLL skin-changer outfits (added). Original outfit paths below are unchanged.
    if cfg.smvll then
        -- undo Celestial's own colors / clothing first so the skin starts from a clean look
        if _savedBodyColors then applyBodyColors(char, _savedBodyColors) end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if tostring(child.Name):sub(1, #OUTFIT_TAG) == OUTFIT_TAG then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        if SMVLLOutfits then SMVLLOutfits.apply(cfg) end
        return
    elseif SMVLLOutfits and SMVLLOutfits.active then
        SMVLLOutfits.clear()
    end

    if not _savedBodyColors then
        _savedBodyColors = captureBodyColors(char)
    end

    if cfg.label == "None" or (not cfg.colors and not cfg.shirt and not cfg.pants) then
        if _savedBodyColors then
            applyBodyColors(char, _savedBodyColors)
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if tostring(child.Name):sub(1, #OUTFIT_TAG) == OUTFIT_TAG then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        return
    end

    -- Apply colors first (most visible local change)
    if cfg.colors then
        applyBodyColors(char, cfg.colors)
        -- keep re-applying briefly so game systems don't overwrite
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 8 do
                task.wait(0.05)
                if LP.Character ~= char then return end
                if currentOutfitIndex ~= index then return end
                applyBodyColors(char, cfg.colors)
            end
        end)
    end
    applyLocalShirtPants(char, cfg.shirt, cfg.pants)
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    _savedBodyColors = nil
    task.delay(0.6, function()
        _savedBodyColors = captureBodyColors(char)
        if currentOutfitIndex and currentOutfitIndex > 1 then
            pcall(function() applyOutfitByIndex(currentOutfitIndex) end)
        end
    end)
end)

function applyCharterToChar(char)
    if not char then return end
    applyHeadlessToChar(char, headlessEnabled)
    applyKorbloxToChar(char, korbloxEnabled)
end
function waitForAnimate(char)
    for _ = 1, 40 do
        local a = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if a and a:FindFirstChild("idle") and a:FindFirstChild("run") and a:FindFirstChild("walk") then
            return a
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return nil
end

function setAnim(animObj, id)
    if animObj and id then
        animObj.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
    end
end

function stopAllTracks(hum)
    if not hum then return end
    for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
    end
end

function ensureAnim(folder, name)
    if not folder then return nil end
    local a = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not a then
        a = Instance.new("Animation")
        a.Name = name
        a.Parent = folder
    end
    return a
end

function ensureIdleSlots(idleFolder, n)
    if not idleFolder then return end
    n = n or 2
    for i=1,n do
        ensureAnim(idleFolder, "Animation" .. i)
    end
end

function pickAnim(pack, ...)
    for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
        local k = select(i, ...)
        local v = pack[k]
        if v ~= nil then return v end
    end
    return nil
end

local applyingAnim = false
function applyAnimPack(packName)
    if applyingAnim then return false end
    applyingAnim = true

    local pack = PACKS[packName]
    if not pack then
        applyingAnim = false
        return false
    end

    local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local animate = waitForAnimate(char)
    if not animate then
        applyingAnim = false
        return false
    end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    stopAllTracks(hum)

    local runObj   = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("run"),   "RunAnim")
    local walkObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("walk"),  "WalkAnim")
    local jumpObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("jump"),  "JumpAnim")
    local fallObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("fall"),  "FallAnim")
    local climbObj = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("climb"), "ClimbAnim")
    local swimObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("swim"),     "Swim")
    local swimIdleObj = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("swimidle"), "SwimIdle")
    local idleFolder = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")

    setAnim(walkObj,  pickAnim(pack, "WalkAnim", "Walk"))
    setAnim(runObj,   pickAnim(pack, "RunAnim", "Run"))
    setAnim(jumpObj,  pickAnim(pack, "JumpAnim", "Jump"))
    setAnim(fallObj,  pickAnim(pack, "FallAnim", "Fall"))
    setAnim(climbObj, pickAnim(pack, "ClimbAnim", "Climb"))
    setAnim(swimObj,      pickAnim(pack, "Swim"))
    setAnim(swimIdleObj,  pickAnim(pack, "SwimIdle") or pickAnim(pack, "Swim"))

    if idleFolder then
        local a1 = pickAnim(pack, "Animation1")
        local a2 = pickAnim(pack, "Animation2")
        if a1 or a2 then
            ensureIdleSlots(idleFolder, 2)
            local id1 = a1 or a2
            local id2 = a2 or a1 or id1
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), id1)
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), id2)
        elseif pack.Idle and #pack.Idle > 0 then
            ensureIdleSlots(idleFolder, math.max(2, #pack.Idle))
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), pack.Idle[1])
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), pack.Idle[2] or pack.Idle[1])
            for i = 3, #pack.Idle do
                local a = idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation" .. i)
                if a then setAnim(a, pack.Idle[i]) end
            end
        end
    end

    animate.Disabled = true
    task.wait(0.06)
    animate.Disabled = false

    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
            task.wait(0.03)
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end)
    end

    animPack = packName
    applyingAnim = false
    return true
end

-- Extra button colours + custom hex (Settings > Buttons Color / Custom Color)
mobileBtnCustomHex = "8B5A2B"
function mobMakePalette(c)
    return {
        bg = c,
        border = c:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.45),
        deep = c:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.55),
        shine = c:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.7),
    }
end
function mobParseHex(s)
    s = tostring(s or ""):gsub("[#%s]", ""):upper()
    if #s == 3 then s = s:sub(1,1):rep(2) .. s:sub(2,2):rep(2) .. s:sub(3,3):rep(2) end
    if not s:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x$") then return nil end
    return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(s:sub(1,2), 16), tonumber(s:sub(3,4), 16), tonumber(s:sub(5,6), 16))
end
function mobRefreshCustom()
    MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Custom = mobMakePalette(mobParseHex(mobileBtnCustomHex) or Color3.fromRGB(139, 90, 43))
end
MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Brown = mobMakePalette(Color3.fromRGB(150, 92, 42))
MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Red   = mobMakePalette(Color3.fromRGB(235, 55, 60))
MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Cyan  = mobMakePalette(Color3.fromRGB(40, 220, 235))
MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.White = mobMakePalette(Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245))
MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Teal  = mobMakePalette(Color3.fromRGB(25, 180, 160))
MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Lime  = mobMakePalette(Color3.fromRGB(160, 235, 50))
mobRefreshCustom()

    table.insert(MOB_GLOW_ORDER, n)
end
