function mobB64Decode(s)
    local alpha="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
    local map={}
    for i=1,64 do map[alpha:byte(i)]=i-1 end
    local out,n,buf,bits={},0,0,0
    for i=1,#s do
        local v=map[s:byte(i)]
        if v then
            buf=buf*64+v;bits=bits+6
            if bits>=8 then
                bits=bits-8
                local p=2^bits
                local byte=math.floor(buf/p)
                buf=buf-byte*p
                n=n+1;out[n]=string.char(byte)
            end
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end
MOB_IMG_CACHE={}
function mobGetBtnImage(name)
    if not name or name=="Off" then return nil end
    if MOB_IMG_CACHE[name] then return MOB_IMG_CACHE[name] end
    local id=MOB_BTN_IMAGE_IDS[name]
    if type(id)=="string" and id:match("^%d+$") then id="rbxassetid://"..id end
    if type(id)=="string" and id~="" then MOB_IMG_CACHE[name]=id;return id end
    local b64=MOB_BTN_IMG_DATA[name]
    if b64 and _getcustomasset and writefile then
        local path="celestial_btn_"..string.lower(name)..".jpg"
        local ok,asset=pcall(function()
            writefile(path,mobB64Decode(b64))
            return _getcustomasset(path)
        end)
        if ok and asset and asset~="" then MOB_IMG_CACHE[name]=asset;return asset end
    end
    return nil
end
-- when an image is selected the glow/stroke colour follows the image colour
function mobEffectiveGlow()
    if mobileBtnImage and mobileBtnImage~="Off" and MOB_GLOW_PALETTE[mobileBtnImage] then return mobileBtnImage end
    return mobileBtnGlowColor
end
function mobAttachBtnImage(frame,cornerR)
    if not mobileBtnImage or mobileBtnImage=="Off" then return nil end
    local asset=mobGetBtnImage(mobileBtnImage)
    if not asset then return nil end
    local img=Instance.new("ImageLabel",frame)
    img.Name="BtnImage"
    img.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    img.BackgroundTransparency=1
    img.BorderSizePixel=0
    img.Image=asset
    img.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    img.ImageTransparency=0.3
    img.ZIndex=102
    Instance.new("UICorner",img).CornerRadius=cornerR
    return img
end

-- stretchPower 1-10 (higher = more stretched). Maps to matrix Y scale.
stretchPower=7
local function stretchAmountFromPower(p)
    p = math.clamp(tonumber(p) or 7, 1, 10)
    -- power 1 ≈ 0.90 (mild), power 10 ≈ 0.25 (extreme)
    return math.clamp(1.0 - (p / 10) * 0.75, 0.25, 0.95)
end
stretchAmount=stretchAmountFromPower(stretchPower)

MOB_POS_FILE="celestialduels_btnpos.json"
forceDefaultBtnPos=false
activeMobDrag=nil -- only one mobile button can drag at a time
local function loadBtnPositions()
    local data={}
    -- primary file (don't require isfile - many executors only have readfile)
    pcall(function()
        local raw=readfile(MOB_POS_FILE)
        if raw and #raw>2 then
            local decoded=HS:JSONDecode(raw)
            if type(decoded)=="table" then data=decoded end
        end
    end)
    -- fallback: positions embedded in main config
    if not data or next(data)==nil then
        pcall(function()
            local raw=readfile("celestialduels.json")
            if raw then
                local cfg=HS:JSONDecode(raw)
                if type(cfg)=="table" and type(cfg.btnPositions)=="table" and next(cfg.btnPositions)~=nil then
                    data=cfg.btnPositions
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    return data or {}
end
local function saveBtnPositions()
    if not mobGuiRef then return end
    local out={}
    for _,child in ipairs(mobGuiRef:GetDescendants()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") and child:GetAttribute("BtnKey") then
            local key=child:GetAttribute("BtnKey")
            out[key]={xs=0,xo=child.Position.X.Offset,ys=0,yo=child.Position.Y.Offset}
        end
    end
    -- also keep any keys already saved that might not be in tree yet
    if next(out)==nil then return end
    if writefile then
        pcall(function() writefile(MOB_POS_FILE,HS:JSONEncode(out)) end)
    end
    -- mirror into main config so it survives executor file quirks
    if writefile and isfile and isfile("celestialduels.json") then
        pcall(function()
            local cfg=HS:JSONDecode(readfile("celestialduels.json"))
            if type(cfg)=="table" then
                cfg.btnPositions=out
                writefile("celestialduels.json",HS:JSONEncode(cfg))
            end
        end)
    end
end
-- autosave every 2s while buttons exist
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(2)
        pcall(saveBtnPositions)
    end
end)

local function addShimmerToLabel(lbl,color1,color2)
    local gr=Instance.new("UIGradient",lbl)
    gr.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,color1 or Color3.fromRGB(160,80,255)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,color2 or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,color1 or Color3.fromRGB(160,80,255))})
    gr.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.3,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.3,0)})
    return gr
end
fovConn=nil
local function applyFOV()
    if fovConn then fovConn:Disconnect(); fovConn=nil end
    -- when Stretch Rez is on, stretch FOV loop owns the camera FOV
    if stretchEnabled then return end
    fovConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if stretchEnabled then return end
        local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then cam.FieldOfView=fovValue end
    end)
end
applyFOV()

local function applyStretchFOV(val)
    local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then pcall(function() cam.FieldOfView=val end) end
end

function enableStretch()
    if stretchConn then return end
    stretchEnabled=true
    local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    if origFOV==nil then origFOV=cam.FieldOfView or 70 end
    -- stop normal FOV loop while stretched
    if fovConn then fovConn:Disconnect(); fovConn=nil end
    applyStretchFOV(stretchFOV)
    -- vertical stretch matrix (same as Clean Hub)
    stretchConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not stretchEnabled then
            if stretchConn then stretchConn:Disconnect(); stretchConn=nil end
            return
        end
        local c=workspace.CurrentCamera
        if c then
            -- stretchAmount ~0.4 = strong vertical stretch (0.7 was mild)
            local s = (type(stretchAmount)=="number" and stretchAmount) or 0.4
            c.CFrame=c.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,0,0,1,0,0,0,s,0,0,0,1)
        end
    end)
    if stretchFovConn then stretchFovConn:Disconnect() end
    stretchFovConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if stretchEnabled then
            applyStretchFOV(stretchFOV)
        else
            if stretchFovConn then stretchFovConn:Disconnect(); stretchFovConn=nil end
        end
    end)
end

function disableStretch()
    stretchEnabled=false
    if stretchConn then stretchConn:Disconnect(); stretchConn=nil end
    if stretchFovConn then stretchFovConn:Disconnect(); stretchFovConn=nil end
    local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        pcall(function() cam.FieldOfView=(origFOV or fovValue or 70) end)
    end
    origFOV=nil
    -- restore normal FOV loop
    applyFOV()
end

-- ============================================================
-- 2.5 SECOND RAGDOLL TIMER (themed)
-- ============================================================
do
    local timerBillboard = nil
    local timerLabel = nil
    local timerActive = false
    local timerConn = nil
    local ragdollCheckConn = nil
    local lastRagdollState = false

    local function getRagdollTheme()
        local th = (type(getTheme)=="function" and getTheme()) or nil
        if type(th)~="table" then
            th = (GUI_THEMES and GUI_THEMES[currentGuiTheme]) or nil
        end
        if type(th)~="table" then
            return {
                BG = Color3.fromRGB(8,8,10),
                ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
                ACCENT2 = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,210),
                WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(235,235,240),
                ROW_BORDER = Color3.fromRGB(32,32,36),
            }
        end
        return th
    end

    local function createTimerBillboard()
        if timerBillboard then timerBillboard:Destroy() end
        
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end

        local th = getRagdollTheme()
        local accent = th.ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        local bgCol = th.BG or Color3.fromRGB(8,8,10)
        local border = th.ROW_BORDER or Color3.fromRGB(32,32,36)
        
        timerBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        timerBillboard.Name = "CELESTIALRagdollTimer"
        timerBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 56)
        timerBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5.6, 0)
        timerBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        timerBillboard.MaxDistance = 120
        timerBillboard.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        timerBillboard.Parent = head
        
        local bgFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        bgFrame.Name = "Bg"
        bgFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundColor3 = bgCol
        bgFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
        bgFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bgFrame.Parent = timerBillboard
        Instance.new("UICorner", bgFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
        
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", bgFrame)
        stroke.Name = "ThemeStroke"
        stroke.Color = accent
        stroke.Thickness = 1.8
        stroke.Transparency = 0.25

        -- thin top accent bar
        local topBar = Instance.new("Frame")
        topBar.Name = "TopAccent"
        topBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
        topBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        topBar.BackgroundColor3 = accent
        topBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        topBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        topBar.ZIndex = 3
        topBar.Parent = bgFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", topBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

        timerLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        timerLabel.Name = "TimerText"
        timerLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -6)
        timerLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
        timerLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        timerLabel.Text = "2.50"
        timerLabel.TextColor3 = accent
        timerLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        timerLabel.TextSize = 26
        timerLabel.TextScaled = true
        timerLabel.ZIndex = 4
        timerLabel.Parent = bgFrame
        local tConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
        tConstraint.MinTextSize = 14
        tConstraint.MaxTextSize = 28
        tConstraint.Parent = timerLabel
        
        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
    end

    local function updateTimerDisplay(time, isReady)
        if not timerLabel or not timerBillboard then return end
        local th = getRagdollTheme()
        local accent = th.ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        local accent2 = th.ACCENT2 or accent
        local bgCol = th.BG or Color3.fromRGB(8,8,10)
        local white = th.WHITE or Color3.fromRGB(235,235,240)
        
        if isReady then
            timerLabel.Text = "READY"
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = accent
            timerLabel.TextScaled = true
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChild("ThemeStroke") or bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = accent
                    stroke.Thickness = 2.4
                    stroke.Transparency = 0.05
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = bgCol
                local top = bg:FindFirstChild("TopAccent")
                if top then
                    top.BackgroundColor3 = accent
                    top.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                end
            end
        else
            timerLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", time)
            -- blend accent as time counts down (warmer near end)
            local t = math.clamp(time / 2.5, 0, 1)
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = accent:Lerp(white, (1 - t) * 0.25)
            timerLabel.TextScaled = true
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChild("ThemeStroke") or bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = accent
                    stroke.Thickness = 1.6 + (1 - t) * 0.8
                    stroke.Transparency = 0.15 + t * 0.15
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = bgCol
                local top = bg:FindFirstChild("TopAccent")
                if top then
                    top.BackgroundColor3 = accent
                    top.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function startRagdollTimer()
        if timerActive then return end
        
        if not timerBillboard or not timerBillboard.Parent then
            createTimerBillboard()
            if not timerBillboard then return end
        end
        
        timerActive = true
        timerBillboard.Enabled = true
        local startTime = tick()
        local duration = 2.5
        
        if timerConn then timerConn:Disconnect() end
        timerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not timerActive then 
                timerConn:Disconnect()
                timerConn = nil
                return 
            end
            
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then
                timerActive = false
                if timerBillboard then timerBillboard.Enabled = false end
                if timerConn then timerConn:Disconnect(); timerConn = nil end
                return
            end
            
            local elapsed = tick() - startTime
            local remaining = math.max(duration - elapsed, 0)
            
            if remaining <= 0 then
                timerActive = false
                updateTimerDisplay(0, true)
                
                if timerConn then timerConn:Disconnect(); timerConn = nil end
                
                task.delay(1.5, function()
                    if timerBillboard then timerBillboard.Enabled = false end
                    timerActive = false
                end)
                return
            end
            
            updateTimerDisplay(remaining, false)
        end)
    end

    local function checkRagdoll()
        if ragdollCheckConn then return end
        
        ragdollCheckConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            
            local isRagdolled = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or 
                               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or 
                               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            
            if isRagdolled and not lastRagdollState and not timerActive then
                if not timerBillboard or not timerBillboard.Parent then
                    createTimerBillboard()
                end
                startRagdollTimer()
            end
            
            lastRagdollState = isRagdolled
        end)
    end
    
    task.spawn(checkRagdoll)
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- CELESTIAL INTRO (10s): creepy hands + watching eyes -> "CelestialDuels | MOGS"
    -- -> 4 hubs get MOGGED one by one (slam, shockwave, sparks, cracks, bass hit)
    -- -> end. Click / tap / Space / Enter / Esc or the SKIP button skips it.
    -- ============================================================
    playIntroAnimation = function()
        if introEnabled == false then return end
        _celestialIntroActive = true

        local INTRO_TIME = 10
        local FADE_OUT = 1.2
        local TITLE_IN, TITLE_OUT = 1.0, 2.3
        local BOARD_IN = 2.45
        local FOCUS_FIRST, SLOT, FOCUS_TO_MOG = 2.7, 1.35, 0.45
        local FINAL_T = 7.9
        local TITLE_TEXT = "CelestialDuels | MOGS"
        local MUSIC_VOLUME = 0.6
        local MOGGED_SOUND_ID = "rbxassetid://132182797103598"
        local ASSETS = {
            IrishHub = "rbxassetid://131513419838182",
            GreenDuels = "rbxassetid://79052318194557",
            VampHub = "rbxassetid://108632168923336",
            AceDuels = "rbxassetid://86968392795085",
        }

        local RED = Color3.fromRGB(229, 22, 34)
        local RED_BRIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 76)
        local BLACK = Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 7)
        local BLACK_2 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 16)
        local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 245)
        local CLAW = Color3.fromRGB(38, 12, 14)

        local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
        local TweenService2 = game:GetService("TweenService")
        local RunService2 = game:GetService("RunService")
        local UIS2 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local SoundService2 = game:GetService("SoundService")
        local Debris2 = game:GetService("Debris")
        local LP2 = Players2.LocalPlayer
        local playerGui = LP2:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

        -- remove any old intro gui
        pcall(function()
            local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALDuelsMogIntro")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local gh = (typeof(gethui)=="function" and gethui) or _gethui
            if gh then
                local h = gh()
                local o = h and h:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALDuelsMogIntro")
                if o then o:Destroy() end
            end
            local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("CELESTIALDuelsMogIntro")
            if cg then cg:Destroy() end
        end)

        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "CELESTIALDuelsMogIntro"
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        pcall(function() gui.ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.None end) -- cover the whole screen (notch areas too)
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.DisplayOrder = 999999
        gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        local parented = false
        pcall(function()
            if parentGui then parentGui(gui); parented = gui.Parent ~= nil end
        end)
        if not parented then
            pcall(function()
                local gh = (typeof(gethui)=="function" and gethui) or _gethui
                if gh then gui.Parent = gh(); parented = true end
            end)
        end
        if not parented then
            parented = pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        end
        if not parented then gui.Parent = playerGui end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vp = (cam and cam.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
        local k = math.clamp(vp.Y * 0.0024, 0.8, 3)       -- hand scale
        local ek = math.clamp(vp.X / 1000, 0.6, 1.5)      -- eye scale

        local root
        local okGroup = pcall(function()
            root = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
            root.GroupTransparency = 0
        end)
        if not okGroup or not root then root = Instance.new("Frame") end
        root.Name = "IntroRoot"
        root.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        root.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(3, 2, 5)
        root.BorderSizePixel = 0
        root.ClipsDescendants = true
        root.ZIndex = 1
        root.Parent = gui

        local function tween(obj, dur, goal, style, dir)
            local t = TweenService2:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(dur, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goal)
            t:Play()
            return t
        end
        local function round(obj, r)
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
            c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)
            c.Parent = obj
            return c
        end
        local function strokeOn(obj, color, th, tr)
            local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            s.Color = color; s.Thickness = th or 1; s.Transparency = tr or 0
            s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            s.Parent = obj
            return s
        end

        -- ---------- sounds ----------
        local introSong = Instance.new("Sound")
        introSong.Name = "CELESTIALMogIntroSong"
        introSong.Volume = 0
        introSong.Looped = false
        introSong.Parent = SoundService2

        local running = true
        local finishing = false
        local songStarted = false
        local songAssetReady = nil

        local intros = {
            "https://files.catbox.moe/8ch5qz.mp3",
            "https://files.catbox.moe/8oogdu.mp3",
            "https://files.catbox.moe/gm26vl.mp3",
            "https://files.catbox.moe/m5kxm3.mp3",
        }
        local selectedMusic = math.clamp(tonumber(selectedIntroMusic) or 1, 1, 4)
        local songUrl = intros[selectedMusic]
        local cacheName = "CELESTIALHubIntro_" .. tostring(selectedMusic) .. ".mp3"

        local function tryGetCustomAsset(path)
            local asset = nil
            pcall(function()
                if celestialGetCustomAsset then asset = celestialGetCustomAsset(path) end
            end)
            if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
            pcall(function() if getcustomasset then asset = getcustomasset(path) end end)
            if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
            pcall(function() if syn and syn.getcustomasset then asset = syn.getcustomasset(path) end end)
            if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
            return nil
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            local cached = tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
            if cached then songAssetReady = cached; return end
            local body = nil
            pcall(function() if celestialHttpGet then body = celestialHttpGet(songUrl) end end)
            if not body or #body < 1000 then pcall(function() body = game:HttpGet(songUrl) end) end
            if body and #body > 1000 then
                pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(cacheName, body) end end)
                task.wait(0.05)
                songAssetReady = tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
            end
        end)

        -- the song plays from the first moment it is ready until the intro ends
        local function startSong()
            if songStarted or not songAssetReady then return end
            songStarted = true
            pcall(function()
                introSong.SoundId = songAssetReady
                introSong.TimePosition = 0
                introSong.Volume = 0
                introSong:Play()
                tween(introSong, 0.8, {Volume = MUSIC_VOLUME})
            end)
        end

        -- layered "mogged" hit: main impact (reverb + eq) + pitched-down bass thump
        local function playHit(pitch, vol, bass)
            pcall(function()
                local s = Instance.new("Sound")
                s.SoundId = MOGGED_SOUND_ID
                s.Volume = vol
                s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch
                local rv = Instance.new("ReverbSoundEffect", s)
                rv.DecayTime = 1.8; rv.Density = 0.9; rv.Diffusion = 0.9; rv.DryLevel = 0; rv.WetLevel = -8
                local eq = Instance.new("EqualizerSoundEffect", s)
                eq.LowGain = bass and 8 or 3
                eq.MidGain = bass and -10 or 1
                eq.HighGain = bass and -20 or 2
                local ds = Instance.new("DistortionSoundEffect", s)
                ds.Level = bass and 0.25 or 0.1
                s.Parent = SoundService2
                s:Play()
                Debris2:AddItem(s, 6)
            end)
        end

        -- ---------- scene (atmosphere behind everything) ----------
        local scene = Instance.new("Frame")
        scene.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        scene.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        scene.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        scene.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scene.ZIndex = 2
        scene.Parent = root
        local sceneScale = Instance.new("UIScale", scene)
        sceneScale.Scale = 1

        local glow = Instance.new("Frame")
        glow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        glow.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(vp.Y * 1.3, vp.Y * 1.3)
        glow.BackgroundColor3 = RED
        glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.9
        glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        glow.ZIndex = 2
        glow.Parent = scene
        Instance.new("UICorner", glow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local glow2 = glow:Clone()
        glow2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(vp.Y * 0.8, vp.Y * 0.8)
        glow2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
        glow2.Parent = scene

        -- ---------- eyes ----------
        local eyes = {}
        local function makeEye(px, py, w)
            w = w * ek
            local h = w * 0.58
            local eye = Instance.new("Frame")
            eye.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            eye.Position = UDim2.fromScale(px, py)
            eye.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 0)
            eye.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(238, 228, 218)
            eye.BorderSizePixel = 0
            eye.ClipsDescendants = true
            eye.ZIndex = 5
            eye.Parent = scene
            Instance.new("UICorner", eye).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", eye)
            st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 24, 28); st.Thickness = math.max(2, h * 0.07); st.Transparency = 0.1
            local eg = Instance.new("UIGradient", eye)
            eg.Rotation = 90
            eg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(140, 105, 105)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 244, 238)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(160, 122, 122)),
            })

            for v = 1, 6 do
                local vein = Instance.new("Frame")
                local left = (v % 2 == 0)
                vein.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(left and 0 or 1, 0.5)
                vein.Position = UDim2.new(left and 0.02 or 0.98, 0, 0.5, (v - 3.5) * h * 0.1)
                vein.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * (0.16 + 0.03 * v), math.max(1, h * 0.02))
                vein.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 28, 38)
                vein.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                vein.BorderSizePixel = 0
                vein.Rotation = (left and 1 or -1) * (v - 3.5) * 8
                vein.ZIndex = 6
                vein.Parent = eye
            end

            local irisD = h * 0.8
            local iris = Instance.new("Frame")
            iris.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            iris.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            iris.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(irisD, irisD)
            iris.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            iris.BorderSizePixel = 0
            iris.ZIndex = 7
            iris.Parent = eye
            Instance.new("UICorner", iris).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local ig = Instance.new("UIGradient", iris)
            ig.Rotation = 90
            ig.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 66)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(96, 0, 10)),
            })
            local ist = Instance.new("UIStroke", iris)
            ist.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 0, 6); ist.Thickness = 1.5

            -- iris detail: inner dark ring + fine streaks
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
            ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ring.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            ring.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.62, 0.62)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.ZIndex = 8
            ring.Parent = iris
            Instance.new("UICorner", ring).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local rs = Instance.new("UIStroke", ring)
            rs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 0, 8); rs.Thickness = math.max(1, irisD * 0.05); rs.Transparency = 0.25
            for sI = 1, 8 do
                local streak = Instance.new("Frame")
                streak.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
                streak.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                streak.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, irisD * 0.025), irisD * 0.34)
                streak.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 100)
                streak.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
                streak.BorderSizePixel = 0
                streak.Rotation = sI * 45
                streak.ZIndex = 8
                streak.Parent = iris
            end

            local pupilD = irisD * 0.42
            local pupil = Instance.new("Frame")
            pupil.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            pupil.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            pupil.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pupilD * 0.55, pupilD)
            pupil.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            pupil.BorderSizePixel = 0
            pupil.ZIndex = 9
            pupil.Parent = iris
            Instance.new("UICorner", pupil).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

            local shine = Instance.new("Frame")
            shine.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            shine.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 0.28)
            shine.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(irisD * 0.16, irisD * 0.16)
            shine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            shine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
            shine.BorderSizePixel = 0
            shine.ZIndex = 10
            shine.Parent = iris
            Instance.new("UICorner", shine).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

            -- wet gloss on the eyeball + eyelid shadow
            local gloss = Instance.new("Frame")
            gloss.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            gloss.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.06)
            gloss.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.6, 0.16)
            gloss.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            gloss.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78
            gloss.BorderSizePixel = 0
            gloss.ZIndex = 11
            gloss.Parent = eye
            Instance.new("UICorner", gloss).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local shade = Instance.new("Frame")
            shade.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            shade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 0, 2)
            shade.BorderSizePixel = 0
            shade.ZIndex = 12
            shade.Parent = eye
            local sg = Instance.new("UIGradient", shade)
            sg.Rotation = 90
            sg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.45),
            })

            local e = {
                frame = eye, iris = iris, pupil = pupil, w = w, h = h, px = px, py = py,
                openAt = 0.2 + math.random() * 1.6, opened = false,
                nextBlink = 4 + math.random() * 2, phase = math.random() * 6, scale = 1,
                pupilD = pupilD,
            }
            eyes[#eyes + 1] = e
            return e
        end

        local EYE_LAYOUT = {
            {0.08, 0.14, 130}, {0.22, 0.08, 74}, {0.37, 0.16, 58}, {0.63, 0.15, 58}, {0.78, 0.08, 86},
            {0.92, 0.16, 120}, {0.05, 0.46, 70}, {0.95, 0.47, 76}, {0.1, 0.8, 110}, {0.26, 0.9, 66},
            {0.41, 0.85, 52}, {0.59, 0.87, 54}, {0.75, 0.91, 76}, {0.9, 0.82, 128}, {0.5, 0.06, 46},
            {0.5, 0.94, 48}, {0.16, 0.3, 40}, {0.84, 0.32, 42}, {0.17, 0.66, 44}, {0.83, 0.68, 46},
        }
        for _, l in ipairs(EYE_LAYOUT) do makeEye(l[1], l[2], l[3]) end
        local touchOnly = UIS2.TouchEnabled and not UIS2.MouseEnabled

        -- ---------- creepy hands ----------
        local hands = {}
        local FINGER_DEFS = {
            {bx = -27, by = -36, a = -14, lens = {20, 16, 12}, widths = {13, 11, 8}},
            {bx = -9,  by = -37, a = -5,  lens = {27, 22, 17}, widths = {14, 12, 9}},
            {bx = 9,   by = -37, a = 4,   lens = {30, 24, 18}, widths = {14, 12, 9}},
            {bx = 27,  by = -36, a = 13,  lens = {25, 20, 15}, widths = {13, 11, 8}},
            {bx = -34, by = 10,  a = -64, lens = {24, 20},     widths = {16, 13}},
        }
        local function shadeColor(c, depth)
            return c:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1 - depth)
        end
        local function makeSeg(parent, w, len, color, z, nail)
            local f = Instance.new("Frame")
            f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, len)
            f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0
            f.ZIndex = z
            f.Parent = parent
            Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.5, 0)
            local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", f)
            g.Rotation = 0
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.35)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, color),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, color:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.45)),
            })
            local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
            s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 9); s.Thickness = 1.3
            -- knuckle crease
            if not nail then
                local cr = Instance.new("Frame")
                cr.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                cr.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.82)
                cr.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 1)
                cr.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 40, 40)
                cr.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                cr.BorderSizePixel = 0
                cr.ZIndex = z
                cr.Parent = f
            end
            return f
        end
        local function makeHand(sx, sy, ex, ey, rot, delay, sizeMul, depth)
            depth = depth or 1
            local hk = k * (sizeMul or 1)
            local skin = shadeColor(Color3.fromRGB(198, 184, 172), depth)
            local skinDark = shadeColor(Color3.fromRGB(150, 134, 126), depth)
            local c = Instance.new("Frame")
            c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            c.Position = UDim2.fromScale(sx, sy)
            c.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300 * hk, 300 * hk)
            c.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            c.Rotation = rot
            c.ZIndex = 10
            c.Parent = scene

            local arm = Instance.new("Frame")
            arm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            arm.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 30 * hk)
            arm.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44 * hk, 420 * hk)
            arm.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            arm.BorderSizePixel = 0
            arm.ZIndex = 9
            arm.Parent = c
            local ag = Instance.new("UIGradient", arm)
            ag.Rotation = 90
            ag.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, skin),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(20, 14, 16)),
            })
            local ast = Instance.new("UIStroke", arm)
            ast.Color = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 9); ast.Thickness = 1.3
            -- veins on the arm
            for vI = 1, 3 do
                local vn = Instance.new("Frame")
                vn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
                vn.Position = UDim2.new(0.25 + vI * 0.2, 0, 0, 10 * hk)
                vn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 2 * hk), 150 * hk)
                vn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 100, 130)
                vn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
                vn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                vn.Rotation = (vI - 2) * 4
                vn.ZIndex = 9
                vn.Parent = arm
            end

            local palm = Instance.new("Frame")
            palm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            palm.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            palm.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74 * hk, 80 * hk)
            palm.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            palm.BorderSizePixel = 0
            palm.ZIndex = 11
            palm.Parent = c
            Instance.new("UICorner", palm).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.22, 0)
            local pst = Instance.new("UIStroke", palm)
            pst.Color = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 9); pst.Thickness = 1.3
            local pg = Instance.new("UIGradient", palm)
            pg.Rotation = 90
            pg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, skin:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.08)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, skinDark),
            })
            -- palm creases
            for cI = 1, 3 do
                local cr = Instance.new("Frame")
                cr.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                cr.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.3 + cI * 0.16)
                cr.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.55 - cI * 0.08, 0)
                cr.Size = UDim2.new(0.55 - cI * 0.08, 0, 0, 1)
                cr.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 48, 48)
                cr.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                cr.BorderSizePixel = 0
                cr.Rotation = (cI - 2) * 7
                cr.ZIndex = 12
                cr.Parent = palm
            end

            local fingers = {}
            for fi, d in ipairs(FINGER_DEFS) do
                local f = {
                    bx = d.bx * hk, by = d.by * hk, a = d.a, lens = {}, segs = {},
                    phase = math.random() * 6.28, speed = 1.4 + math.random() * 2.2,
                    curlMax = 0.55 + math.random() * 0.7, dir = (d.bx <= 0) and 1 or -1,
                    tremor = math.random() * 6.28,
                }
                for si = 1, #d.lens do
                    f.lens[si] = d.lens[si] * hk
                    local isTip = (si == #d.lens)
                    local col = isTip and shadeColor(CLAW, depth) or (si == 1 and skin or skinDark)
                    f.segs[si] = makeSeg(c, d.widths[si] * hk, d.lens[si] * hk + 2, col, 12 + si, isTip)
                end
                fingers[fi] = f
            end

            local h = {
                frame = c, fingers = fingers, sx = sx, sy = sy, ex = ex, ey = ey, rot = rot,
                delay = delay, started = false, phase = math.random() * 6.28, hk = hk,
            }
            hands[#hands + 1] = h
            return h
        end

        -- start (off screen) -> end, rotation, delay, size, depth (darker = further away)
        makeHand(-0.18, 0.36, 0.13, 0.40, 90, 0.4, 1.0, 1.0)
        makeHand(1.18, 0.62, 0.87, 0.58, -90, 0.6, 1.0, 1.0)
        makeHand(0.20, 1.30, 0.22, 0.84, 0, 0.8, 1.0, 0.9)
        makeHand(0.80, 1.30, 0.78, 0.86, 12, 1.0, 1.0, 0.9)
        makeHand(0.27, -0.30, 0.30, 0.16, 180, 0.7, 0.9, 0.8)
        makeHand(0.73, -0.30, 0.70, 0.18, 170, 0.9, 0.9, 0.8)
        makeHand(0.50, 1.35, 0.50, 1.00, 0, 1.2, 1.2, 0.7)
        makeHand(-0.18, 0.84, 0.08, 0.74, 66, 1.3, 0.85, 0.85)

        -- scene dimmer (darkens the creepy backdrop behind the board)
        local sceneDim = Instance.new("Frame")
        sceneDim.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        sceneDim.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        sceneDim.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sceneDim.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sceneDim.ZIndex = 20
        sceneDim.Parent = root

        -- ---------- board with the 4 hubs ----------
        local bw = math.min(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.95)
        local bh = bw * 0.94
        local boardGroup
        local okBG = pcall(function()
            boardGroup = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
            boardGroup.GroupTransparency = 1
        end)
        if not okBG or not boardGroup then boardGroup = Instance.new("Frame") end
        boardGroup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        boardGroup.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        boardGroup.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bw, bh)
        boardGroup.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        boardGroup.ZIndex = 30
        boardGroup.Visible = false
        boardGroup.Parent = root
        local boardIsGroup = boardGroup:IsA("CanvasGroup")
        local boardScale = Instance.new("UIScale", boardGroup)
        boardScale.Scale = 1.12

        local board = Instance.new("Frame")
        board.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        board.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK
        board.BorderSizePixel = 0
        board.ClipsDescendants = true
        board.Parent = boardGroup
        round(board, 16)
        strokeOn(board, RED, 2, 0.22)

        local boardInner = Instance.new("Frame")
        boardInner.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5)
        boardInner.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10)
        boardInner.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK_2
        boardInner.BorderSizePixel = 0
        boardInner.ClipsDescendants = true
        boardInner.Parent = board
        round(boardInner, 12)

        local function divider(vertical)
            local d = Instance.new("Frame")
            d.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            d.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            d.Size = vertical and UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
            d.BackgroundColor3 = RED
            d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            d.BorderSizePixel = 0
            d.ZIndex = 40
            d.Parent = boardInner
        end
        divider(true); divider(false)

        local panelData = {
            { name = "IRISH HUB",   image = ASSETS.IrishHub,   col = 0, row = 0 },
            { name = "GREEN DUELS", image = ASSETS.GreenDuels, col = 1, row = 0 },
            { name = "VAMP HUB",    image = ASSETS.VampHub,    col = 0, row = 1 },
            { name = "ACE DUELS",   image = ASSETS.AceDuels,   col = 1, row = 1 },
        }
        local panels = {}
        local panelW = (bw - 10) * 0.5

        for order, info in ipairs(panelData) do
            local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
            panel.Name = info.name
            panel.Position = UDim2.fromScale(info.col * 0.5, info.row * 0.5)
            panel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            panel.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK_2
            panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            panel.ClipsDescendants = true
            panel.ZIndex = 10
            panel.Parent = boardInner
            local pScale = Instance.new("UIScale", panel)
            pScale.Scale = 1

            local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            image.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            image.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK_2
            image.BorderSizePixel = 0
            image.Image = info.image
            image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            image.ZIndex = 11
            image.Parent = panel

            local darken = Instance.new("Frame")
            darken.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            darken.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK
            darken.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
            darken.BorderSizePixel = 0
            darken.ZIndex = 12
            darken.Parent = panel

            local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            nameLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 9)
            nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22)
            nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            nameLabel.Text = info.name
            nameLabel.TextColor3 = WHITE
            nameLabel.TextTransparency = 0.15
            nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            nameLabel.TextSize = math.clamp(math.floor(bw / 30), 10, 16)
            nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            nameLabel.ZIndex = 14
            nameLabel.Parent = panel
            local nst = Instance.new("UIStroke", nameLabel)
            nst.Color = BLACK; nst.Thickness = 1.5; nst.Transparency = 0.1

            local cracks = Instance.new("Frame")
            cracks.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            cracks.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            cracks.ZIndex = 16
            cracks.Parent = panel

            local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
            bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            bar.Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.55, 0.56)
            bar.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.24, 0)
            bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bar.ZIndex = 20
            bar.Parent = panel
            strokeOn(bar, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34), 1, 0.28)
            local barScale = Instance.new("UIScale", bar)
            barScale.Scale = 1

            local mogged = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            mogged.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            mogged.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            mogged.Text = "MOGGED"
            mogged.TextColor3 = RED_BRIGHT
            mogged.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            mogged.TextScaled = true
            mogged.ZIndex = 21
            mogged.Parent = bar
            local mc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", mogged)
            mc.MinTextSize = 14; mc.MaxTextSize = 42
            local ms = Instance.new("UIStroke", mogged)
            ms.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 0, 4); ms.Thickness = 1.3; ms.Transparency = 0.12

            local flash = Instance.new("Frame")
            flash.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            flash.BackgroundColor3 = RED_BRIGHT
            flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            flash.BorderSizePixel = 0
            flash.ZIndex = 25
            flash.Parent = panel

            panels[order] = {
                group = panel, scale = pScale, image = image, darken = darken, bar = bar,
                barScale = barScale, flash = flash, cracks = cracks, info = info,
            }
        end

        -- ---------- title ----------
        local titleHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
        titleHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        titleHolder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        titleHolder.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.82, 0.18)
        titleHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        titleHolder.ZIndex = 60
        titleHolder.Visible = false
        titleHolder.Parent = root
        local titleScale = Instance.new("UIScale", titleHolder)
        titleScale.Scale = 1.5

        local function makeTitleLabel(color, z)
            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            l.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            l.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Text = TITLE_TEXT
            l.TextColor3 = color
            l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            l.TextScaled = true
            l.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0
            l.ZIndex = z
            l.Parent = titleHolder
            local c = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", l)
            c.MaxTextSize = 96; c.MinTextSize = 10
            return l
        end
        local ghostR = makeTitleLabel(Color3.fromRGB(255, 20, 40), 61)
        local ghostC = makeTitleLabel(Color3.fromRGB(30, 230, 255), 62)
        local titleLbl = makeTitleLabel(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 63)
        local tg = Instance.new("UIGradient", titleLbl)
        tg.Rotation = 90
        tg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 235)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, RED_BRIGHT),
        })
        local tstroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", titleLbl)
        tstroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0); tstroke.Thickness = 3

        -- ---------- film look: vignette, grain, flicker, flash ----------
        local function edge(anchor, pos, size, rot)
            local f = Instance.new("Frame")
            f.AnchorPoint = anchor
            f.Position = pos
            f.Size = size
            f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0
            f.ZIndex = 70
            local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", f)
            g.Rotation = rot
            g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.05),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
            })
            f.Parent = root
        end
        edge(Vector2.new(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.22), 90)
        edge(Vector2.new(0, 1), UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.22), 270)
        edge(Vector2.new(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0.16, 1), 0)
        edge(Vector2.new(1, 0), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0.16, 1), 180)

        local grain = {}
        for gI = 1, 36 do
            local gp = Instance.new("Frame")
            local sz = math.random(1, 3)
            gp.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz)
            gp.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            gp.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
            gp.BorderSizePixel = 0
            gp.ZIndex = 72
            gp.Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.random(), math.random())
            gp.Parent = root
            grain[gI] = gp
        end

        local flicker = Instance.new("Frame")
        flicker.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        flicker.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        flicker.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        flicker.BorderSizePixel = 0
        flicker.ZIndex = 74
        flicker.Parent = root

        local flash = Instance.new("Frame")
        flash.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        flash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        flash.BorderSizePixel = 0
        flash.ZIndex = 80
        flash.Parent = root

        -- ---------- skip ----------
        local skipBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        skipBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        skipBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, -22)
        skipBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(96, 34)
        skipBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
        skipBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        skipBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        skipBtn.Text = "SKIP  \u{25B6}"
        skipBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        skipBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        skipBtn.TextSize = 13
        skipBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        skipBtn.ZIndex = 100
        skipBtn.Parent = gui
        Instance.new("UICorner", skipBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        local sst = Instance.new("UIStroke", skipBtn)
        sst.Color = RED; sst.Thickness = 1.5; sst.Transparency = 0.2

        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hint.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        hint.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -22)
        hint.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 18)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "tap / click / space to skip"
        hint.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 200)
        hint.TextTransparency = 0.45
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        hint.TextSize = 11
        hint.ZIndex = 100
        hint.Parent = gui

        -- ---------- finish / cleanup ----------
        local conn, inputConn
        local function cleanup()
            running = false
            _celestialIntroActive = false
            if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
            if inputConn then inputConn:Disconnect(); inputConn = nil end
            pcall(function() introSong:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() introSong:Destroy() end)
            pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
        end
        local function finish(fast)
            if finishing then return end
            finishing = true
            local dur = fast and 0.3 or FADE_OUT
            pcall(function()
                if root:IsA("CanvasGroup") then
                    tween(root, dur, {GroupTransparency = 1})
                else
                    tween(root, dur, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
                end
                tween(skipBtn, dur, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextTransparency = 1})
                tween(hint, dur, {TextTransparency = 1})
                tween(introSong, dur, {Volume = 0})
            end)
            task.delay(dur + 0.05, cleanup)
        end
        skipBtn.Activated:Connect(function() finish(true) end)
        inputConn = UIS2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            local t = input.UserInputType
            if t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                finish(true)
            elseif t == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                local kc = input.KeyCode
                if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Space or kc == Enum.KeyCode.Return or kc == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                    finish(true)
                end
            end
        end)

        -- ---------- effects ----------
        local t0 = tick()
        local nowT = 0
        local excite = 1
        local shakeUntil, shakeAmp = 0, 0
        local lookTarget, lookUntil = nil, 0
        local titleShown = false
        local titleStart = 0
        local glitchUntil = 0

        local function shake(amp, dur)
            shakeAmp = amp
            shakeUntil = nowT + dur
        end

        local function shockwave(parent, color)
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
            ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ring.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.56)
            ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.ZIndex = 26
            ring.Parent = parent
            Instance.new("UICorner", ring).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", ring)
            st.Color = color; st.Thickness = 5; st.Transparency = 0.05
            tween(ring, 0.55, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(panelW * 1.5, panelW * 1.5)})
            tween(st, 0.55, {Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1})
            Debris2:AddItem(ring, 0.7)
        end

        local function sparks(parent)
            for sI = 1, 14 do
                local s = Instance.new("Frame")
                s.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                s.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.56)
                s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 12)
                s.BackgroundColor3 = (sI % 2 == 0) and RED_BRIGHT or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                s.BorderSizePixel = 0
                s.ZIndex = 27
                local ang = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local dist = panelW * (0.3 + math.random() * 0.4)
                s.Rotation = math.deg(ang) + 90
                s.Parent = parent
                tween(s, 0.4 + math.random() * 0.25, {
                    Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(ang) * dist, 0.56, math.sin(ang) * dist),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                Debris2:AddItem(s, 0.8)
            end
        end

        local function addCracks(p)
            for cI = 1, 5 do
                local c = Instance.new("Frame")
                c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                c.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.15 + math.random() * 0.7, 0.15 + math.random() * 0.7)
                c.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, panelW * (0.35 + math.random() * 0.45))
                c.Rotation = math.random(-70, 70)
                c.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                c.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                c.BorderSizePixel = 0
                c.ZIndex = 17
                c.Parent = p.cracks
                tween(c, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.45})
            end
        end

        local focusedIdx = 0
        local function focus(idx)
            local p = panels[idx]
            if not p then return end
            focusedIdx = idx
            p.group.ZIndex = 15
            tween(p.darken, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.05})
            tween(p.scale, 0.25, {Scale = 1.05}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end

        local PITCH = {1.0, 0.93, 0.86, 0.78}
        local function mog(idx)
            local p = panels[idx]
            if not p then return end
            playHit(PITCH[idx] or 1, 1.0, false)
            playHit((PITCH[idx] or 1) * 0.5, 1.3, true)

            tween(p.bar, 0.26, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.56)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            p.barScale.Scale = 1.7
            tween(p.barScale, 0.3, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            p.flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            tween(p.flash, 0.5, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            shockwave(p.group, RED_BRIGHT)
            sparks(p.group)
            addCracks(p)
            tween(p.darken, 0.4, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 0, 8)})
            tween(p.scale, 0.22, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            p.group.ZIndex = 10
            shake(math.clamp(bw * 0.018, 4, 12), 0.34)
            excite = 2.6
            for _, e in ipairs(eyes) do
                if e.opened then
                    tween(e.pupil, 0.12, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(e.pupilD * 0.3, e.pupilD * 1.1)})
                    task.delay(0.5, function()
                        if running and e.pupil.Parent then
                            tween(e.pupil, 0.3, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(e.pupilD * 0.55, e.pupilD)})
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            local sx = vp.X / 2 + (p.info.col == 0 and -1 or 1) * bw * 0.25
            local sy = vp.Y / 2 + (p.info.row == 0 and -1 or 1) * bh * 0.25
            lookTarget = Vector2.new(sx, sy)
            lookUntil = nowT + 0.9
        end

        local function showTitle()
            titleShown = true
            titleStart = nowT
            titleHolder.Visible = true
            titleLbl.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0
            ghostR.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0
            ghostC.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0
            titleScale.Scale = 1.5
            playHit(1.05, 0.8, false)
            tween(titleScale, 0.45, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            flash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            tween(flash, 0.5, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            glitchUntil = nowT + 1.0
            excite = 2.4
            for _, e in ipairs(eyes) do
                e.scale = 1.18
                if e.opened then
                    tween(e.frame, 0.25, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(e.w, e.h * e.scale)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                end
            end
        end
        local function hideTitle()
            titleShown = false
            titleHolder.Visible = false
        end

        local function showBoard()
            boardGroup.Visible = true
            tween(sceneDim, 0.5, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.45})
            if boardIsGroup then tween(boardGroup, 0.4, {GroupTransparency = 0}) end
            tween(boardScale, 0.45, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            playHit(0.7, 0.7, true)
            shake(5, 0.25)
        end

        local function finalHit()
            playHit(0.6, 1.2, true)
            playHit(1.1, 1.0, false)
            flash.BackgroundColor3 = RED_BRIGHT
            flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
            tween(flash, 0.7, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            if boardIsGroup then tween(boardGroup, 0.4, {GroupTransparency = 0.55}) end
            tween(sceneDim, 0.4, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.25})
            shake(math.clamp(bw * 0.03, 6, 16), 0.5)
            showTitle()
        end

        local events = {
            { t = TITLE_IN, fn = showTitle },
            { t = TITLE_OUT, fn = hideTitle },
            { t = BOARD_IN, fn = showBoard },
        }
        for idx = 1, 4 do
            local ft = FOCUS_FIRST + (idx - 1) * SLOT
            events[#events + 1] = { t = ft, fn = function() focus(idx) end }
            events[#events + 1] = { t = ft + FOCUS_TO_MOG, fn = function() mog(idx) end }
        end
        events[#events + 1] = { t = FINAL_T, fn = finalHit }

        conn = RunService2.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not running then return end
            local t = tick() - t0
            nowT = t

            if t >= INTRO_TIME - FADE_OUT and not finishing then finish(false) end
            if not songStarted then startSong() end
            for _, ev in ipairs(events) do
                if not ev.done and t >= ev.t then
                    ev.done = true
                    local ok, err = pcall(ev.fn)
                    if not ok then warn("[CelestialIntro] " .. tostring(err)) end
                end
            end
            excite = excite + (1 - excite) * math.min(1, dt * 1.6)

            -- camera: slow push-in + handheld drift + hit shake
            sceneScale.Scale = 1 + 0.035 * math.min(1, t / INTRO_TIME)
            local ox = math.sin(t * 1.3) * 1.5 + math.sin(t * 7.1) * 0.6
            local oy = math.cos(t * 1.1) * 1.5 + math.cos(t * 6.3) * 0.6
            if t < shakeUntil then
                local f = (shakeUntil - t) / 0.35
                f = math.clamp(f, 0, 1)
                ox = ox + (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * shakeAmp * f
                oy = oy + (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * shakeAmp * f
            end
            scene.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, ox, 0.5, oy)
            boardGroup.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -ox * 1.5, 0.5, -oy * 1.5)

            -- atmosphere
            glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.9 + math.sin(t * 2.2) * 0.04
            glow2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.87 + math.cos(t * 3.1) * 0.04
            if math.random() < 0.03 then
                flicker.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8 + math.random() * 0.15
            else
                flicker.BackgroundTransparency = math.min(1, flicker.BackgroundTransparency + dt * 4)
            end
            for gI = 1, #grain do
                if (gI + math.floor(t * 30)) % 2 == 0 then
                    grain[gI].Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.random(), math.random())
                    grain[gI].BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.25
                end
            end

            local mouse = UIS2:GetMouseLocation()
            local looking = lookTarget ~= nil and t < lookUntil

            for _, e in ipairs(eyes) do
                if not e.opened and t >= e.openAt then
                    e.opened = true
                    tween(e.frame, 0.35, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(e.w, e.h * e.scale)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                    e.nextBlink = t + 1.5 + math.random() * 2.5
                end
                if e.opened then
                    if t >= e.nextBlink then
                        e.nextBlink = t + 1.2 + math.random() * 3
                        tween(e.frame, 0.07, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(e.w, e.h * 0.08)})
                        task.delay(0.1, function()
                            if running and e.frame.Parent then
                                tween(e.frame, 0.1, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(e.w, e.h * e.scale)})
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    local cx = vp.X * e.px
                    local cy = vp.Y * e.py
                    local dx, dy
                    if looking then
                        local vx, vy = lookTarget.X - cx, lookTarget.Y - cy
                        local m = math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy)
                        if m < 1 then m = 1 end
                        dx, dy = vx / m, vy / m
                    elseif touchOnly then
                        dx = math.cos(t * 0.8 + e.phase)
                        dy = math.sin(t * 0.6 + e.phase * 1.3)
                    else
                        local vx, vy = mouse.X - cx, mouse.Y - cy
                        local m = math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy)
                        if m < 1 then m = 1 end
                        local pull = math.clamp(m / 250, 0, 1)
                        dx, dy = vx / m * pull, vy / m * pull
                    end
                    e.iris.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, dx * e.w * 0.2, 0.5, dy * e.h * 0.1)
                end
            end

            -- hands
            for _, h in ipairs(hands) do
                if not h.started and t >= h.delay then
                    h.started = true
                    tween(h.frame, 2.0, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(h.ex, h.ey)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                end
                if h.started then
                    h.frame.Rotation = h.rot + math.sin(t * 1.1 + h.phase) * 5 * excite
                end
                for _, f in ipairs(h.fingers) do
                    local px, py = f.bx, f.by
                    local ang = math.rad(f.a) + math.sin(t * 0.9 + f.phase) * 0.08 * excite + math.sin(t * 17 + f.tremor) * 0.012
                    local c = math.sin(t * f.speed * excite + f.phase) * 0.5 + 0.5
                    c = c * c
                    local curl = f.curlMax * c * f.dir
                    for si, seg in ipairs(f.segs) do
                        ang = ang + curl * (0.5 + 0.28 * (si - 1))
                        local len = f.lens[si]
                        local ddx, ddy = math.sin(ang), -math.cos(ang)
                        seg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, px + ddx * len * 0.5, 0.5, py + ddy * len * 0.5)
                        seg.Rotation = math.deg(ang)
                        px, py = px + ddx * len, py + ddy * len
                    end
                end
            end

            -- title typewriter + glitch
            if titleShown then
                local n = math.floor((t - titleStart) / 0.05) + 1
                if n > #TITLE_TEXT then n = #TITLE_TEXT end
                titleLbl.MaxVisibleGraphemes = n
                ghostR.MaxVisibleGraphemes = n
                ghostC.MaxVisibleGraphemes = n
                if t > glitchUntil and math.random() < 0.025 then glitchUntil = t + 0.1 end
                local gl = (t < glitchUntil)
                local amp = gl and 12 or 2
                ghostR.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, (math.random() - 0.5) * amp * 2 + 2, 0.5, (math.random() - 0.5) * amp)
                ghostC.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, (math.random() - 0.5) * amp * 2 - 2, 0.5, (math.random() - 0.5) * amp)
                ghostR.TextTransparency = gl and 0.2 or 0.7
                ghostC.TextTransparency = gl and 0.2 or 0.7
                titleHolder.Rotation = gl and (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 or 0
            end
        end)

        -- hard stop no matter what
        task.delay(INTRO_TIME + 0.2, function()
            if running then cleanup() end
        end)
    end

    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if ragdollCheckConn then 
            ragdollCheckConn:Disconnect()
            ragdollCheckConn = nil
        end
        timerActive = false
        lastRagdollState = false
        if timerConn then timerConn:Disconnect(); timerConn = nil end
        if timerBillboard then timerBillboard:Destroy(); timerBillboard = nil end
        
        task.wait(0.5)
        createTimerBillboard()
        checkRagdoll()
        -- intro intentionally NOT played on reset/respawn
    end)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        createTimerBillboard()
    end)
end

local function createRagdollBillboard(duration,labelText,color)
    if not ragdollGuiEnabled then return nil end
    local PINK  = color or Color3.fromRGB(160,80,255)
    local BG    = Color3.fromRGB(6,4,14)
    local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    local W,H   = 210,80
    local guiName="CELESTIALRagdollTimer_"..labelText
    pcall(function()
        local cg=game:GetService("CoreGui");local old=cg:FindFirstChild(guiName);if old then old:Destroy() end
        local pg=LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui");if pg then local o=pg:FindFirstChild(guiName);if o then o:Destroy() end end
    end)
    local sg=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name=guiName;sg.ResetOnSpawn=false;sg.IgnoreGuiInset=true;sg.DisplayOrder=25
    parentGui(sg)
    -- Outer card - dark, thick pink border, matches photo
    local card=Instance.new("Frame",sg)
    card.Size=UDim2.new(0,W,0,H);card.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-W/2,0,58)
    card.BackgroundColor3=BG;card.BackgroundTransparency=0.05
    card.BorderSizePixel=0;card.ZIndex=30;card.Active=true
    Instance.new("UICorner",card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,14)
    -- Static border (no glow pulse)
    local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",card)
    stroke.Color=PINK;stroke.Thickness=1;stroke.Transparency=0.6
    -- Title (white bold, centered, photo style - no colored bar)
    local titleLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",card)
    titleLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,28);titleLbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,6)
    titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1
    titleLbl.Text=(labelText=="RAGDOLL" and "RAGDOLL TIMER" or (labelText=="STONE" and "STONE TIMER" or labelText.." TIMER"))
    titleLbl.TextColor3=WHITE;titleLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack;titleLbl.TextSize=13
    titleLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center;titleLbl.ZIndex=32
    -- Thin pink divider under title
    local divider=Instance.new("Frame",card)
    divider.Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,1);divider.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,34)
    divider.BackgroundColor3=PINK;divider.BackgroundTransparency=0.5;divider.BorderSizePixel=0;divider.ZIndex=31
    -- Countdown label (big, pink, shimmer)
    local timerLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",card)
    timerLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,H-38);timerLbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,36)
    timerLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1;timerLbl.Text=string.format("%.1f",duration).."s"
    timerLbl.TextColor3=PINK;timerLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack;timerLbl.TextSize=24
    timerLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center;timerLbl.ZIndex=32
    local shimmer=addShimmerToLabel(timerLbl,PINK,WHITE)
    task.spawn(function() local t=0;while timerLbl and timerLbl.Parent do t=t+0.04;shimmer.Offset=Vector2.new(math.sin(t)*0.5,0);task.wait(0.04) end end)
    -- Drag support
    local dragStart,dragStartPos,dragging=nil,nil,false
    card.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging=true;dragStart=inp.Position;dragStartPos=card.Position
            inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if inp.UserInputState==Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging=false
                        -- persist steal/info bar position
                        pcall(function()
                            local pos = frame.Position
                            stealBarPos = {sx=pos.X.Scale, ox=pos.X.Offset, sy=pos.Y.Scale, oy=pos.Y.Offset}
                            if type(saveConfig)=="function" then saveConfig() end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if dragging and (inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d=inp.Position-dragStart
            card.Position=UDim2.new(dragStartPos.X.Scale,dragStartPos.X.Offset+d.X,dragStartPos.Y.Scale,dragStartPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
        end
    end)
    -- Auto-countdown and destroy
    local startTime=tick();local conn
    conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local remaining=math.max(0,duration-(tick()-startTime))
        if remaining<=0 then conn:Disconnect();pcall(function() sg:Destroy() end)
        elseif timerLbl and timerLbl.Parent then timerLbl.Text=string.format("%.1f",remaining).."s" end
    end)
    return sg
end
local function onHumanoidStateChanged(old,new)
    local char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
    local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");if not hum then return end
    local isRag=(new==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or new==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or new==Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown)
    if isRag and not hum.PlatformStand and not activeBatBillboard then
        activeBatBillboard=createRagdollBillboard(2.5,"RAGDOLL",Color3.fromRGB(160,80,255))
        task.delay(2.6,function() if activeBatBillboard then pcall(function() activeBatBillboard:Destroy() end);activeBatBillboard=nil end end)
    end
end
local function onMedusaStateChanged()
    local char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
    local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.PlatformStand and not activeMedusaBillboard then
        activeMedusaBillboard=createRagdollBillboard(4.5,"STONE",Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255))
        task.delay(4.5,function() if activeMedusaBillboard then pcall(function() activeMedusaBillboard:Destroy() end);activeMedusaBillboard=nil end end)
    end
end
local function setupRagdollTriggers()
    local char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
    local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum.StateChanged:Connect(onHumanoidStateChanged);hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlatformStand"):Connect(onMedusaStateChanged) end
end
local function getSpeedThemeColors()
    local th=getTheme()
    local accent=th and th.ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    local accent2=th and th.ACCENT2 or Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
    local gray=th and th.GRAY or Color3.fromRGB(200,200,210)
    return accent,accent2,gray
end
local function applySpeedIndicatorTheme()
    local char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
    local head=char:FindFirstChild("Head");if not head then return end
    local bb=head:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALSpeedBB");if not bb then return end
    local accent,accent2,gray=getSpeedThemeColors()
    for _,lbl in ipairs(bb:GetChildren()) do
        if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") then
            if lbl.Name=="DiscordLbl" then
                lbl.TextColor3=gray
                local gr=lbl:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                if gr then gr.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,gray),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,accent),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,gray)}) end
            else
                lbl.TextColor3=accent
                local gr=lbl:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                if gr then gr.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,accent2),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,accent),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,accent2)}) end
            end
        end
    end
end

    local head=char:WaitForChild("Head",5);if not head then return end
    pcall(function()

    end)
