    discordLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1;discordLabel.Text="CELESTIAL BUYER"
    discordLabel.TextColor3=gray;discordLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
    discordLabel.TextScaled=true;discordLabel.TextStrokeTransparency=0.4
    speedLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel",bb)
    speedLabel.Name="SpeedLbl";speedLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0.55,0)
    speedLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0.38,0);speedLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1
    speedLabel.Text="0.0";speedLabel.TextColor3=accent
    speedLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold;speedLabel.TextScaled=true
    speedLabel.TextStrokeTransparency=0.35
    local gr1=addShimmerToLabel(speedLabel,accent2,accent)
    local gr2=addShimmerToLabel(discordLabel,gray,accent)
    task.spawn(function()
        local t=0
        while bb and bb.Parent do
            t=t+0.03
            pcall(function()
                gr1.Offset=Vector2.new(math.sin(t)*0.4,0)
                gr2.Offset=Vector2.new(math.sin(t)*0.4,0)
            end)
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)
end
local function getActiveMoveSpeed()
    if autoSwitchSpeedEnabled and _autoCarryLatched then return CS end
    if laggerCarryActive then return LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED
    elseif laggerModeEnabled then return LAGGER_SPEED
    elseif carrySpeedActive then return CS
    elseif customSpeedActive and type(customSpeeds)=="table" and customSpeeds[selectedCustomIndex] then
        local v = tonumber(customSpeeds[selectedCustomIndex])
        if v and v > 0 then return v end
    end
    return NS
end
local function getAutoPathSpeed()
    if laggerModeEnabled then return LAGGER_SPEED
    else return NS end
end



-- ============================================================
-- VELOCITY SPOOF (from Synapse X Celestial Hub)
-- When the game's scripts read AssemblyLinearVelocity / Velocity
-- on your character root, they get a capped value (max horiz 20)
-- so high carry/normal speeds don't drop brainrot.
-- Real velocity is unchanged — only the *reported* value is faked.
-- Requires executor APIs: getrawmetatable, setreadonly, newcclosure, checkcaller
-- ============================================================
CelestialVelocitySpoof = CelestialVelocitySpoof or {}
do
    local S = {
        spoofed = Vector3.zero,
        hooked = false,
        enabled = true,
    }

    local function _V3(x, y, z)
        return Vector3.new(x, y, z)
    end

    -- Update the value game scripts will see (cap horizontal magnitude at 20)
    function CelestialVelocitySpoof.update(realVel)
        if not S.enabled then return end
        if typeof(realVel) ~= "Vector3" then
            S.spoofed = Vector3.zero
            return
        end
        local y = realVel.Y
        local hx, hz = realVel.X, realVel.Z
        local mag = math.sqrt(hx * hx + hz * hz)
        if mag < 0.05 then
            S.spoofed = _V3(0, y, 0)
            return
        end
        local vis = math.min(mag, 20)
        local scale = vis / mag
        S.spoofed = _V3(hx * scale, y, hz * scale)
    end

    function CelestialVelocitySpoof.clear(y)
        S.spoofed = _V3(0, tonumber(y) or 0, 0)
    end

    function CelestialVelocitySpoof.get()
        return S.spoofed
    end

    function CelestialVelocitySpoof.setEnabled(on)
        S.enabled = on == true
        if not S.enabled then
            S.spoofed = Vector3.zero
        end
    end

    -- Hook game metatable so non-executor (game) reads see spoofed velocity
    pcall(function()
        if not (getrawmetatable and setreadonly and newcclosure and checkcaller) then return end
        if S.hooked then return end
        local mt = getrawmetatable(game)
        if not mt then return end
        setreadonly(mt, false)
        local oldIndex = mt.__index
        local oldNewIndex = mt.__newindex

        local function isOurRoot(t, k)
            local tk = tostring(k)
            if tk ~= "AssemblyLinearVelocity" and tk ~= "Velocity" then return false end
            if typeof(t) ~= "Instance" or not t:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
            local tn = t.Name
            if tn ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and tn ~= "Torso" and tn ~= "UpperTorso" then return false end
            local char = LP and LP.Character
            return char ~= nil and t:IsDescendantOf(char)
        end

        if type(oldIndex) == "function" or type(oldIndex) == "table" then
            mt.__index = newcclosure(function(self, key)
                if S.enabled and not checkcaller() then
                    local good, mine = pcall(isOurRoot, self, key)
                    if good and mine then return S.spoofed end
                end
                if type(oldIndex) == "function" then return oldIndex(self, key) end
                return oldIndex[key]
            end)
        end

        if type(oldNewIndex) == "function" then
            mt.__newindex = newcclosure(function(self, key, value)
                if S.enabled and not checkcaller() then
                    local good, mine = pcall(isOurRoot, self, key)
                    if good and mine then
                        -- game tried to write velocity — keep spoof in sync with what it wants
                        if typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
                            CelestialVelocitySpoof.update(value)
                        end
                        return
                    end
                end
                return oldNewIndex(self, key, value)
            end)
        end

        setreadonly(mt, true)
        S.hooked = true
    end)

    -- Keep spoof in sync with actual HRP velocity each frame (covers all movement paths)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.03)
            if not S.enabled or not S.hooked then continue end
            pcall(function()
                local char = LP and LP.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    CelestialVelocitySpoof.update(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end


-- ============================================================
-- Movement: mass ApplyImpulse + CFrame displacement to HIT the set speed
-- (impulse alone under-delivers; CFrame makes studs/sec match the number)
-- ============================================================
local _moveDt = 1/60
local _speedActive = false
local _lastHorizSpeed = 0
local _actualSpeed = 0
local _lastPos = nil
local _lastPosT = 0

local function destroySpeedConstraint()
    _speedActive = false
end

local function clearSpeedConstraint()
    _speedActive = false
    _lastHorizSpeed = 0
end

-- Impulse to reach a target world velocity: impulse = (target - current) * mass
local function applyImpulseVel(part, targetVel)
    if not part or not part.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        local mass = part.AssemblyMass
        if not mass or mass ~= mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end
        local current = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local delta = targetVel - current
        part:ApplyImpulse(delta * mass)
    end)
end

local function applyVel(part, targetVel)
    applyImpulseVel(part, targetVel)
end

local function applyAngVel(part, targetAng)
    if not part or not part.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        local mass = part.AssemblyMass
        if not mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end
        local current = part.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        local target = targetAng or Vector3.zero
        part:ApplyAngularImpulse((target - current) * mass * 0.35)
    end)
end

-- Drive horizontal speed with CFrame only (exact studs/sec = set number)
-- Impulse is NOT added here - stacking it made the counter read ~6 high.
-- Speed drive + active brake (kills residual XZ so you don't skate on uneven ground)
local _lastGroundNormalY = 1
local function sampleGround(hrp)
    -- pcall only returns ONE value from the fn; pack results in a table
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local origin = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local char = hrp.Parent
        if char then params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char} end
        params.IgnoreWater = true
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), params)
        if hit and hit.Normal then
            return {ny = hit.Normal.Y, grounded = (hit.Distance < 4.2)}
        end
        return {ny = 1, grounded = false}
    end)
    if ok and type(res) == "table" and type(res.ny) == "number" then
        _lastGroundNormalY = res.ny
        return res.ny, res.grounded == true
    end
    return _lastGroundNormalY, false
end

local function setSpeedConstraint(hrp, horizVel)
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
    local hx, hz = horizVel.X, horizVel.Z
    local mag = math.sqrt(hx * hx + hz * hz)
    _lastHorizSpeed = mag
    _speedActive = mag > 0.05
    if mag < 0.05 then
        if CelestialVelocitySpoof then
            local y = 0
            pcall(function() y = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y end)
            CelestialVelocitySpoof.clear(y)
        end
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        local ny, grounded = sampleGround(hrp)
        local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local mass = hrp.AssemblyMass
        if not mass or mass ~= mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end

        local gain = 0.85
        if grounded then
            if ny < 0.55 then
                gain = 0.45
            elseif ny < 0.78 then
                gain = 0.62
            else
                gain = 0.9
            end
        else
            gain = 0.7
        end

        local target = Vector3.new(hx, v.Y, hz)
        hrp:ApplyImpulse((target - v) * mass * gain)
        -- Report capped velocity to game scripts (holds brainrot at high speed)
        if CelestialVelocitySpoof then
            CelestialVelocitySpoof.update(target)
        end
    end)
end

-- Hard-stop residual horizontal velocity when grounded & not intending to move
local function brakeHorizontal(hrp)
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        local ny, grounded = sampleGround(hrp)
        if not grounded then return end
        local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local hx, hz = v.X, v.Z
        local hMag = math.sqrt(hx * hx + hz * hz)
        if hMag < 0.8 then
            if hMag > 0.05 then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0)
            end
            return
        end
        local mass = hrp.AssemblyMass
        if not mass or mass ~= mass or mass <= 0 then mass = 1 end
        local target = Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0)
        hrp:ApplyImpulse((target - v) * mass * 0.92)
    end)
end

local function applyCFrameMove(part, dirUnit, spd, dt)
    if not part or not part.Parent then return end
    if not dirUnit or dirUnit.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
    _moveDt = dt or _moveDt
    setSpeedConstraint(part, Vector3.new(dirUnit.X, 0, dirUnit.Z).Unit * spd)
end

local function manualJumpBoost(boost)
    if not infJumpEnabled or infJumpMode~="manual" then return end
    local char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
    local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");if root then applyVel(root, Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,boost,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)) end
end
local function startHoldInfJump()
    if holdInfJumpConn then holdInfJumpConn:Disconnect() end
    holdInfJumpConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not infJumpEnabled or infJumpMode~="hold" then return end
        local char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
        local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");if not root or not hum then return end
        local isJumpHeld=UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or (hum.Jump==true)
        local cv=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if isJumpHeld and cv.Y<35 then applyVel(root, Vector3.new(cv.X,55,cv.Z)) end
        cv=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if cv.Y<-120 then applyVel(root, Vector3.new(cv.X,-120,cv.Z)) end
    end)
end
local function stopHoldInfJump() if holdInfJumpConn then holdInfJumpConn:Disconnect();holdInfJumpConn=nil end end
UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function() manualJumpBoost(50) end)
UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Space and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
        task.delay(0.12,function() if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then manualJumpBoost(50) end end)
    end
end)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then manualJumpBoost(50) end end)
-- blacklist/kick removed; insta-reset restored (S2 logic)

_antiKickConns={}
antiKickE01Enabled=false
setAntiKickE01Visual=nil

-- S2-style carry / brainrot detection (shared by Anti Kick + E01 warning)
local function celestialIsCarryingBrainrot()
    local ok, val = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
    if ok and val == true then return true end
    local ok2, val2 = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("AntiKick") end)
    if ok2 and val2 == true then return true end
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local ok3, val3 = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
    if ok3 and val3 == true then return true end
    for _, name in ipairs({"Carrying","IsCarrying","Grabbed","Holding","StealHold","HasGrab"}) do
        local v = char:FindFirstChild(name, true)
        if v then
            if v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value then return true end
            if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then return true end
            if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Value ~= "" then return true end
        end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            local n = child.Name:lower()
            if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("skibidi") or n:find("toilet") or n:find("brain") then
                return true
            end
        elseif child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then
            local n = child.Name:lower()
            if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("animal") or n:find("carry") or n:find("stolen") or n:find("held") then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    local okAttrs, attrs = pcall(function() return char:GetAttributes() end)
    if okAttrs and type(attrs) == "table" then
        for attrName, attrValue in pairs(attrs) do
            local name = tostring(attrName):lower()
            if (name:find("carrying") or name:find("carry") or name:find("stealing") or name:find("isstealing") or name:find("hasbrainrot")) and attrValue == true then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function antiKickForceStopCombat()
    if autoBatEnabled then
        autoBatEnabled = false
        if stopBatAimbot then pcall(stopBatAimbot) end
        if resetAutoBatMotion then pcall(resetAutoBatMotion) end
        if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false) end
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if stopAutoLeft then pcall(stopAutoLeft) end
        if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if stopAutoRight then pcall(stopAutoRight) end
        if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.autoRight then mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end
    end
    if tpBatEnabled then
        tpBatEnabled = false
        if stopTPBat then pcall(stopTPBat) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false) end
    end
end

enableAntiKick=function()
    antiKickEnabled=true
    task.spawn(function()
        while antiKickEnabled do
            task.wait(0.25)
            local found = celestialIsCarryingBrainrot()
            brainrotDetected = found
            if found then
                antiKickForceStopCombat()
            end
        end
    end)
end
disableAntiKick=function()
    antiKickEnabled=false;brainrotDetected=false
    for _,c in ipairs(_antiKickConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end;_antiKickConns={}
end

-- ============================================================
-- MUSIC PLAYER (Settings > Music)
-- Toggle on, pick a track with Prev / Next. Local files from CelestialMusic folder also work.
-- ============================================================
_celestialIntroActive = false
CelestialMusic = {
    enabled = false, index = 1, volume = 0.5,
    _token = 0, _sound = nil, onChange = nil,
    TRACKS = {
        { name = "panzerkhnacker",     url = "https://files.catbox.moe/8ch5qz.mp3" },
        { name = "national treasure",  url = "https://files.catbox.moe/8oogdu.mp3" },
        { name = "blue bands",         url = "https://files.catbox.moe/gm26vl.mp3" },
        { name = "Noord Africano",     url = "https://files.catbox.moe/m5kxm3.mp3" },
    },
}
;(function()
    local M = CelestialMusic

    local function hashStr(s)
        local h = 5381
        for i = 1, #s do h = (h * 33 + s:byte(i)) % 2147483647 end
        return tostring(h)
    end

    local function tryGetCustomAsset(path)
        local asset = nil
        pcall(function() if celestialGetCustomAsset then asset = celestialGetCustomAsset(path) end end)
        if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
        pcall(function() if getcustomasset then asset = getcustomasset(path) end end)
        if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
        pcall(function() if syn and syn.getcustomasset then asset = syn.getcustomasset(path) end end)
        if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
        return nil
    end

    -- Drop your own audio files (mp3 / ogg / wav) into  <executor workspace>/CelestialMusic
    -- They show up in the Track list automatically.
    M.FOLDER = "CelestialMusic"
    M._failed = {}
    function M.scanFolder()
        for i = #M.TRACKS, 1, -1 do
            if M.TRACKS[i].file then table.remove(M.TRACKS, i) end
        end
        local ok, files = pcall(function()
            if isfolder and makefolder and not isfolder(M.FOLDER) then makefolder(M.FOLDER) end
            if listfiles then return listfiles(M.FOLDER) end
            return {}
        end)
        if not ok or type(files) ~= "table" then return 0 end
        local added = 0
        for _, path in ipairs(files) do
            local fname = tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)$") or tostring(path)
            local ext = (fname:match("%.(%w+)$") or ""):lower()
            if ext == "mp3" or ext == "ogg" or ext == "wav" then
                local base = fname:gsub("%.%w+$", "")
                table.insert(M.TRACKS, { name = base, file = path })
                added = added + 1
            end
        end
        if M.onChange then pcall(M.onChange) end
        return added
    end
    pcall(function() M.scanFolder() end)

    function M.sourceOf(track)
        if not track then return nil end
        local s
        if track.file then s = track.file
        else s = track.url end
        s = tostring(s or "")
        s = s:gsub("^%s+", "")
        s = s:gsub("%s+$", "")
        if s == "" then return nil end
        return s
    end

    function M.displayName(i)
        local tr = M.TRACKS[i or M.index]
        if not tr then return "-" end
        local src = M.sourceOf(tr)
        if src and M._failed[src] then return tr.name .. " (file not found)" end
        return tr.name
    end

    -- turns a link / id into something a Sound can play (downloads + caches mp3 links)
    function M._resolve(src)
        -- a file in your executor workspace: tries the name as typed, then inside the CelestialMusic folder
        if not src:lower():match("^https?://") and not src:match("^%d+$") and not src:lower():match("^rbxassetid://") then
            local candidates = { src, M.FOLDER .. "/" .. src, M.FOLDER .. "\\" .. src }
            for _, path in ipairs(candidates) do
                local okF, isF = pcall(function() return isfile and isfile(path) end)
                if okF and isF then
                    local a = tryGetCustomAsset(path)
                    if a then M._failed[src] = nil; return a end
                end
            end
            M._failed[src] = true
            return nil
        end
        if src:match("^%d+$") then return "rbxassetid://" .. src end
        if src:lower():match("^rbxassetid://%d+") then return src end
        if src:lower():match("^https?://") then
            local cacheName = "CelestialMusic_" .. hashStr(src) .. ".mp3"
            local cached = tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
            if cached then return cached end
            local body = nil
            pcall(function() if celestialHttpGet then body = celestialHttpGet(src) end end)
            if not body or #body < 1000 then pcall(function() body = game:HttpGet(src) end) end
            if body and #body > 1000 then
                pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(cacheName, body) end end)
                task.wait(0.05)
                return tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    function M._ensureSound()
        if M._sound and M._sound.Parent then return M._sound end
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.Name = "CelestialMusic"
        s.Looped = false
        s.Volume = M.volume
        s.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        s.Ended:Connect(function()
            if M.enabled then M.step(1, true) end
        end)
        M._sound = s
        return s
    end

    function M.stop()
        M._token = M._token + 1
        if M._sound then pcall(function() M._sound:Stop() end) end
    end

    function M.play()
        M._token = M._token + 1
        local my = M._token
        if M._sound then pcall(function() M._sound:Stop() end) end
        local src = M.sourceOf(M.TRACKS[M.index])
        if not src then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            while _celestialIntroActive do
                task.wait(0.25)
                if my ~= M._token then return end
            end
            local asset = M._resolve(src)
            if M.onChange then pcall(M.onChange) end
            if my ~= M._token or not M.enabled or not asset then return end
            local s = M._ensureSound()
            s.SoundId = asset
            s.Volume = M.volume
            s.TimePosition = 0
            s:Play()
        end)
    end

    -- move to the next / previous track (auto-advance skips tracks that have no link yet)
    function M.step(dir, auto)
        local n = #M.TRACKS
        local i = M.index
        for _ = 1, n do
            i = ((i - 1 + dir) % n) + 1
            if not auto or M.sourceOf(M.TRACKS[i]) then break end
        end
        M.index = i
        if M.onChange then pcall(M.onChange) end
        if M.enabled then M.play() end
    end

    function M.setIndex(i)
        M.index = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(i) or 1), 1, #M.TRACKS)
        if M.onChange then pcall(M.onChange) end
        if M.enabled then M.play() end
    end

    function M.setEnabled(on)
        M.enabled = on == true
        if M.enabled then M.play() else M.stop() end
    end

    function M.setVolume(v)
        M.volume = math.clamp((tonumber(v) or 50) / 100, 0, 1)
        if M._sound then M._sound.Volume = M.volume end
    end

    function M.setLink(slot, link)
        -- link slots removed (Moonlight / Custom)
    end
end)()

-- ============================================================
-- CLEAN HUB PROTECTION (ported): Anti Ragdoll V1/V2, Anti Die, Anti Fling Shield
-- ============================================================
CleanProtect = { ragMode = "off", dieOn = false, flingOn = false, onChange = nil }
;(function()
    -- ---------- Anti Ragdoll V1 ----------
    local BOOST_SPEED, AR_DEFAULT_SPEED = 400, 16
    local stateV1 = { active=false, isBoosting=false, cachedChar=nil, conns={} }
    local function disconnectV1()
        for _, c in ipairs(stateV1.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        stateV1.conns = {}
    end
    local function cacheV1()
        local char = LP.Character; if not char then return false end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not root then return false end
        stateV1.cachedChar = { character=char, humanoid=hum, root=root }
        return true
    end
    local function isRagV1()
        local cc = stateV1.cachedChar
        if not cc or not cc.humanoid then return false end
        local st = cc.humanoid:GetState()
        return st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    end
    local function exitRagV1()
        local cc = stateV1.cachedChar
        if not cc or not cc.humanoid or not cc.root then return end
        local hum, root = cc.humanoid, cc.root
        pcall(function() LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) end)
        for _, d in ipairs(cc.character:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or (d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then d:Destroy() end
        end
        if not stateV1.isBoosting then stateV1.isBoosting = true; hum.WalkSpeed = BOOST_SPEED end
        if hum.Health > 0 then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end
        root.Anchored = false
    end
    local function loopV1()
        while stateV1.active do
            task.wait()
            if isRagV1() then
                exitRagV1()
            elseif stateV1.isBoosting then
                stateV1.isBoosting = false
                if stateV1.cachedChar and stateV1.cachedChar.humanoid then stateV1.cachedChar.humanoid.WalkSpeed = AR_DEFAULT_SPEED end
            end
        end
    end
    local function stopV1()
        stateV1.active = false
        if stateV1.isBoosting and stateV1.cachedChar and stateV1.cachedChar.humanoid then
            stateV1.cachedChar.humanoid.WalkSpeed = AR_DEFAULT_SPEED
        end
        stateV1.isBoosting = false
        disconnectV1()
        stateV1.cachedChar = nil
    end
    local function startV1()
        if stateV1.active then return end
        stopV1()
        if not cacheV1() then return end
        stateV1.active = true; stateV1.isBoosting = false
        table.insert(stateV1.conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam and stateV1.cachedChar and stateV1.cachedChar.humanoid then cam.CameraSubject = stateV1.cachedChar.humanoid end
        end))
        table.insert(stateV1.conns, LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            stateV1.isBoosting = false
            task.wait(0.5)
            cacheV1()
        end))
        task.spawn(loopV1)
    end

    -- ---------- Anti Ragdoll V2 ----------
    local v2conn, v2cool = nil, 0
    local function stopV2()
        if v2conn then v2conn:Disconnect(); v2conn = nil end
        v2cool = 0
    end
    local function startV2()
        if v2conn then return end
        v2conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local char = LP.Character; if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not root then return end
            if hum.Health <= 0 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then return end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                local now = tick()
                if now - v2cool > 0.15 then
                    v2cool = now
                    pcall(function()
                        if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp then return end
                        if hum.Health <= 0 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then return end
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                        root.Velocity = Vector3.zero; root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then obj.Enabled = true end
                            if obj:IsA("Constraint") then obj.Enabled = true end
                        end
                        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                        local PM = LP.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
                        if PM then
                            local CM = require(PM:FindFirstChild("ControlModule"))
                            if CM then CM:Enable() end
                        end
                        hum.AutoRotate = true; hum.PlatformStand = false; hum.Sit = false
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function CleanProtect.setRagMode(mode)
        if mode ~= "v1" and mode ~= "v2" then mode = "off" end
        if stateV1.active then stopV1() end
        if v2conn then stopV2() end
        CleanProtect.ragMode = mode
        if mode == "v1" then startV1() elseif mode == "v2" then startV2() end
        if CleanProtect.onChange then pcall(CleanProtect.onChange) end
    end

    -- ---------- Anti Die ----------
    local die = { enabled=false, healthConn=nil, diedConn=nil, charConn=nil, humanoid=nil, reviving=false }
    local function dieDisconnectHum()
        if die.healthConn then die.healthConn:Disconnect(); die.healthConn = nil end
        if die.diedConn then die.diedConn:Disconnect(); die.diedConn = nil end
        die.humanoid = nil
    end
    local function dieActivate(char)
        if not die.enabled then return end
        char = char or LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
        if not hum or not die.enabled then return end
        dieDisconnectHum()
        die.humanoid = hum
        pcall(function()
            hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dying, false)
        end)
        die.healthConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
            if not die.enabled or not hum.Parent then return end
            if hum.Health <= 0 and not die.reviving then
                die.reviving = true
                pcall(function()
                    hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                    hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                    if hum.Health > 0 then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end
                end)
                task.delay(0.2, function() die.reviving = false end)
            end
        end)
        die.diedConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()
            if not die.enabled or not char.Parent then return end
            if die.reviving then return end
            die.reviving = true
            task.defer(function()
                if not die.enabled or not char.Parent or not hum.Parent then die.reviving = false; return end
                pcall(function()
                    hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                end)
                die.reviving = false
            end)
        end)
    end
    function CleanProtect.setAntiDie(on)
        on = on == true
        CleanProtect.dieOn = on
        if on then
            die.enabled = true
            if die.charConn then die.charConn:Disconnect() end
            die.charConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                if die.enabled then task.defer(function() dieActivate(char) end) end
            end)
            task.defer(function() dieActivate(LP.Character) end)
        else
            die.enabled = false
            if die.charConn then die.charConn:Disconnect(); die.charConn = nil end
            local hum = die.humanoid
            dieDisconnectHum()
            if hum and hum.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
                    hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- ---------- Anti Fling Shield ----------
    local fling = { enabled=false, loop=nil, threshold=80 }
    function CleanProtect.setAntiFling(on)
        on = on == true
        CleanProtect.flingOn = on
        fling.enabled = on
        if fling.loop then fling.loop:Disconnect(); fling.loop = nil end
        if not on then return end
        fling.loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not fling.enabled then return end
            local char = LP.Character
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root or not root.Parent then return end
            if tpBatEnabled then return end   -- don't fight the TP Bat desync
            local ok, v = pcall(function() return root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
            if not ok or typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" then return end
            if v.Magnitude <= fling.threshold then return end
            local stab = Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0)
            pcall(function() root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = stab end)
            pcall(function() root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
            pcall(function() root.Velocity = stab end)
            pcall(function() root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
        end)
    end
end)()

-- ============================================================
-- ANTI-KICK E01 WARNING (S2-style countdown banner)
-- Shows 3s WAIT when you pick up brainrot, then SAFE
-- ============================================================
local e01Gui,e01Card,e01Ring,e01RingTxt,e01Title,e01Status,e01Stroke=nil,nil,nil,nil,nil,nil,nil
local e01WasCarrying=false
local e01Conn=nil

local function e01BuildGui()
    if e01Gui and e01Gui.Parent then return end
    e01Gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    e01Gui.Name="CELESTIALE01Warning"
    e01Gui.ResetOnSpawn=false
    e01Gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
    e01Gui.DisplayOrder=50
    parentGui(e01Gui)
    e01Card=Instance.new("Frame",e01Gui)
    e01Card.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0)
    e01Card.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,12)
    e01Card.Size=UDim2.new(0,250,0,58)
    e01Card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,12,16)
    e01Card.BorderSizePixel=0
    e01Card.Visible=false
    e01Card.ZIndex=50
    Instance.new("UICorner",e01Card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,12)
    e01Stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",e01Card)
    e01Stroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,94)
    e01Stroke.Thickness=1.5
    e01Stroke.Transparency=0.15
    e01Ring=Instance.new("Frame",e01Card)
    e01Ring.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0.5,-19)
    e01Ring.Size=UDim2.new(0,38,0,38)
    e01Ring.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,94)
    e01Ring.BorderSizePixel=0
    e01Ring.ZIndex=51
    Instance.new("UICorner",e01Ring).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    e01RingTxt=Instance.new("TextLabel",e01Ring)
    e01RingTxt.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    e01RingTxt.BackgroundTransparency=1
    e01RingTxt.Text="3.0"
    e01RingTxt.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,12,16)
    e01RingTxt.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    e01RingTxt.TextSize=13
    e01RingTxt.ZIndex=52
    e01Title=Instance.new("TextLabel",e01Card)
    e01Title.Position=UDim2.new(0,58,0,8)
    e01Title.Size=UDim2.new(1,-66,0,18)
    e01Title.BackgroundTransparency=1
    e01Title.Text="KICK WARNING"
    e01Title.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(235,235,240)
    e01Title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    e01Title.TextSize=12
    e01Title.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    e01Title.ZIndex=51
    e01Status=Instance.new("TextLabel",e01Card)
    e01Status.Position=UDim2.new(0,58,0,26)
    e01Status.Size=UDim2.new(1,-66,0,20)
    e01Status.BackgroundTransparency=1
    e01Status.Text="WAIT — 3.0s"
    e01Status.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,94)
    e01Status.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
    e01Status.TextSize=10
    e01Status.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    e01Status.ZIndex=51
end

local function e01Run()
    e01BuildGui()
    if not e01Card then return end
    e01Card.Visible=true
    e01Stroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,94)
    e01Ring.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,94)
    e01RingTxt.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,12,16)
    e01Status.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,94)
    local duration=3
    local startT=tick()
    if e01Conn then e01Conn:Disconnect(); e01Conn=nil end
    e01Conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        pcall(function()
            local remaining=math.max(0,duration-(tick()-startT))
            if remaining>0 then
                e01RingTxt.Text=string.format("%.1f",remaining)
                e01Status.Text=string.format("WAIT — %.1fs  (don\'t move / combat)",remaining)
            else
                if e01Conn then e01Conn:Disconnect(); e01Conn=nil end
                e01Stroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(61,220,132)
                e01Ring.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(61,220,132)
                e01RingTxt.Text="OK"
                e01Status.Text="SAFE — GO"
                e01Status.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(61,220,132)
                task.delay(2,function() if e01Card then e01Card.Visible=false end end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        pcall(function()
            if antiKickE01Enabled then
                local carrying=celestialIsCarryingBrainrot()
                if carrying and not e01WasCarrying then
                    e01Run()
                end
                e01WasCarrying=carrying
            else
                e01WasCarrying=false
                if e01Card then e01Card.Visible=false end
            end
        end)
    end
end)


-- ============================================================
-- INSTA RESET (logic from standalone InstaReset — camera locked, hipheight kill)
-- Celestial buttons / keybinds still call cursedInstaReset (GUI unchanged)
-- ============================================================
local _resetCooldown = false
local _resetThread = nil
local _resetCameraLocked = false
local _resetLockedCFrame = nil
local _lastResetRequest = 0
local _resetStopSequence = false

local function stopCursedInstaReset()
    _resetStopSequence = true
    _resetCooldown = false
    if _resetThread then
        pcall(function() task.cancel(_resetThread) end)
        _resetThread = nil
    end
    _resetCameraLocked = false
    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                hum.HipHeight = 2
                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then root.CanCollide = true end
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanCollide = true
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function instaResetFast()
    if _resetCooldown then return end
    _resetCooldown = true
    _resetStopSequence = false
    _resetCameraLocked = false

    local character = LP.Character
    if not character then _resetCooldown = false return end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then _resetCooldown = false return end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        _resetLockedCFrame = cam.CFrame
        _resetCameraLocked = true
    end

    local isRespawning = false
    local resetSuccessful = false

    _resetThread = task.spawn(function()
        local attempts = 0
        local maxAttempts = 40
        local originalHipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight

        while character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning and not _resetStopSequence do
            if LP.Character ~= character then
                isRespawning = true
                break
            end
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.HipHeight = 1e30
                humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then root.CanCollide = false end
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)

            if not character or not character.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or LP.Character ~= character then
                resetSuccessful = true
                break
            end

            attempts = attempts + 1
            if attempts >= maxAttempts then break end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        if not resetSuccessful then
            if character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning then
                pcall(function() humanoid.Health = 0 end)
                task.wait(0.1)
                if not character.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                    resetSuccessful = true
                end
            end
        end

        if not resetSuccessful and character and character.Parent and humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.HipHeight = originalHipHeight
                local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then root.CanCollide = true end
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanCollide = true
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        _resetCameraLocked = false
        _resetCooldown = false
        _resetThread = nil
        _resetStopSequence = false
    end)
end

cursedInstaReset = function()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - _lastResetRequest < 0.75 then return end
    _lastResetRequest = now
    instaResetFast()
end

if not _G._CELESTIALResetCameraLock then
    _G._CELESTIALResetCameraLock = true
    -- camera lock loop (same behavior as standalone: hold CFrame while resetting)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.016)
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if _resetCameraLocked and _resetLockedCFrame and cam then
                cam.CFrame = _resetLockedCFrame
            end
        end
    end)
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        stopCursedInstaReset()
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- AUTO SWITCH SPEED (grab brainrot → Carry Speed, drop → Normal)
-- ============================================================
local function playerHoldingBrainrot()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end
    for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
            local n = tool.Name:lower()
            if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("skibidi") or n:find("toilet") then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    -- also check Stealing attribute used by the game
    local ok, val = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
    if ok and val == true then return true end
    local ok2, val2 = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
    if ok2 and val2 == true then return true end
    return false
end

local function applyAutoSwitchSpeed(holding)
    if not autoSwitchSpeedEnabled then return end
    if holding then
        if not carrySpeedActive then
            carrySpeedActive = true
            laggerModeEnabled = false
            laggerCarryActive = false
            _autoSwitchForcedCarry = true
            pcall(refreshSpeedModeLabel)
            if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(true) end
            if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.lagger then mobBtnRefs.lagger(false) end
            if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(false) end
        else
            _autoSwitchForcedCarry = true -- already on; still mark so we can undo on drop
        end
    else
        if _autoSwitchForcedCarry then
            carrySpeedActive = false
            _autoSwitchForcedCarry = false
            pcall(refreshSpeedModeLabel)
            if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(false) end
        end
    end
end

-- Fast detect via ChildAdded/Removed + light poll backup
local _autoSwitchConn = nil
local function startAutoSwitchSpeedWatch()
    if _autoSwitchConn then return end
    _autoSwitchConn = true
    local function hookChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if not autoSwitchSpeedEnabled then return end
            if child:IsA("Tool") then
                task.defer(function()
                    if playerHoldingBrainrot() then applyAutoSwitchSpeed(true) end
                end)
            end
        end)
        char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if not autoSwitchSpeedEnabled then return end
            if child:IsA("Tool") then
                task.defer(function()
                    if not playerHoldingBrainrot() then applyAutoSwitchSpeed(false) end
                end)
            end
        end)
        -- initial check
        task.defer(function()
            if autoSwitchSpeedEnabled and playerHoldingBrainrot() then
                applyAutoSwitchSpeed(true)
            end
        end)
    end
    if LP.Character then hookChar(LP.Character) end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookChar)
    -- backup poll (handles attribute-only steals / edge cases)
    task.spawn(function()
        local lastHold = false
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)
            if not autoSwitchSpeedEnabled then
                if _autoSwitchForcedCarry then
                    applyAutoSwitchSpeed(false)
                end
                lastHold = false
            else
                local hold = playerHoldingBrainrot()
                if hold ~= lastHold then
                    lastHold = hold
                    applyAutoSwitchSpeed(hold)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(startAutoSwitchSpeedWatch)


-- ============================================================
-- SAFE MODE (VYNX-style: lock combat during duel countdown / holding brainrot)
-- ============================================================
local function safeModeGetCountdownLabel()
    local ok, label = pcall(function()
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return nil end
        local top = pg:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
        if not top then return nil end
        local inner = top:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame") or top
        local timer = inner:FindFirstChild("Timer", true)
        if not timer then return nil end
        return timer:FindFirstChild("Label") or timer:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
    end)
    return (ok and label) or nil
end
local function safeModeCountdownNumber(text)
    local t = tostring(text or ""):upper():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if t == "GO" or t == "START" or t == "READY" then return true end
    -- Accept common countdown formats such as 5, 0:05, 00:05, and "5s".
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n ~= nil then return n >= 0 and n <= 10 end
    local mins, secs = t:match("^(%d+):(%d%d)$")
    if mins and secs then
        local total = (tonumber(mins) or 0) * 60 + (tonumber(secs) or 0)
        return total >= 0 and total <= 10
    end
    local seconds = t:match("^(%d+)%s*S$")
    if seconds then
        local total = tonumber(seconds)
        return total ~= nil and total >= 0 and total <= 10
    end
    return false
end
local function safeModeInDuelCountdown()
    local label = safeModeGetCountdownLabel()
    return label and safeModeCountdownNumber(label.Text) or false
end

-- If a bat knocks the character down, allow a queued path to start immediately.
local function celestialIsBatStunned()
    local char = LP.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    local state = hum:GetState()
    return hum.PlatformStand == true
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
end

local function safeModeHoldingBrainrot()
    if brainrotDetected then return true end
    if type(celestialIsCarryingBrainrot)=="function" then
        return celestialIsCarryingBrainrot()
    end
    local ok, val = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
    if ok and val == true then return true end
    local ok2, val2 = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("AntiKick") end)
    if ok2 and val2 == true then return true end
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local ok3, val3 = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
    if ok3 and val3 == true then return true end
    for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
            local n = tool.Name:lower()
            if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("skibidi") or n:find("toilet") then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end
-- S2 logic: Anti Kick OR Safe Mode both lock combat during countdown / carry
local function safeModeIsLocked()
    if not (safeModeEnabled or antiKickEnabled) then return false end
    return (safeModeInDuelCountdown() and not celestialIsBatStunned()) or safeModeHoldingBrainrot()
end
local function safeModeForceStop(reason)
    if autoBatEnabled then
        autoBatEnabled=false
        if stopBatAimbot then pcall(stopBatAimbot) end
        if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false) end
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled=false
        if stopAutoLeft then pcall(stopAutoLeft) end
        if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled=false
        if stopAutoRight then pcall(stopAutoRight) end
        if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.autoRight then mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end
    end
    if tpBatEnabled then
        tpBatEnabled=false
        if stopTPBat then pcall(stopTPBat) end
    end
end
local function safeModeTryStart()
    if safeModeIsLocked() then
        safeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK")
        return false
    end
    return true
end

-- Queue Auto Left / Auto Right / Auto Duel until the game's 5-second duel
-- countdown finishes. A bat knockdown bypasses the wait for a faster recovery.
local _celestialPendingPathKey = nil
local _celestialPathStartToken = 0

local function cancelCelestialQueuedPath()
    if not _celestialPendingPathKey then return end
    local key = _celestialPendingPathKey
    _celestialPendingPathKey = nil
    _celestialPathStartToken = _celestialPathStartToken + 1
    if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs[key] then pcall(mobBtnRefs[key], false) end
    if key == "autoLeft" and autoLeftSetVisual then pcall(autoLeftSetVisual, false) end
    if key == "autoRight" and autoRightSetVisual then pcall(autoRightSetVisual, false) end
    if key == "autoDuel" and autoDuelSetVisual then pcall(autoDuelSetVisual, false) end
end

local function queueCelestialPathStart(key)
    cancelCelestialQueuedPath()
    _celestialPathStartToken = _celestialPathStartToken + 1
    local token = _celestialPathStartToken
    _celestialPendingPathKey = key
    if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs[key] then pcall(mobBtnRefs[key], true) end

    task.spawn(function()
        -- Keep the selected button queued/on until the countdown really ends.
        -- If Safe Mode is also blocking the action (for example, while carrying),
        -- continue waiting instead of switching the button off and requiring another tap.
        while token == _celestialPathStartToken
            and _celestialPendingPathKey == key do
            local stunned = celestialIsBatStunned()
            local countdown = safeModeInDuelCountdown()
            local carrying = safeModeHoldingBrainrot()
            if stunned and not carrying then break end
            if not countdown and not carrying then break end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        if token ~= _celestialPathStartToken or _celestialPendingPathKey ~= key then return end

        -- Re-check before starting so a countdown/carry lock never cancels the queued request.
        if safeModeTryStart and not safeModeTryStart() then
            task.wait(0.05)
            if token ~= _celestialPathStartToken or _celestialPendingPathKey ~= key then return end
            -- Re-enter the queue with the same token; the request stays selected.
            while token == _celestialPathStartToken
                and _celestialPendingPathKey == key
                and safeModeTryStart and not safeModeTryStart() do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            if token ~= _celestialPathStartToken or _celestialPendingPathKey ~= key then return end
        end

        _celestialPendingPathKey = nil

        if key == "autoLeft" then
            autoLeftEnabled = true
            if startAutoLeft then startAutoLeft() end
            if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(true) end
        elseif key == "autoRight" then
            autoRightEnabled = true
            if startAutoRight then startAutoRight() end
            if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(true) end
        elseif key == "autoDuel" then
            if startAutoDuel then startAutoDuel() end
            if autoDuelSetVisual then autoDuelSetVisual(true) end
        end
        if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs[key] then pcall(mobBtnRefs[key], true) end
    end)
end

local function enableSafeMode()
    safeModeEnabled = true
end
local function disableSafeMode()
    safeModeEnabled = false
end
if not _G._SafeModeMonitorStarted_CELESTIAL then
    _G._SafeModeMonitorStarted_CELESTIAL = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.15)
            if (safeModeEnabled or antiKickEnabled) and safeModeIsLocked() then
                safeModeForceStop("ANTI-KICK / SAFE LOCK")
            end
        end
    end)
end

AP_L1,AP_L2=Vector3.new(-476.47,-6.28,92.73),Vector3.new(-483.12,-4.95,94.81)
AP_R1,AP_R2=Vector3.new(-476.16,-6.52,25.62),Vector3.new(-483.06,-5.03,25.48)
PLOT_POSITIONS={[3]=Vector3.new(-476.7524719238281,10.464664459228516,7.107429504394531),[7]=Vector3.new(-476.7524719238281,10.464664459228516,114.10742950439453)}
local function getPlotOwner(part)
    if not part then return nil end
    for _,c in ipairs(part:GetDescendants()) do
        if c:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
            for _,l in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                if l:IsA("TextLabel") and l.Text~="" then return l.Text end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function checkMyPlot()
    local scanRoot=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") or workspace
    for plotNum,pos in pairs(PLOT_POSITIONS) do
        for _,obj in ipairs(scanRoot:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name=="PlotSign" and (obj.Position-pos).Magnitude<5 then
                local owner=getPlotOwner(obj)
                if owner and (owner:find(LP.Name,1,true) or owner:find(LP.DisplayName,1,true)) then
                    return plotNum
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
Steal={AutoStealEnabled=false,StealRadius=62,StealDuration=1.3,PausePercent=70,ContinueRadius=10,WaitTimeout=1.7,Data={},plotCache={},plotCacheTime={},cachedPrompts={}}
lastStealTick=0
isStealing,stealStartTime=false,nil
Conns={autoSteal=nil,antiRag=nil,batCounter=nil,anchor={}}
MEDUSA_COOLDOWN=25;batCounterDebounce=false
modeValLbl=nil;lastMoveDir=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
_lastRagExitTime=0  -- only re-apply lastMoveDir for a short window after ragdoll exit
MOVE_KEYS={[Enum.KeyCode.W]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.A]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.S]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.D]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Up]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Left]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Down]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Right]=true}
local function isRagdollState(hum)
    if not hum then return true end
    local st=hum:GetState()
    -- FallingDown is normal (jumps/falls) - treating it as ragdoll caused random death loops
    return hum.PlatformStand or st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
end
local function isMyPlotByName(plotName)
    local ct=tick()
    if Steal.plotCache[plotName] ~= nil and (ct-(Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] or 0)) < 2 then
        return Steal.plotCache[plotName]
    end
    local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots"); if not plots then Steal.plotCache[plotName]=false; Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName]=ct; return false end
    local plot=plots:FindFirstChild(plotName); if not plot then Steal.plotCache[plotName]=false; Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName]=ct; return false end
    local sign=plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    if sign then
        local yb=sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            local r = yb.Enabled==true
            Steal.plotCache[plotName]=r; Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName]=ct
            return r
        end
        -- fallback: owner name on sign matches local display name
        local sg = sign:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui")
        local frame = sg and sg:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        local lbl = frame and frame:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
        if lbl and lbl.Text and lbl.Text ~= "Empty Base" then
            local owner = lbl.Text:gsub("'s [Bb]ase$", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            local r = (owner == LP.DisplayName) or (owner == LP.Name)
            Steal.plotCache[plotName]=r; Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName]=ct
            return r
        end
    end
    Steal.plotCache[plotName]=false; Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName]=ct
    return false
end

-- ============================================================
-- AUTO CARRY NEAR ENEMY BASE (Clean Hub logic)
-- Near an enemy podium -> Carry Speed. Stays on while you hold the brainrot.
-- Drop it -> back to Normal (and stays Normal until you leave the base radius).
-- Controlled by the "Auto Switch Speed" toggle.
-- ============================================================
function autoCarryNearEnemyBase(radius)
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return false end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return false end
    local rpos = root.Position
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        if plot:IsA("Model") and not isMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if pods then
                for _, pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
                    local base = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                    local sp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if sp and (sp.Position - rpos).Magnitude <= radius then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

task.spawn(function()
    local wasHolding = false
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        if not autoSwitchSpeedEnabled then
            _autoCarryLatched = false
            _autoCarrySuppressed = false
            wasHolding = false
        else
            local okN, near = pcall(autoCarryNearEnemyBase, tonumber(autoCarryRadius) or 10)
            near = okN and near or false
            local okH, holding = pcall(playerHoldingBrainrot)
            holding = okH and holding or false

            -- just dropped it: go Normal now, even if still near the base
            if wasHolding and not holding then
                _autoCarrySuppressed = near
                _autoCarryLatched = false
            end
            wasHolding = holding

            if not near then _autoCarrySuppressed = false end

            if near and not _autoCarrySuppressed then
                _autoCarryLatched = true      -- close to enemy base -> Carry Speed
            elseif _autoCarryLatched and not holding then
                _autoCarryLatched = false     -- not holding anymore -> Normal
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- AUTO GRAB / STEAL  (ported from Celestial.V2)
-- Modes: normal / semi / v1 / v2 / v3 / v4
-- ============================================================

}

;(function()
    local startSemiAutoSteal, stopSemiAutoSteal

    -- ---------- NORMAL mode ----------
    local function findNearestPrompt()
        local char=LP.Character;if not char then return nil end
        local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");if not root then return nil end
        local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots");if not plots then return nil end
        local nearest,dist=nil,math.huge
        for _,plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            if isMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then continue end
            local pods=plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums");if not pods then continue end
            for _,pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
                local base=pod:FindFirstChild("Base");local sp=base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if sp then
                    local d=(sp.Position-root.Position).Magnitude
                    if d<=Steal.StealRadius and dist>d then
                        local att=sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                        if att then for _,prompt in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do if prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and prompt.ActionText:find("Steal") then nearest,dist=prompt,d end end end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nearest
    end

    local function executeSteal(prompt)
        if isStealing then return end
        if not Steal.Data[prompt] then
            Steal.Data[prompt]={hold={},trigger={},ready=true}
            if getconnections then
                for _,c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].hold,c.Function) end end
                for _,c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].trigger,c.Function) end end
            end
        end
        local data=Steal.Data[prompt];if not data.ready then return end
        data.ready=false;isStealing=true;stealStartTime=tick()
        task.spawn(function()
            for _,fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(fn) end
            task.wait(Steal.StealDuration)
            for _,fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(fn) end
            task.wait(.06)
            data.ready=true;isStealing=false;stealStartTime=nil
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- SEMI NORMAL mode (old Trubl logic: pause at Pause %, wait for close range or timeout, resume) ----------
    local snConn=nil
    local function promptPos(prompt)
        local att=prompt and prompt.Parent
        local sp=att and att.Parent
        return sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") and sp.Position or nil
    end
    local function executeSemiNormal(prompt)
        if isStealing or not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
        if not Steal.Data[prompt] then
            Steal.Data[prompt]={hold={},trigger={},ready=true}
            if getconnections then
                for _,c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].hold,c.Function) end end
                for _,c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].trigger,c.Function) end end
            end
        end
        local data=Steal.Data[prompt]; if not data.ready then return end
        data.ready=false; isStealing=true; stealStartTime=tick()
        local S=SemiSteal.State
        S.active=true; S.startTime=stealStartTime; S.progress=0; S.paused=false; S.phase="holding"; S.inRange=false
        task.spawn(function()
            for _,fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(fn) end
            local duration=tonumber(Steal.StealDuration) or 1.3
            local radius=tonumber(Steal.StealRadius) or 62
            local pausePct=math.clamp((tonumber(Steal.PausePercent) or 70)/100,0.01,0.99)
            local continueRadius=tonumber(Steal.ContinueRadius) or 10
            local waitTimeout=tonumber(Steal.WaitTimeout) or 1.7
            local startTime=tick()
            local paused,snap,pauseStart=false,false,0
            local fired=false
            while Steal.AutoStealEnabled and stealMode=="semi_normal" and prompt.Parent do
                local pos=promptPos(prompt)
                local root=LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not pos or not root then break end
                local d=(root.Position-pos).Magnitude
                if d>radius then break end
                S.inRange=d<=continueRadius
                if not paused then
                    local ratio=math.clamp((tick()-startTime)/duration,0,1)
                    if ratio>=pausePct and not snap then
                        paused=true; snap=true; pauseStart=tick()
                        S.paused=true; S.phase="waiting"; S.progress=pausePct
                    else
                        S.progress=ratio
                        if ratio>=1 then
                            for _,fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(fn) end
                            fired=true; S.progress=1
                            break
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if tick()-pauseStart>=waitTimeout or d<=continueRadius then
                        paused=false; S.paused=false; S.phase="holding"
                        startTime=tick()-pausePct*duration
                    end
                end
                task.wait()
            end
            if fired then
                S.totalSteals=S.totalSteals+1; S.lastResult="Stole (semi normal)"
            else
                S.failedSteals=S.failedSteals+1; S.lastResult="Semi normal cancelled"
            end
            S.lastResultTime=tick()
            task.wait(.05)
            S.active=false; S.progress=0; S.inRange=false; S.paused=false; S.phase="idle"
            isStealing=false; stealStartTime=nil
            data.ready=true
        end)
    end
    local function startSemiNormal()
        if snConn then return end
        snConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not Steal.AutoStealEnabled or stealMode~="semi_normal" or isStealing or SemiSteal.State.active then return end
            local p=findNearestPrompt(); if p then executeSemiNormal(p) end
        end)
    end
    local function stopSemiNormal()
        if snConn then snConn:Disconnect(); snConn=nil end
    end

    -- ---------- SEMI mode engine ----------
    local function _initSemiStealEngine()
        local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local SEMI_CONFIG = SemiSteal.CONFIG

        local syncRemotes = nil
        local plots = nil
        pcall(function()
            plots = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 8)
            local folder = RS:WaitForChild("Packages", 8):WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 8)
            syncRemotes = {
                channelFolder = folder:WaitForChild("Channel", 8),
                routeRemote   = folder:WaitForChild("CommunicationRoute", 8),
                requestData   = folder:FindFirstChild("RequestData"),
            }
        end)

        local AnimalsData = nil
        pcall(function()
            AnimalsData = require(RS:WaitForChild("Datas", 8):WaitForChild("Animals", 8))
        end)

        local plotAnimalSync = { caches = {}, connections = {} }
        local allAnimalsCache = {}
        local SemiPromptCache = {}
        local SemiCallbackCache = {}

        local function splitSyncPath(path)
            if type(path)=="table" then return path end
            local out={}
            for part in string.gmatch(tostring(path),"[^%.]+") do
                table.insert(out, tonumber(part) or part)
            end
            return out
        end

        local function resolveSyncPath(path, root)
            local current,parent,key=root,nil,nil
            for _,part in ipairs(splitSyncPath(path)) do
                parent=current; key=part
                current=current and current[part] or nil
            end
            return current,parent,key
        end

        local function applyPlotSyncDiff(channelName, packet)
            local cache=plotAnimalSync.caches[channelName]
            if type(cache)~="table" then return end
            local path,action,a,b=packet[1],packet[2],packet[3],packet[4]
            local current,parent,key=resolveSyncPath(path,cache)
            if action=="Changed" then if parent then parent[key]=a end
            elseif action=="ArrayInsert" then if current then table.insert(current,b,a) end
            elseif action=="ArrayRemoved" then if current then table.remove(current,b) end
            elseif action=="DictionaryInsert" then if current then current[b]=a end
            elseif action=="DictionaryRemoved" then if current then current[b]=nil end
            end
        end

        local function attachPlotChannel(remote)
            if not plots or not syncRemotes then return end
            if plotAnimalSync.connections[remote] then return end
            local channelName=tostring(remote.Name)
            if not plots:FindFirstChild(channelName) then return end
            if syncRemotes.requestData and plotAnimalSync.caches[channelName]==nil then
                local ok,data=pcall(function() return syncRemotes.requestData:InvokeServer(channelName) end)
                plotAnimalSync.caches[channelName]=(ok and type(data)=="table") and data or {}
            elseif plotAnimalSync.caches[channelName]==nil then
                plotAnimalSync.caches[channelName]={}
            end
            plotAnimalSync.connections[remote]=remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(queue)
                for _,packet in ipairs(queue) do applyPlotSyncDiff(channelName,packet) end
            end)
        end

        local function detachPlotChannel(channelName)
            for remote,conn in pairs(plotAnimalSync.connections) do
                if tostring(remote.Name)==tostring(channelName) then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                    plotAnimalSync.connections[remote]=nil
                    plotAnimalSync.caches[tostring(channelName)]=nil
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        pcall(function()
            if not syncRemotes then return end
            for _,child in ipairs(syncRemotes.channelFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") then attachPlotChannel(child) end
            end
            syncRemotes.channelFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") then attachPlotChannel(child) end
            end)
            syncRemotes.routeRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(actions)
                for _,action in ipairs(actions) do
                    local kind,channelName=action[1],tostring(action[2])
                    if not plots:FindFirstChild(channelName) then continue end
                    if kind=="ListenerAdded" then
                        local r=syncRemotes.channelFolder:FindFirstChild(channelName)
                        if r and r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then attachPlotChannel(r) end
                    elseif kind=="ListenerRemoved" then detachPlotChannel(channelName) end
                end
            end)
        end)

        local function getPlotOwner2(plot)
            local sign=plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local frame=sign and sign:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui") and sign.SurfaceGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
            local label=frame and frame:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
            if not label or label.Text=="Empty Base" then return nil end
            return label.Text:gsub("'s [Bb]ase$",""):gsub("%s+$","")
        end

        local function isMyBaseAnimal(animalData)
            if not animalData or not animalData.plot then return false end
            local plot=plots and plots:FindFirstChild(animalData.plot)
            if not plot then return false end
            local sign=plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if sign then local yb=sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase"); if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then return yb.Enabled==true end end
            return getPlotOwner2(plot)==LP.DisplayName
        end

        local function scanAllPlots()
            if not plots then return end
            local newCache={}
            for _,plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local cache=plotAnimalSync.caches[plot.Name]
                if not cache then continue end
                local animalList=cache.AnimalList
                if type(animalList)~="table" then continue end
                for slot,animalData in pairs(animalList) do
                    if type(animalData)=="table" then
                        local animalName=animalData.Index
                        local displayName=animalName
                        if AnimalsData then
                            local info=AnimalsData[animalName]
                            if info then displayName=info.DisplayName or animalName end
                        end
                        table.insert(newCache,{
                            name=displayName, plot=plot.Name,
                            slot=tostring(slot), uid=plot.Name.."_"..tostring(slot),
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
            allAnimalsCache=newCache
        end

        task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(5); pcall(scanAllPlots) end end)
        pcall(scanAllPlots)

        local function getAnimalPosition(animalData)
            if not plots then return nil end
            local plot=plots:FindFirstChild(animalData.plot); if not plot then return nil end
            local podiums=plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums"); if not podiums then return nil end
            local podium=podiums:FindFirstChild(animalData.slot); if not podium then return nil end
            return podium:GetPivot().Position
        end

        local function distToAnimal(animalData)
            local char=LP.Character; if not char then return math.huge end
            local hrp=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not hrp then return math.huge end
            local pos=getAnimalPosition(animalData); if not pos then return math.huge end
            return (hrp.Position-pos).Magnitude
        end

        local function findProximityPromptForAnimal(animalData)
            if not animalData then return nil end
            local cached=SemiPromptCache[animalData.uid]
            if cached and cached.Parent then return cached end
            if not plots then return nil end
            local plot=plots:FindFirstChild(animalData.plot); if not plot then return nil end
            local podiums=plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums"); if not podiums then return nil end
            local podium=podiums:FindFirstChild(animalData.slot); if not podium then return nil end
            local base=podium:FindFirstChild("Base"); if not base then return nil end
            local spawn=base:FindFirstChild("Spawn"); if not spawn then return nil end
            local attach=spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment"); if not attach then return nil end
            for _,p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
                if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then SemiPromptCache[animalData.uid]=p; return p end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function pickClosest()
            local char=LP.Character; if not char then return nil end
            local hrp=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not hrp then return nil end
            local best,bestDist=nil,math.huge
            for _,animalData in ipairs(allAnimalsCache) do
                if isMyBaseAnimal(animalData) then continue end
                local pos=getAnimalPosition(animalData); if not pos then continue end
                local dist=(hrp.Position-pos).Magnitude
                if dist>SEMI_CONFIG.PRIME_RANGE then continue end
                if dist<bestDist then bestDist=dist; best=animalData end
            end
            return best
        end

        local function buildSemiCallbacks(prompt)
            if SemiCallbackCache[prompt] then return end
            local data={holdCallbacks={},triggerCallbacks={},ready=true}
            local ok1,conns1=pcall(getconnections,prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
            if ok1 and type(conns1)=="table" then
                for _,conn in ipairs(conns1) do if type(conn.Function)=="function" then table.insert(data.holdCallbacks,conn.Function) end end
            end
