    laggerCarryActive = not laggerCarryActive
    if laggerCarryActive then
        carrySpeedActive = false
        laggerModeEnabled = false
        customSpeedActive = false
    end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(carrySpeedActive) end
    if mobBtnRefs.lagger then mobBtnRefs.lagger(laggerModeEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(laggerCarryActive) end
    for ci=1,3 do if mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci] then mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci](false) end end
end

-- ============================================================
-- RESPAWN SAFETY: reset humanoid death flags on every spawn
-- ============================================================
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.defer(function()
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not hum then return end
        if tpBatEnabled then return end -- TP bat anti-die owns state while active
        pcall(function()
            hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
            hum.RequiresNeck = true
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, true)
            -- clear any leftover physics ownership desync
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root and sethiddenproperty then
                pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(root, "NetworkIsSleeping", false) end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end)

-- ============================================================
-- IMPROVED ANTI RAGDOLL (from new file)
-- ============================================================
antiRagdollCached = {}

local function cacheCharacterAntiRag()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not hum or not root then return false end

    antiRagdollCached = {
        character = char,
        humanoid = hum,
        root = root
    }

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        camera.CameraSubject = hum
    end

    return true
end

local function isRagdolledAntiRag()
    local hum = antiRagdollCached.humanoid
    if not hum then return false end

    local state = hum:GetState()

    if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
    or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
    or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
        return true
    end

    local endTime = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
    if endTime then
        local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        if (endTime - now) > 0 then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

local function removeRagdollConstraints()
    for _, v in ipairs(antiRagdollCached.character:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or 
           (v:IsA("Attachment") and v.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
            pcall(function()
                v:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function forceExitRagdoll()
    local hum = antiRagdollCached.humanoid
    local root = antiRagdollCached.root

    if not hum or not root then return end
    if hum.Health <= 0 then return end   -- don't touch camera on dead humanoid

    pcall(function()
        LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
    end)

    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)

    root.Anchored = false
    applyVel(root, Vector3.zero)
    applyAngVel(root, Vector3.zero)

    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(root.Orientation.Y), 0)

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera and hum.Health > 0 and camera.CameraSubject ~= hum then
        camera.CameraSubject = hum
    end
end

startAntiRagdoll = function()
    -- LaPeace anti-ragdoll logic (ported)
    if Conns.antiRag then return end
    Conns.antiRag = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiRagdollEnabled then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        local state = hum:GetState()
        local isRagdolled = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        if isRagdolled then
            pcall(function()
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    pcall(function()
                        if applyVel then applyVel(root, Vector3.zero) end
                    end)
                end
                for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then
                        obj.Enabled = true
                    elseif obj:IsA("Constraint") or obj:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or obj:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
                        obj.Enabled = true
                    end
                end
                if workspace.CurrentCamera then
                    workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                end
                local ps = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
                local PM = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
                if PM then
                    local CM = PM:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")
                    if CM then
                        local okm, module = pcall(require, CM)
                        if okm and module and module.Enable then
                            pcall(function() module:Enable() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                hum.AutoRotate = true
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                hum.Sit = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end

stopAntiRagdoll = function()
    if Conns.antiRag then
        Conns.antiRag:Disconnect()
        Conns.antiRag = nil
    end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and workspace.CurrentCamera then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
    end
    if antiRagdollEnabled then
        stopAntiRagdoll()
        startAntiRagdoll()
    end
end)
startUnwalk=function()
    local c=LP.Character;if not c then return end;local hum=c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then for _,t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop() end end
    local anim=c:FindFirstChild("Animate");if anim then unwalkSavedAnimate=anim:Clone();anim:Destroy() end
end
stopUnwalk=function() local c=LP.Character;if c and unwalkSavedAnimate then unwalkSavedAnimate:Clone().Parent=c;unwalkSavedAnimate=nil end end

-- ============================================================
-- STEAL BAR
-- ============================================================



-- ============================================================
-- ANTI FLING / WALKFLING (always on - no toggle)
-- Global clamp + stronger protection while any aimbot is active
-- ============================================================
-- Raised thresholds so normal bumps/slopes are not clipped into slide/desync deaths
local ANTI_FLING_MAX_LIN = 160
local ANTI_FLING_MAX_ANG = 45
-- ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_* already set earlier
local _antiFlingConn = nil

local function _antiFlingApply(root, char, maxLin, maxAng)
    if not root or not root.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local horiz = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        local hMag = horiz.Magnitude
        -- Prefer clamping horizontal only so vertical (jumps / slopes) is preserved
        if hMag > maxLin then
            local scale = maxLin / hMag
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * scale, v.Y, v.Z * scale)
        elseif v.Magnitude > maxLin * 1.35 then
            -- extreme full-vector only (true fling)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v.Unit * maxLin
        end
        local av = root.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        if av.Magnitude > maxAng then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if char then
            for _, p in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and p ~= root then
                    local pv = p.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local ph = Vector3.new(pv.X, 0, pv.Z)
                    if ph.Magnitude > maxLin * 1.2 then
                        local scale = (maxLin * 1.05) / ph.Magnitude
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(pv.X * scale, pv.Y, pv.Z * scale)
                    end
                    if p.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > maxAng then
                        p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- applyAimbotAntiFling defined earlier (near runDrop); global clamp uses _antiFlingApply below

local function startAntiFling()
    if _antiFlingConn then return end
    _antiFlingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
        local aimOn = autoBatEnabled or tpBatEnabled
        local maxLin = aimOn and ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_LIN or ANTI_FLING_MAX_LIN
        local maxAng = aimOn and ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_ANG or ANTI_FLING_MAX_ANG
        _antiFlingApply(root, char, maxLin, maxAng)
    end)
end

-- always-on (no stop / no toggle)
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.35)
    startAntiFling()
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.defer(startAntiFling)
end)

-- ============================================================
-- NO PLAYER COLLISION (local character ignores other players)
-- ============================================================
local _noPlayerColConn = nil
local _noPlayerColHB = nil
local function applyNoPlayerCollision()
    -- NEVER change local character CollisionGroup — that can break floor collision.
    -- Only disable collision on OTHER players' parts locally.
    pcall(function()
        local function stripOther(char)
            if not char then return end
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end)
                end
            end
        end
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then stripOther(plr.Character) end
        end
        if not _noPlayerColConn then
            _noPlayerColConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
                plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    stripOther(char)
                end)
                if plr.Character then stripOther(plr.Character) end
            end)
        end
        if not _noPlayerColHB then
            _noPlayerColHB = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
                        for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
                            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                                part.CanCollide = false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1)
    applyNoPlayerCollision()
end)

-- ============================================================
-- PLAYER ESP + TRACERS (theme color) + CELESTIAL user marker
-- ============================================================
local CELESTIAL_MARKER = "CELESTIALDuelsMarker"
playerEspEnabled = playerEspEnabled or false
playerTracersEnabled = playerTracersEnabled or false
local _espFolder = nil
local _espConn = nil
local _tracerLayer = nil -- ScreenGui for 2D tracer lines
local _espObjects = {} -- [player] = { highlight, billboard, tracer, uiLine, drawLine, ... }

local function getEspThemeColor()
    local th = (type(getTheme)=="function" and getTheme()) or nil
    return (th and th.ACCENT) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
end

local function getTracerBrightColor()
    local c = getEspThemeColor()
    -- push toward white so tracers pop more
    local r = math.min(1, c.R * 1.35 + 0.2)
    local g = math.min(1, c.G * 1.35 + 0.2)
    local b = math.min(1, c.B * 1.35 + 0.2)
    return Color3.new(r, g, b)
end

local function ensureEspFolder()
    if _espFolder and _espFolder.Parent then return _espFolder end
    pcall(function()
        local old = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("CELESTIALPlayerESP")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end)
    local f = Instance.new("Folder")
    f.Name = "CELESTIALPlayerESP"
    pcall(function()
        if _gethui then f.Parent = _gethui()
        else f.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end
    end)
    if not f.Parent then
        pcall(function() f.Parent = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") end)
    end
    _espFolder = f
    return f
end

-- Silent CELESTIAL detection (NO chat).
-- Multi-property Player fingerprint (camera zoom + name display distance).
-- These Player properties replicate so other CELESTIAL clients can read them.
local _celestialUsers = {} -- [userId] = true
local CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MAX = 127.89134
local CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MIN = 0.41278
local CELESTIAL_NAME_DIST = 101.337

local function markCELESTIALUserId(uid)
    if type(uid) == "number" and uid ~= LP.UserId then
        _celestialUsers[uid] = true
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- CELESTIAL PRESENCE BACKEND (shared HTTP)
-- All CELESTIAL clients heartbeat into the same JSON blob so other
-- users running this source show "USING CELESTIAL" above their head.
-- ============================================================
local CELESTIAL_PRESENCE_URL = "https://jsonblob.com/api/jsonBlob/019fd958-1bb9-79ea-ac53-d695c6d450e6"
local CELESTIAL_PRESENCE_TTL = 25 -- seconds; stale entries ignored
local _presenceLastPush = 0
local _presenceBusy = false

local function _presenceRequest(method, body)
    local resBody = nil
    if _request then
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            local opts = {
                Url = CELESTIAL_PRESENCE_URL,
                Method = method,
                Headers = {
                    ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                    ["Accept"] = "application/json",
                },
            }
            if body then opts.Body = body end
            return _request(opts)
        end)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" then
            resBody = res.Body or res.body or res.Data or res.data
        elseif ok and type(res) == "string" then
            resBody = res
        end
    end
    if not resBody and method == "GET" then
        pcall(function()
            resBody = game:HttpGet(CELESTIAL_PRESENCE_URL)
        end)
    end
    return resBody
end

local function _presenceDecode(raw)
    if type(raw) ~= "string" or #raw < 2 then return nil end
    local ok, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(raw) end)
    if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end
    return nil
end

local function presencePush()
    do return end -- disabled: external HTTP presence can flag PC clients
    if _presenceBusy then return end
    _presenceBusy = true
    pcall(function()
        local jobId = tostring(game.JobId or "unknown")
        local uid = tostring(LP.UserId)
        local now = os.time()

        local data = _presenceDecode(_presenceRequest("GET")) or { v = 1, servers = {} }
        if type(data.servers) ~= "table" then data.servers = {} end
        if type(data.servers[jobId]) ~= "table" then data.servers[jobId] = {} end

        -- write self
        data.servers[jobId][uid] = now

        -- prune stale entries across all servers (keep map small)
        for jid, users in pairs(data.servers) do
            if type(users) == "table" then
                for u, ts in pairs(users) do
                    if type(ts) ~= "number" or (now - ts) > (CELESTIAL_PRESENCE_TTL * 3) then
                        users[u] = nil
                    end
                end
                if next(users) == nil then
                    data.servers[jid] = nil
                end
            end
        end

        local encoded = HS:JSONEncode(data)
        _presenceRequest("PUT", encoded)
        _presenceLastPush = tick()
    end)
    _presenceBusy = false
end

local function presencePull()
    do return end -- disabled: external HTTP presence can flag PC clients
    pcall(function()
        local jobId = tostring(game.JobId or "unknown")
        local now = os.time()
        local data = _presenceDecode(_presenceRequest("GET"))
        if not data or type(data.servers) ~= "table" then return end
        local users = data.servers[jobId]
        if type(users) ~= "table" then return end
        for u, ts in pairs(users) do
            local uid = tonumber(u)
            if uid and uid ~= LP.UserId and type(ts) == "number" and (now - ts) <= CELESTIAL_PRESENCE_TTL then
                markCELESTIALUserId(uid)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- heartbeat loop (push + pull)
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1.2)
    while true do
        pcall(presencePush)
        pcall(presencePull)
        task.wait(4)
    end
end)

-- faster local tag refresh after pull
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(2)
        pcall(presencePull)
        pcall(function()
            if updateCELESTIALTags then updateCELESTIALTags() end
        end)
    end
end)

local function applyCELESTIALFingerprint()
    -- Local-only fingerprint (no character StringValues/Billboards — those replicate and can trigger PC anti-cheat)
    pcall(function()
        LP.CameraMaxZoomDistance = CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MAX
        LP.CameraMinZoomDistance = CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MIN
        LP.NameDisplayDistance = CELESTIAL_NAME_DIST
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            getgenv()._CELESTIALDuelsFP = "v2"
            getgenv()._CELESTIALDuelsRunning = true
        end
    end)
end

local function placeCELESTIALMarker(char)
    applyCELESTIALFingerprint()
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.3)
    placeCELESTIALMarker(char)
end)


local function _matchesCELESTIALFP(plr)
    if not plr then return false end
    -- Primary: character marker (replicates via character network ownership)
    local char = plr.Character
    if char then
        local marker = char:FindFirstChild(CELESTIAL_MARKER, true)
        if marker then return true end
        local tag = char:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALDuelsTag", true)
        if tag then return true end
        local cAttrOk, cAttr = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("CELESTIALDuels") end)
        if cAttrOk and cAttr == true then return true end
    end
    local hits = 0
    local ok1, zmax = pcall(function() return plr.CameraMaxZoomDistance end)
    if ok1 and typeof(zmax) == "number" and math.abs(zmax - CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MAX) < 0.002 then
        hits = hits + 1
    end
    local ok2, zmin = pcall(function() return plr.CameraMinZoomDistance end)
    if ok2 and typeof(zmin) == "number" and math.abs(zmin - CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MIN) < 0.002 then
        hits = hits + 1
    end
    local ok3, nd = pcall(function() return plr.NameDisplayDistance end)
    if ok3 and typeof(nd) == "number" and math.abs(nd - CELESTIAL_NAME_DIST) < 0.05 then
        hits = hits + 1
    end
    if hits >= 2 then return true end
    if hits >= 1 and ok1 and typeof(zmax) == "number" and math.abs(zmax - CELESTIAL_ZOOM_MAX) < 0.0005 then
        return true
    end
    local attrOk, attr = pcall(function() return plr:GetAttribute("CELESTIALDuels") end)
    if attrOk and attr == true then return true end
    local fpOk, fp = pcall(function() return plr:GetAttribute("CELESTIALFP") end)
    if fpOk and fp == "v2" then return true end
    return false
end

local function isCELESTIALUser(plr)
    if not plr or plr == LP then return false end
    if _celestialUsers[plr.UserId] then return true end
    if _matchesCELESTIALFP(plr) then
        markCELESTIALUserId(plr.UserId)
        return true
    end
    return false
end


-- Always-on "USING CELESTIAL" tags (works even if Player ESP toggle is off)
local _celestialTagFolder = nil
local function ensureCELESTIALTagFolder()
    if _celestialTagFolder and _celestialTagFolder.Parent then return _celestialTagFolder end
    local f = Instance.new("Folder")
    f.Name = "CELESTIALUserTags"
    local parented = false
    pcall(function()
        if parentGui then parentGui(f); parented = f.Parent ~= nil end
    end)
    if not parented then
        pcall(function() f.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    end
    if not f.Parent then
        pcall(function() f.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end)
    end
    _celestialTagFolder = f
    return f
end
local _celestialBillboards = {}
local function updateCELESTIALTags()
    local folder = ensureCELESTIALTagFolder()
    local seen = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and isCELESTIALUser(plr) then
            seen[plr] = true
            local char = plr.Character
            local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            if head then
                local o = _celestialBillboards[plr]
                if not o or not o.Parent then
                    pcall(function() if o then o:Destroy() end end)
                    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    bb.Name = "UsingCELESTIAL_" .. tostring(plr.UserId)
                    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 36)
                    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
                    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    bb.MaxDistance = 600
                    bb.Adornee = head
                    bb.Parent = folder
                    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
                    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    lbl.Text = "USING CELESTIAL"
                    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    lbl.TextSize = 18
                    lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
                    lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    _celestialBillboards[plr] = bb
                else
                    o.Adornee = head
                    o.Enabled = true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for plr, bb in pairs(_celestialBillboards) do
        if not seen[plr] then
            pcall(function() bb:Destroy() end)
            _celestialBillboards[plr] = nil
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.75)
        pcall(updateCELESTIALTags)
    end
end)

-- Keep fingerprint applied every frame (games often reset zoom) + scan others often
task.spawn(function()
    applyCELESTIALFingerprint()
    local acc = 0
    while true do
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        pcall(applyCELESTIALFingerprint)
        acc = acc + 1
        if acc >= 30 then -- ~0.5s at 60fps
            acc = 0
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LP then
                    pcall(function()
                        if _matchesCELESTIALFP(plr) then
                            markCELESTIALUserId(plr.UserId)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- PLAYER ESP + TRACERS (S2-style, lightweight)
-- Creates UI once per player; updates positions only (no rebuild every frame)
-- ============================================================
ESP_ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
BoxedESPOptions = BoxedESPOptions or {box=false, tracer=false}
BoxedESPData = BoxedESPData or {}
BoxedESPConn = BoxedESPConn or nil
local BoxedESPGui = nil

local function _espAccent()
    local th = (type(getTheme)=="function" and getTheme()) or nil
    if th and th.ACCENT then return th.ACCENT end
    return Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
end

local function _boxedESPLayer()
    if BoxedESPGui and BoxedESPGui.Parent then return BoxedESPGui end
    local hui = _gethui and _gethui()
    local old = hui and hui:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALBoxESP")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("CELESTIALBoxESP")
        if cg then cg:Destroy() end
    end)
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "CELESTIALBoxESP"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 900
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    parentGui(gui)
    BoxedESPGui = gui
    return gui
end

local function _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
    local data = BoxedESPData[player]
    if not data then return end
    pcall(function() if data.holder then data.holder:Destroy() end end)
    pcall(function() if data.tracer then data.tracer:Destroy() end end)
    BoxedESPData[player] = nil
end

local function _cleanupBoxedESP()
    for player, _ in pairs(BoxedESPData) do
        _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
    end
end

local function _buildBoxedESPEntry(player)
    local layer = _boxedESPLayer()
    local accent = _espAccent()
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "ESP_" .. player.Name
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    holder.Visible = false
    holder.ZIndex = 2
    holder.Parent = layer
    local edge = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    edge.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    edge.Color = accent
    edge.Thickness = 2
    edge.Transparency = 0
    edge.Parent = holder
    local tracer = Instance.new("Frame")
    tracer.Name = "Tracer"
    tracer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    tracer.BackgroundColor3 = accent
    tracer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tracer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)
    tracer.Visible = false
    tracer.ZIndex = 1
    tracer.Parent = layer
    local data = {holder = holder, edge = edge, tracer = tracer}
    BoxedESPData[player] = data
    return data
end

local function _updateBoxedESP()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
    if not anyOn then
        _cleanupBoxedESP()
        return
    end
    ESP_ACCENT = _espAccent()
    local view = cam.ViewportSize
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LP then
            local char = player.Character
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not root or not head then
                _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
            else
                local rootPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
                local headPos = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0))
                local data = BoxedESPData[player]
                if not data then data = _buildBoxedESPEntry(player) end
                if not onScreen or rootPos.Z < 0 then
                    if data.holder then data.holder.Visible = false end
                    if data.tracer then data.tracer.Visible = false end
                else
                    local scale = math.clamp(1 / (rootPos.Z * 0.02), 0.45, 2.2)
                    local h = math.abs(headPos.Y - rootPos.Y) * 2.15 * scale
                    local w = h * 0.55
                    local cx, cy = rootPos.X, rootPos.Y
                    if BoxedESPOptions.box == true and data.holder then
                        data.holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h)
                        data.holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx - w / 2, 0, cy - h / 2)
                        data.holder.Visible = true
                        if data.edge then data.edge.Color = ESP_ACCENT end
                    elseif data.holder then
                        data.holder.Visible = false
                    end
                    local targetX, targetY = cx, cy + h / 2
                    if data.tracer then
                        data.tracer.BackgroundColor3 = ESP_ACCENT
                        local localChar = LP.Character
                        local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local fromX, fromY
                        if localRoot then
                            local localScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(localRoot.Position)
                            fromX = localScreen.X
                            fromY = localScreen.Y + 15
                        end
                        if not fromX or not fromY then
                            fromX = view.X / 2
                            fromY = view.Y - 88
                        end
                        if BoxedESPOptions.tracer == true then
                            local dx, dy = targetX - fromX, targetY - fromY
                            local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                            data.tracer.Size = UDim2.new(0, len, 0, 2)
                            data.tracer.Position = UDim2.new(0, fromX + dx / 2, 0, fromY + dy / 2)
                            data.tracer.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
                            data.tracer.Visible = true
                        else
                            data.tracer.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function refreshBoxedESP()
    local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
    if anyOn and not BoxedESPConn then
        BoxedESPConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(_updateBoxedESP)
    elseif (not anyOn) and BoxedESPConn then
        BoxedESPConn:Disconnect()
        BoxedESPConn = nil
        _cleanupBoxedESP()
    end
end

local function espApplyFlags()
    BoxedESPOptions.box = playerEspEnabled == true
    BoxedESPOptions.tracer = playerTracersEnabled == true
    refreshBoxedESP()
end

startPlayerEsp = function()
    playerEspEnabled = true
    espApplyFlags()
end

stopPlayerEsp = function()
    playerEspEnabled = false
    espApplyFlags()
end

startPlayerTracers = function()
    playerTracersEnabled = true
    espApplyFlags()
end

stopPlayerTracers = function()
    playerTracersEnabled = false
    espApplyFlags()
end

-- keep fingerprint/marker on our character
task.spawn(function()
    local function onChar(char)
        task.wait(0.25)
        placeCELESTIALMarker(char)
    end
    if LP.Character then onChar(LP.Character) end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(onChar)
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    pcall(function() _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(plr) end)
    if plr then _celestialUsers[plr.UserId] = nil end
end)



-- ============================================================
-- CONTROLLER / GAMEPAD SUPPORT
-- ============================================================
local function enableControllerGui(root)
    pcall(function()
        local gs = game:GetService("GuiService")
        gs.GuiNavigationEnabled = true
        gs.AutoSelectGuiEnabled = true
    end)
    if not root then return end
    local function mark(gui)
        if not gui then return end
        pcall(function()
            if gui:IsA("TextButton") or gui:IsA("ImageButton") or gui:IsA("TextBox") then
                gui.Selectable = true
                if gui:IsA("GuiButton") then
                    gui.AutoButtonColor = true
                end
            elseif gui:IsA("Frame") or gui:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                -- keep frames non-selectable unless they are clickable shells
            end
        end)
        for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then
                    d.Selectable = true
                elseif d:IsA("TextBox") then
                    d.Selectable = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    mark(root)
end

local function setupControllerDefaults()
    -- ensure default gamepad binds if still empty (fresh install / reset)
    local defaults = {
        DropBrainrot = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX,
        AutoLeft = Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft,
        AutoRight = Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight,
        AutoBat = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1,
        TPBat = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR3,
        TPFloor = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonY,
        GuiHide = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect,
        SpeedToggle = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1,
        LaggerToggle = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2,
        LaggerCarry = Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2,
        AntiDesync = Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp,
        LaggerPanel = Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown,
    }
    for name, code in pairs(defaults) do
        if KB[name] and KB[name].gp == nil then
            KB[name].gp = code
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local gs = game:GetService("GuiService")
        gs.GuiNavigationEnabled = true
        gs.AutoSelectGuiEnabled = true
    end)
end
setupControllerDefaults()

local function createStealBar()
    -- LaPeace-style status bar look: STEAL | SEARCHING | FPS | PING
    pcall(function()
        if destroyOldGui then destroyOldGui("CELESTIALInfoBar") end
        if destroyOldGui then destroyOldGui("CELESTIALStealBar") end
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        for _, n in ipairs({"CELESTIALInfoBar", "CELESTIALStealBar"}) do
            local o = cg:FindFirstChild(n); if o then o:Destroy() end
        end
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            for _, n in ipairs({"CELESTIALInfoBar", "CELESTIALStealBar"}) do
                local o = pg:FindFirstChild(n); if o then o:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end)

    local SB_ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(140, 90, 255)
    local SB_BG = Color3.fromRGB(8, 9, 15)
    local SB_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local SB_W, SB_H = 320, 36

    local function mkCorner(p, r)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner", p)
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        return c
    end

    local infoGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    infoGui.Name = "CELESTIALInfoBar"
    infoGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    infoGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    infoGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    infoGui.DisplayOrder = 50
    pcall(function()
        if parentGui then parentGui(infoGui) else infoGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end
    end)
    if not infoGui.Parent then
        pcall(function() infoGui.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end)
    end

    local infoBar = Instance.new("Frame", infoGui)
    infoBar.Name = "StealStatusBar"
    infoBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, SB_W, 0, SB_H)
    if type(stealBarPos) == "table" and stealBarPos.sx ~= nil then
        infoBar.Position = UDim2.new(stealBarPos.sx or 0.5, stealBarPos.ox or -SB_W / 2, stealBarPos.sy or 0.06, stealBarPos.oy or 0)
    else
        infoBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -SB_W / 2, 0.06, 0)
    end
    infoBar.BackgroundColor3 = SB_BG
    infoBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    infoBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    infoBar.Active = true
    infoBar.ClipsDescendants = true
    infoBar.ZIndex = 10
    mkCorner(infoBar)
    pcall(function() CelestialBG.register("bar", infoBar) end)
    local barStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", infoBar)
    barStroke.Color = SB_ACCENT
    barStroke.Thickness = 2
    barStroke.Transparency = 0.15
    barStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    -- subtle pulse on stroke
    task.spawn(function()
        local t = 0
        while barStroke and barStroke.Parent do
            t = t + 0.05
            barStroke.Transparency = 0.08 + math.abs(math.sin(t * 2)) * 0.28
            task.wait(0.04)
        end
    end)

    -- drag (respect uiLocked)
    do
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
        infoBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
            if uiLocked then return end
            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = inp.Position
                startPos = infoBar.Position
                inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging = false
                        pcall(function()
                            local pos = infoBar.Position
                            stealBarPos = {sx = pos.X.Scale, ox = pos.X.Offset, sy = pos.Y.Scale, oy = pos.Y.Offset}
                            if type(saveConfig) == "function" then saveConfig() end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
            if not dragging then return end
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            local d = inp.Position - dragStart
            infoBar.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end)
    end

    -- progress fill behind labels (full height)
    progressFill = Instance.new("Frame", infoBar)
    progressFill.Name = "ProgressFill"
    progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    progressFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = SB_ACCENT
    progressFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
    progressFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progressFill.ZIndex = 11
    mkCorner(progressFill)
    local fillGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", progressFill)
    fillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(120, 70, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(200, 160, 255)),
    })

    -- ===== AUTO STEAL FILL ANIMATION (NEON CHARGE) =====
    -- theme-colored flowing fill + scrolling charge stripes + comet orb + lightning flicker + finish flash
    progressFill.ClipsDescendants = true

    local STRIPE_N, STRIPE_GAP = 13, 26
    local stripes = {}
    for i = 1, STRIPE_N do
        local st = Instance.new("Frame", progressFill)
        st.Name = "Stripe" .. i
        st.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        st.Size = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 70)
        st.Rotation = 25
        st.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        st.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86
        st.BorderSizePixel = 0
        st.ZIndex = 11
        st.Visible = false
        stripes[i] = st
    end

    local trail = Instance.new("Frame", progressFill)
    trail.Name = "CometTrail"
    trail.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    trail.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 1, 0)
    trail.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    trail.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    trail.BorderSizePixel = 0
    trail.ZIndex = 11
    trail.Visible = false
    local trailGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", trail)
    trailGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.35),
    })

    local orb = Instance.new("Frame", infoBar)
    orb.Name = "CometOrb"
    orb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    orb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
    orb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    orb.BorderSizePixel = 0
    orb.ZIndex = 11
    orb.Visible = false
    Instance.new("UICorner", orb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local orbRing = Instance.new("UIStroke", orb)
    orbRing.Thickness = 3
    orbRing.Color = SB_ACCENT
    orbRing.Transparency = 0.4

    local bolts = {}
    for i = 1, 4 do
        local b = Instance.new("Frame", infoBar)
        b.Name = "Bolt" .. i
        b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 12)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 240, 255)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.ZIndex = 11
        b.Visible = false
        bolts[i] = b
    end

    local flash = Instance.new("Frame", infoBar)
    flash.Name = "FinishFlash"
    flash.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    flash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    flash.BorderSizePixel = 0
    flash.ZIndex = 11
    Instance.new("UICorner", flash).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local defaultFillColors = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(120, 70, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(200, 160, 255)),
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = tick()
        local active, flashed, lastBolt = false, false, 0
        while progressFill and progressFill.Parent do
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            local t = tick() - t0
            local pct = progressFill.Size.X.Scale
            if pct > 0.001 then
                active = true
                local w = infoBar.AbsoluteSize.X

                -- colors follow the selected GUI theme (updates live when you switch themes)
                local th = (type(getTheme) == "function" and getTheme()) or GUI_THEMES[currentGuiTheme] or {}
                local base = th.STEAL_FILL or th.ACCENT or SB_ACCENT
                local accent = th.STEAL_ACCENT or th.ACCENT or base
                local flow = (math.sin(t * 2.5) + 1) / 2
                local c1 = base:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.35 - flow * 0.2)
                local c2 = base
                local c3 = accent:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.15 + flow * 0.3)
                fillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c1),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, c2),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c3),
                })
                progressFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.38 + math.sin(t * 8) * 0.06
                barStroke.Color = c3
                barStroke.Thickness = 2 + pct

                -- scrolling charge stripes
                for i, st in ipairs(stripes) do
                    st.Visible = true
                    st.Position = UDim2.new(0, ((i * STRIPE_GAP + t * 70) % (STRIPE_N * STRIPE_GAP)) - 18, 0.5, 0)
                end

                -- comet trail + pulsing orb
                trail.Visible = true
                trail.BackgroundColor3 = c3
                local x = w * pct
                local pulse = (math.sin(t * 12) + 1) / 2
                local sz = 10 + pulse * 4
                orb.Visible = true
                orb.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0.5, 0)
                orb.Size = UDim2.new(0, sz, 0, sz)
                orbRing.Color = c3
                orbRing.Transparency = 0.3 + pulse * 0.4

                -- lightning flicker at the tip
                if t - lastBolt > 0.05 then
                    lastBolt = t
                    for _, b in ipairs(bolts) do
                        if math.random() < 0.55 then
                            b.Visible = true
                            b.BackgroundColor3 = c3
                            b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x - math.random(0, 10), 0.5, math.random(-12, 12))
                            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, math.random(6, 16))
                            b.Rotation = math.random(-60, 60)
                        else
                            b.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- finish flash
                if pct >= 0.995 and not flashed then
                    flashed = true
                    flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                    pcall(function()
                        TweenService:Create(flash, TweenInfo.new(0.45), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                    end)
                elseif pct < 0.5 then
                    flashed = false
                end
            elseif active then
                active = false
                flashed = false
                for _, st in ipairs(stripes) do st.Visible = false end
                for _, b in ipairs(bolts) do b.Visible = false end
                trail.Visible = false
                orb.Visible = false
                fillGrad.Color = defaultFillColors
                progressFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
                barStroke.Color = SB_ACCENT
                barStroke.Thickness = 2
            end
        end
    end)
    -- ===== END FILL ANIMATION =====

    -- STEAL label
    local stealTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", infoBar)
    stealTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 1, 0)
    stealTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    stealTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stealTitle.Text = "STEAL"
    stealTitle.TextColor3 = SB_TEXT
    stealTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    stealTitle.TextSize = 13
    stealTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    stealTitle.ZIndex = 12

    -- status (SEARCHING / %)
    local stealPctLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", infoBar)
    stealPctLbl.Name = "StatusLbl"
    stealPctLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 1, 0)
    stealPctLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 0)
    stealPctLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stealPctLbl.Text = "SEARCHING"
    stealPctLbl.TextColor3 = SB_TEXT
    stealPctLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    stealPctLbl.TextSize = 12
    stealPctLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    stealPctLbl.ZIndex = 12

    -- FPS
    local fpsLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", infoBar)
    fpsLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 1, 0)
    fpsLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 168, 0, 0)
    fpsLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    fpsLbl.Text = "FPS:--"
    fpsLbl.TextColor3 = SB_TEXT
    fpsLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    fpsLbl.TextSize = 12
    fpsLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    fpsLbl.ZIndex = 12

    -- PING
    local pingLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", infoBar)
    pingLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 1, 0)
    pingLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 230, 0, 0)
    pingLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pingLbl.Text = "PING:--"
    pingLbl.TextColor3 = SB_TEXT
    pingLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    pingLbl.TextSize = 12
    pingLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    pingLbl.ZIndex = 12

    -- hidden compat labels used by old formatters
    local stealLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", infoBar)
    stealLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    stealLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stealLbl.TextTransparency = 1
    stealLbl.Text = ""
    stealLbl.Visible = false

    function formatStealPct(prog)
        local pct = math.floor((prog or 0) * 100)
        return pct .. "%"
    end

    function formatRadDurPing()
        return "STEAL"
    end

    function setStealStatusText(status, extra)
        if not stealPctLbl then return end
        if status == "SEARCHING" then
            stealPctLbl.Text = "SEARCHING"
        elseif status == "STEALING" then
            stealPctLbl.Text = formatStealPct(extra or 0)
        elseif status == "STOLE" then
            stealPctLbl.Text = "STOLE!"
        elseif status == "MISSED" then
            stealPctLbl.Text = "MISSED"
        elseif status == "IDLE" then
            stealPctLbl.Text = "IDLE"
        else
            stealPctLbl.Text = tostring(status)
        end
    end

    -- FPS updater
    task.spawn(function()
        local frames, t0 = 0, tick()
        while fpsLbl and fpsLbl.Parent do
            frames = frames + 1
            local now = tick()
            if now - t0 >= 0.5 then
                local fps = math.floor(frames / (now - t0) + 0.5)
                fpsLbl.Text = "FPS:" .. tostring(fps)
                frames = 0
                t0 = now
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)

    -- Ping updater
    task.spawn(function()
        local pingItem = nil
        pcall(function()
            pingItem = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
        end)
        while pingLbl and pingLbl.Parent do
            local pv = nil
            pcall(function()
                if getPingMs then
                    pv = getPingMs()
                elseif pingItem then
                    pv = pingItem:GetValue()
                end
            end)
            if type(pv) == "number" then
                local n = math.floor(pv + 0.5)
                pingLbl.Text = "PING:" .. tostring(n)
                if n > 200 then
                    pingLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                elseif n > 100 then
                    pingLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 60)
                else
                    pingLbl.TextColor3 = SB_TEXT
                end
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)

    -- idle status loop
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.25) do
            pcall(function()
                if not isStealing then
                    if Steal.AutoStealEnabled then
                        setStealStatusText("SEARCHING")
                    else
                        setStealStatusText("IDLE")
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    function resetProgressBar()
        if progressFill then progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0) end
        if Steal.AutoStealEnabled then
            setStealStatusText("SEARCHING")
        else
            setStealStatusText("IDLE")
        end
    end

    stealBarFrame = infoBar
end

local function destroyMobileButtons()
    pcall(saveBtnPositions) -- persist before destroy
    if mobGuiRef then pcall(function() mobGuiRef:Destroy() end);mobGuiRef=nil end
    for _,n in ipairs({"CELESTIALMobileButtons"}) do
        local old=game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild(n);if old then old:Destroy() end
        local pgui=LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui");if pgui then local o=pgui:FindFirstChild(n);if o then o:Destroy() end end
    end
    mobBtnRefs={}
end
local function buildMobileButtons()
    destroyMobileButtons();if not mobileButtonsEnabled then return end
    perButtonDragEnabled=true -- always drag each button independently
    local savedPositions=loadBtnPositions()

    -- OLD size formula restored (mobileButtonsSize default=80 -> BTN_SIZE=44)
    local BTN_SIZE=math.floor(mobileButtonsSize*0.55)
    local BTN_GAP=12
    local CORNER_R=circleButtonsEnabled and BTN_SIZE or math.max(8,math.floor(BTN_SIZE*0.22))
    local fontSize=math.max(9,math.floor(mobileButtonsSize*0.12))

    -- Mobile buttons (2 cols).
    -- Row1: DROP BR      | AUTO LEFT
    -- Row2: BAT AIMBOT   | AUTO RIGHT
    -- Row3: TP DOWN      | CARRY SPD
    -- Row4: LAGGER MODE  | LAGGER CARRY
    -- Row5: TP BAT
    -- + up to 3 custom speed buttons (added from Settings)
    local buttons={
        {key="drop",        label="DROP\nBR",       toggle=false, exclusive=false},
    }
    if autoPathMode=="autoDuel" then
        table.insert(buttons, {key="autoDuel", label="AUTO\nDUEL", toggle=true, exclusive=true})
    else
        table.insert(buttons, {key="autoLeft",  label="AUTO\nLEFT",  toggle=true, exclusive=true})
    end
    table.insert(buttons, {key="autoBat", label="BAT\nAIMBOT", toggle=true, exclusive=true})
    table.insert(buttons, {key="bypassAimbot", label="BYPASS\nAIMBOT", toggle=true, exclusive=true})
    if autoPathMode~="autoDuel" then
        table.insert(buttons, {key="autoRight", label="AUTO\nRIGHT", toggle=true, exclusive=true})
    end
    table.insert(buttons, {key="tpDown",      label="TP\nDOWN",       toggle=false, exclusive=false})
    table.insert(buttons, {key="carrySpeed",  label="CARRY\nSPD",     toggle=true,  exclusive=false})
    table.insert(buttons, {key="lagger",      label="LAGGER\nMODE",   toggle=true,  exclusive=false})
    table.insert(buttons, {key="laggerCarry", label="LAGGER\nCARRY",  toggle=true,  exclusive=false})
    table.insert(buttons, {key="tpBat",       label="TP\nBAT",        toggle=true,  exclusive=false})
    table.insert(buttons, {key="instaReset",  label="RESET",          toggle=false, exclusive=false})
    -- Append custom speed buttons (max 3)
    if type(customSpeeds)=="table" then
        for ci, cval in ipairs(customSpeeds) do
            if ci > 3 then break end
            local n = tonumber(cval) or 50
            table.insert(buttons, {
                key = "custom"..ci,
                label = "C"..ci.."\n"..tostring(math.floor(n+0.5)),
                toggle = true,
                exclusive = false,
                customIndex = ci,
            })
        end
    end

    local COLS=2
    local totalW=(COLS*(BTN_SIZE+BTN_GAP))-BTN_GAP
    local totalH=(math.ceil(#buttons/COLS)*(BTN_SIZE+BTN_GAP))-BTN_GAP

    -- Panel padding for background card
    local PAD=10

    local vp=workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
    local savedPanel=savedPositions["_panel"]
    local defPanelX=vp.X-totalW-PAD*2-18
    local defPanelY=math.max(10,vp.Y/2-(totalH+PAD*2)/2)
    local panelXO=savedPanel and savedPanel.xo or defPanelX
    local panelYO=savedPanel and savedPanel.yo or defPanelY

    local mobGui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    mobGui.Name="CELESTIALMobileButtons";mobGui.ResetOnSpawn=false;mobGui.DisplayOrder=15;mobGui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
    parentGui(mobGui)
    mobGuiRef=mobGui

    -- Colors from active GUI theme
    local th = getTheme()
    local PINK_ON      = th.MOB_ON
    -- Force solid black when OFF; keep theme ON / animation colors
    local BTN_BG_OFF   = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local PANEL_BG     = th.BG
    local BORDER_OFF   = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    local BORDER_ON    = th.MOB_ON
    local TEXT_OFF     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local TEXT_ON      = th.MOB_TEXT_ON
    local STROKE_W     = 1

    -- Outer panel only created when NOT using per-button drag
    local panel
    if not perButtonDragEnabled then
        panel=Instance.new("Frame",mobGui)
        panel.Name="MobPanel"
        panel.Size=UDim2.new(0,totalW+PAD*2,0,totalH+PAD*2)
        panel.Position=UDim2.new(0,panelXO,0,panelYO)
        panel.BackgroundColor3=PANEL_BG
        panel.BackgroundTransparency=1
        panel.BorderSizePixel=0;panel.Active=true;panel.ZIndex=99
        local panelStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",panel)
        panelStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(80,80,80);panelStroke.Thickness=1;panelStroke.Transparency=0.7

        -- Drag handle on panel
        local gDragStart,gDragStartPos,gDragDown=nil,nil,false
        panel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if uiLocked then return end
            if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                gDragDown=true;gDragStart=input.Position;gDragStartPos=panel.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState==Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        gDragDown=false
                        if writefile then
                            local posData=loadBtnPositions()
                            posData["_panel"]={xo=panel.Position.X.Offset,yo=panel.Position.Y.Offset}
                            pcall(function() writefile(MOB_POS_FILE,HS:JSONEncode(posData)) end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if gDragDown and not uiLocked and (input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta=input.Position-gDragStart
                panel.Position=UDim2.new(0,gDragStartPos.X.Offset+delta.X,0,gDragStartPos.Y.Offset+delta.Y)
            end
        end)
    end

    for i,def in ipairs(buttons) do
        local col=(i-1)%COLS
        local rowN=math.floor((i-1)/COLS)
        local localX=PAD+col*(BTN_SIZE+BTN_GAP)
        local localY=PAD+rowN*(BTN_SIZE+BTN_GAP)

        -- Position: saved absolute pixels, OR default grid pinned to RIGHT edge
        local frameParent=perButtonDragEnabled and mobGui or panel
        local frame=Instance.new("Frame",frameParent)
        frame.Name="MobBtn_"..def.key
        frame.Size=UDim2.new(0,BTN_SIZE,0,BTN_SIZE)
        local sp = (not forceDefaultBtnPos) and savedPositions[def.key] or nil
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vpX = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.X) or 800
        local vpY = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
        -- only use saved pos if on the right half (rejects old broken left saves)
        if sp and tonumber(sp.xo)~=nil and tonumber(sp.yo)~=nil and tonumber(sp.xo) > vpX*0.35 then
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0,tonumber(sp.xo),0,tonumber(sp.yo))
        else
            -- neat grid on the RIGHT using pure offsets (no scale = no drag jump)
            local xo = vpX - (totalW + PAD + 18) + localX
            local yo = math.max(10, vpY/2 - (totalH+PAD*2)/2) + localY
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0,xo,0,yo)
        end
        -- Premium palette from Settings > Buttons Color
        local _gpal = (MOB_GLOW_PALETTE and MOB_GLOW_PALETTE[mobEffectiveGlow()]) or (MOB_GLOW_PALETTE and MOB_GLOW_PALETTE.Purple) or {bg=Color3.fromRGB(130,70,255), border=Color3.fromRGB(210,170,255), deep=Color3.fromRGB(60,20,140), shine=Color3.fromRGB(230,200,255)}
        local GLOW_BG        = _gpal.bg
        local GLOW_BORDER    = _gpal.border
        local GLOW_DEEP      = _gpal.deep or Color3.fromRGB(40,15,90)
        local GLOW_SHINE     = _gpal.shine or Color3.fromRGB(240,220,255)
        local GLOW_TEXT      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        local OFF_BG         = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) -- gradient below provides the colour
        local OFF_BG2        = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24)
        local OFF_BORDER     = GLOW_BG:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.72)
        local OFF_TEXT       = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        local SHINE_OFF      = GLOW_BG:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.12)

        local cornerR = circleButtonsEnabled and UDim.new(1,0) or UDim.new(0, math.max(10, CORNER_R + 2))

        frame.BackgroundColor3 = OFF_BG
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = true
        frame.ZIndex = 102
        frame.ClipsDescendants = false -- allow outer glow halos to render outside
        frame:SetAttribute("BtnKey", def.key)

        local uic = Instance.new("UICorner", frame)
        uic.CornerRadius = cornerR
        mobAttachBtnImage(frame, cornerR) -- Visual > Button Images

        -- Scale for press pop
        local btnScale = Instance.new("UIScale", frame)
        btnScale.Name = "PressScale"
        btnScale.Scale = 1

        -- Vertical glass gradient (dark depth)
        local bgGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", frame)
        bgGrad.Name = "BgGrad"
        bgGrad.Rotation = 90
        bgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, GLOW_BG:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.62)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, GLOW_BG:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.86)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 16)),
        })

        -- Main border
        local fstroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
        fstroke.Name = "MainStroke"
        fstroke.Color = OFF_BORDER
        fstroke.Thickness = 1.6
        fstroke.Transparency = 0.2

        -- Outer soft glow halo
        local glowHalo = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
        glowHalo.Name = "GlowHalo"
        glowHalo.Size = UDim2.new(1, 8, 1, 8)
        glowHalo.Position = UDim2.new(0, -4, 0, -4)
        glowHalo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        glowHalo.BorderSizePixel = 0
        glowHalo.ZIndex = 100
        local glowCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", glowHalo)
        glowCorner.CornerRadius = circleButtonsEnabled and UDim.new(1,0) or UDim.new(0, math.max(12, CORNER_R + 6))
        local glowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", glowHalo)
        glowStroke.Name = "GlowStroke"
        glowStroke.Color = GLOW_BORDER
        glowStroke.Thickness = 5
        glowStroke.Transparency = 1

        -- Secondary tighter halo (inner bloom)
        local glowInner = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
        glowInner.Name = "GlowInner"
        glowInner.Size = UDim2.new(1, 8, 1, 8)
        glowInner.Position = UDim2.new(0, -4, 0, -4)
        glowInner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        glowInner.BorderSizePixel = 0
        glowInner.ZIndex = 101
        local glowInnerCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", glowInner)
        glowInnerCorner.CornerRadius = cornerR
        local glowInnerStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", glowInner)
        glowInnerStroke.Name = "GlowInnerStroke"
        glowInnerStroke.Color = GLOW_SHINE
        glowInnerStroke.Thickness = 2
        glowInnerStroke.Transparency = 1

        -- (TopShine + AccentBar removed — clean buttons, no top strip)

        -- Soft fill overlay for ON state
        local fillOverlay = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
        fillOverlay.Name = "FillOverlay"
        fillOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        fillOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = GLOW_BG
        fillOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        fillOverlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fillOverlay.ZIndex = 102
        Instance.new("UICorner", fillOverlay).CornerRadius = cornerR
        local fillGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", fillOverlay)
        fillGrad.Rotation = 135
        fillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, GLOW_BG),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, GLOW_DEEP),
        })

        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btn.Text = def.label
        btn.TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = math.max(10, fontSize + 1)
        btn.LineHeight = 1.08
        btn.TextWrapped = true
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 106
        btn.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        btn.TextStrokeTransparency = 1

        local isOn = false
        local isPressed = false

        local function applyVisual(on, pressed)
            local active = on or pressed
            local _bimg = frame:FindFirstChild("BtnImage")
            btn.TextStrokeTransparency = _bimg and 0.35 or 1
            local tInfo = TweenInfo.new(pressed and 0.07 or 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            local tFast = TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

            if active then
                TweenService:Create(frame, tInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = GLOW_DEEP, BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(fstroke, tInfo, {
                    Color = GLOW_BORDER,
                    Thickness = pressed and 2.2 or 2.0,
                    Transparency = pressed and 0.05 or 0.1,
                }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(btn, tInfo, {TextColor3 = GLOW_TEXT}):Play()
                -- tight glow: stay close to the button edge, no big bloom on click
                TweenService:Create(glowStroke, tInfo, {
                    Transparency = pressed and 0.45 or 0.55,
                    Thickness = pressed and 3.5 or 3,
                    Color = GLOW_BORDER,
                }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(glowInnerStroke, tInfo, {
                    Transparency = pressed and 0.25 or 0.4,
                    Thickness = pressed and 1.5 or 1.2,
                    Color = GLOW_SHINE,
                }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(fillOverlay, tInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = _bimg and (pressed and 0.55 or 0.72) or (pressed and 0.2 or 0.32)}):Play()
                if _bimg then TweenService:Create(_bimg, tInfo, {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play() end
                TweenService:Create(btnScale, tFast, {Scale = pressed and 0.96 or 1}):Play()
            else
                TweenService:Create(frame, tInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = OFF_BG, BackgroundTransparency = 0.08}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(fstroke, tInfo, {Color = OFF_BORDER, Thickness = 1.6, Transparency = 0.2}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(btn, tInfo, {TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(glowStroke, tInfo, {Transparency = 1, Thickness = 4}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(glowInnerStroke, tInfo, {Transparency = 1, Thickness = 2}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(fillOverlay, tInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                if _bimg then TweenService:Create(_bimg, tInfo, {ImageTransparency = 0.3}):Play() end
                TweenService:Create(btnScale, tFast, {Scale = 1}):Play()
            end
        end

        local function setOn(v)
            isOn = v == true
            frame:SetAttribute("BtnIsOn", isOn)
            if not isPressed then
                applyVisual(isOn, false)
            end
        end

        mobBtnRefs[def.key] = setOn

        local function flash()
            isPressed = true
            applyVisual(true, true)
            task.delay(0.14, function()
                isPressed = false
                applyVisual(isOn, false)
            end)
        end

        -- Per-button independent drag (one at a time, pure offset = no jump)
        local dragStart2,dragStartPos2,dragMoved,dragDown=nil,nil,false,false
        btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if uiLocked then return end
            if activeMobDrag and activeMobDrag~=frame then return end -- another button is already dragging
            if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragMoved=false
                dragDown=true
                activeMobDrag=frame
                dragStart2=input.Position
                -- use current Position offsets (buttons always pure offset now)
                dragStartPos2=Vector2.new(frame.Position.X.Offset, frame.Position.Y.Offset)
                -- soft blue glow while finger is down
                isPressed = true
                applyVisual(isOn, true)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if not dragDown or uiLocked or activeMobDrag~=frame then return end
            if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local delta=input.Position-dragStart2
                if delta.Magnitude>18 then dragMoved=true end
                if dragMoved then
                    frame.Position=UDim2.new(0,dragStartPos2.X+delta.X,0,dragStartPos2.Y+delta.Y)
                end
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if not dragDown or activeMobDrag~=frame then return end
            if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local wasDrag=dragMoved
                dragDown=false
                activeMobDrag=nil
                isPressed = false
                applyVisual(isOn, false) -- return to ON or OFF look after release
                if wasDrag then pcall(saveBtnPositions) end
                task.delay(0.08,function() dragMoved=false end)
            end
        end)

        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if dragMoved then return end

            if def.key=="tpDown" then runTPFloor();flash();return end
            if def.key=="drop" then local d=armDrop();runDrop(d);flash();return end
            if def.key=="instaReset" then pcall(cursedInstaReset);flash();return end

            if def.exclusive then
                local alreadyOn=(def.key=="autoLeft" and (autoLeftEnabled or _celestialPendingPathKey=="autoLeft")) or (def.key=="autoRight" and (autoRightEnabled or _celestialPendingPathKey=="autoRight")) or (def.key=="autoBat" and autoBatEnabled) or (def.key=="bypassAimbot" and antiBatBypassLockEnabled) or (def.key=="autoDuel" and (autoDuelEnabled or _celestialPendingPathKey=="autoDuel"))
                if alreadyOn then
                    if _celestialPendingPathKey == def.key then
                        cancelCelestialQueuedPath()
                        setOn(false)
                        return
                    end
                    if def.key=="autoLeft" then autoLeftEnabled=false;stopAutoLeft();if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end;setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key=="autoRight" then autoRightEnabled=false;stopAutoRight();if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end;setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key=="autoBat" then stopBatAimbot();if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end;setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key=="bypassAimbot" then StopAntiBatBypassLock();setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key=="autoDuel" then stopAutoDuel();if autoDuelSetVisual then autoDuelSetVisual(false) end;setOn(false)
                    end
                    return
                end
                if _celestialPendingPathKey and _celestialPendingPathKey ~= def.key then cancelCelestialQueuedPath() end
                if autoLeftEnabled and def.key~="autoLeft" then autoLeftEnabled=false;stopAutoLeft();if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end;if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end end
                if autoRightEnabled and def.key~="autoRight" then autoRightEnabled=false;stopAutoRight();if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end;if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end end
                if autoBatEnabled and def.key~="autoBat" then stopBatAimbot();if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end;if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false) end end
                if antiBatBypassLockEnabled and def.key~="bypassAimbot" then StopAntiBatBypassLock();if mobBtnRefs.bypassAimbot then mobBtnRefs.bypassAimbot(false) end end
                if autoDuelEnabled and def.key~="autoDuel" then stopAutoDuel();if autoDuelSetVisual then autoDuelSetVisual(false) end;if mobBtnRefs.autoDuel then mobBtnRefs.autoDuel(false) end end
                if def.key=="autoLeft" then queueCelestialPathStart("autoLeft");setOn(true)
                elseif def.key=="autoRight" then queueCelestialPathStart("autoRight");setOn(true)
                elseif def.key=="autoBat" then queueAutoBatStart();if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(true) end;setOn(true)
                elseif def.key=="bypassAimbot" then
                    if safeModeTryStart and not safeModeTryStart() then return end
                    StartAntiBatBypassLock()
                    setOn(antiBatBypassLockEnabled == true)
                elseif def.key=="autoDuel" then queueCelestialPathStart("autoDuel");setOn(true)
                end
                return
            end
            if def.key=="tpBat" then
                -- TP Bat only
                if tpBatEnabled then
                    stopTPBat()
                    setOn(false)
                    pcall(cleanupPredBall)
                else
                    startTPBat()
                    setOn(true)
                end
                -- force visual to match actual state
                setOn(tpBatEnabled == true)
                saveConfig(); return
            end
            if def.key=="carrySpeed" then toggleCarryMode();setOn(carrySpeedActive);if mobBtnRefs.lagger then mobBtnRefs.lagger(laggerModeEnabled) end;if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(laggerCarryActive) end;for ci=1,3 do if mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci] then mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci](false) end end;saveConfig();return end
            if def.key=="lagger" then toggleLaggerMode();setOn(laggerModeEnabled);if mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(carrySpeedActive) end;if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(laggerCarryActive) end;for ci=1,3 do if mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci] then mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci](false) end end;saveConfig();return end
            if def.key=="laggerCarry" then toggleLaggerCarryMode();setOn(laggerCarryActive);if mobBtnRefs.lagger then mobBtnRefs.lagger(laggerModeEnabled) end;if mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(carrySpeedActive) end;for ci=1,3 do if mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci] then mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci](false) end end;saveConfig();return end
            -- Custom speed buttons (C1 / C2 / C3)
            if def.customIndex then
                local idx = def.customIndex
                if customSpeedActive and selectedCustomIndex == idx then
                    customSpeedActive = false
                    setOn(false)
                else
                    selectedCustomIndex = idx
                    customSpeedActive = true
                    carrySpeedActive = false
                    laggerModeEnabled = false
                    laggerCarryActive = false
                    if mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.lagger then mobBtnRefs.lagger(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(false) end
                    for ci=1,3 do
                        if mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci] then
                            mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci](ci == idx)
                        end
                    end
                    setOn(true)
                end
                pcall(refreshSpeedModeLabel)
                saveConfig()
                return
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Sync initial states
    if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(autoLeftEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then mobBtnRefs.autoRight(autoRightEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoDuel then mobBtnRefs.autoDuel(autoDuelEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(autoBatEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(tpBatEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(carrySpeedActive) end
    if mobBtnRefs.lagger then mobBtnRefs.lagger(laggerModeEnabled) end
    if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(laggerCarryActive) end
    for ci=1,3 do
        if mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci] then
            mobBtnRefs["custom"..ci](customSpeedActive and selectedCustomIndex == ci)
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- MAIN GUI - clean minimal style
-- ============================================================
local function buildGui()
    clearPersistentConns()
    pcall(createStealBar)
    pcall(function() enableControllerGui(mainFrame) end)
    pcall(function() enableControllerGui(mobGuiRef) end)

    -- Color palette from active GUI theme
    local th = getTheme() or GUI_THEMES["Obsidian"]
    local BG, BG2, ROW_BG, ROW_BORDER = th.BG, th.BG2, th.ROW_BG, th.ROW_BORDER
    local WHITE, GRAY, INP = th.WHITE, th.GRAY, th.INP
    local PINK, PINK2, PINK3 = th.ACCENT, th.ACCENT2, th.ACCENT3
    local OFF, TAB_ACTIVE, TAB_INACT, SECT_LBL = th.OFF, th.ACCENT, th.GRAY, th.SECT

    -- Destroy previous GUI completely (gethui / CoreGui / PlayerGui)
    pcall(function()
        if mainGuiRef then mainGuiRef:Destroy() end
    end)
    mainGuiRef = nil
    pcall(function() destroyOldGui("CELESTIALDuels") end)
    pcall(function()
        local cg=game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local o=cg:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALDuels"); if o then o:Destroy() end
        local pg=LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then local o2=pg:FindFirstChild("CELESTIALDuels"); if o2 then o2:Destroy() end end
    end)

    local gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui");gui.Name="CELESTIALDuels";gui.ResetOnSpawn=false;gui.DisplayOrder=10;gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
    parentGui(gui)
    mainGuiRef = gui

    -- Slimmer window: 330 wide
    local W, H = 300, 360
    mainFrame=Instance.new("Frame",gui);mainFrame.Name="Main"
    mainFrame.Size=UDim2.new(0,W,0,H);mainFrame.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-W/2,0.5,-H/2)
    mainFrame.BackgroundColor3=BG;mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency=guiTransparencyEnabled and 0.55 or 0.15
    mainFrame.BorderSizePixel=0;mainFrame.ClipsDescendants=true;mainFrame.ZIndex=5
    Instance.new("UICorner",mainFrame).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,14)

    pcall(function() CelestialBG.register("main", mainFrame) end)
    local bgImage=Instance.new("ImageLabel",mainFrame)
    bgImage.Name="CELESTIALBG"
    bgImage.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    bgImage.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
    bgImage.BackgroundTransparency=1

    cdLogo.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.48)

    cdLogo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    cdLogo.Text = "CD"
    cdLogo.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235,235,240)
    cdLogo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    cdLogo.TextScaled = true

    cdLogoConstraint.MinTextSize = 70

    local cdHub = Instance.new("TextLabel", mainFrame)
    cdHub.Name = "CDHub"

    local topAccent=Instance.new("Frame",mainFrame)

    local bgImage=Instance.new("Frame",mainFrame)
